-- Zenith | Decompiler GUI.
local playersService = game:GetService("Players")
local userInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local tweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local httpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

local WINDOW_WIDTH = 400
local WINDOW_HEIGHT = 600
local TITLE_HEIGHT = 34
local TAB_HEIGHT = 30
local MIN_WIDTH = 320
local MIN_HEIGHT = 480
local MAX_WIDTH = 700
local MAX_HEIGHT = 1050
local CONFIG_FOLDER = "Zenith"
local CONFIG_FILE = "Zenith/Zenith.json"
local TAB_NAMES = { "Decompiler", "RSpy", "Core", "Config" }
local TITLE_TEXT = "Zenith | Decompiler"

local ACCENT_A = Color3.fromRGB(225, 225, 225)
local ACCENT_B = Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 116)
local BACKGROUND_TOP = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 27)
local BACKGROUND_BOTTOM = Color3.fromRGB(9, 9, 10)
local ROW_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(34, 34, 36)
local ELEMENT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 19)
local TEXT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(238, 238, 238)
local OFF_TEXT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(192, 192, 196)
local DIM_TEXT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 124)

local TEXT_FONT = Enum.Font.SourceSans
local BOLD_FONT = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
local CODE_FONT = Enum.Font.Code

local FAST_TWEEN = TweenInfo.new(0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local SLIDE_TWEEN = TweenInfo.new(0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local MEDIUM_TWEEN = TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local POP_TWEEN = TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

local COLOR_PRESETS = {
	Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
	Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70),
	Color3.fromRGB(70, 255, 120),
	Color3.fromRGB(70, 160, 255),
	Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 70),
	Color3.fromRGB(210, 90, 255),
	Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
}

local localPlayer = playersService.LocalPlayer

local Zenith = {
	version = "1.0.0",
	startTime = os.clock(),
	values = {},
	defaults = {},
	setters = {},
	actions = {},
	hooks = {},
	internal = {},
	outputs = {},
	lists = {},
	transient = {},
	pages = {},
	tabButtons = {},
	tabIndex = {},
	searchables = {},
	sections = {},
	boxes = {},
	sectionBoxes = {},
	connections = {},
	uiConnections = {},
	keybinds = {},
	keyLabels = {},
	listening = nil,
}

local animationSpeed = 1

Zenith.log = function() end

function Zenith.setItems(name, items)
	local list = Zenith.lists[name]
	if list then
		list.set(items)
	end
end

function Zenith.copy(text)
	if typeof(setclipboard) == "function" then
		setclipboard(text)
		return true
	end
	return false
end

function Zenith.announce(name, state)
	if Zenith.notify and Zenith.values["Toggle Notifications"] ~= false then
		Zenith.notify(name, state)
	end
end

local function emit(name, value)
	local internalHook = Zenith.internal[name]
	if internalHook then
		pcall(internalHook, value)
	end

	local hook = Zenith.hooks[name]
	if hook then
		local ok, err = pcall(hook, value)
		if not ok then
			Zenith.log("Error", name .. ": " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end
end

local function create(className, properties, parent)
	local instance = Instance.new(className)
	for key, value in properties do
		instance[key] = value
	end
	instance.Parent = parent
	return instance
end

local function tween(instance, info, goal)
	local scaled = TweenInfo.new(info.Time / animationSpeed, info.EasingStyle, info.EasingDirection, info.RepeatCount, info.Reverses, info.DelayTime)
	local newTween = tweenService:Create(instance, scaled, goal)
	newTween:Play()
	return newTween
end

local function connectInput(signal, callback)
	local connection = signal:Connect(callback)
	table.insert(Zenith.uiConnections, connection)
	return connection
end

function Zenith.disconnectUI()
	for i = #Zenith.uiConnections, 1, -1 do
		local conn = Zenith.uiConnections[i]
		pcall(function()
			conn:Disconnect()
		end)
	end
	table.clear(Zenith.uiConnections)
end

local function isPress(input)
	return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end

local function isMove(input)
	return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end

local function addGradient(parent, rotation)
	return create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(ACCENT_A, ACCENT_B), Rotation = rotation }, parent)
end

local function addStroke(parent, color, transparency, thickness)
	return create("UIStroke", {
		Color = color,
		Transparency = transparency,
		Thickness = thickness or 1,
		ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
	}, parent)
end

local function createCard(className, parent, text, height)
	local card = create(className, {
		Name = text,
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height),
		BackgroundColor3 = ROW_COLOR,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.65,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, Zenith.sectionBoxes[parent] or parent)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5) }, card)
	local stroke = addStroke(card, ACCENT_A, 1)
	card.ClipsDescendants = true

	local group = Zenith.currentGroup
	if group then
		table.insert(group.cards, card)
		card:SetAttribute("GroupHidden", group.collapsed)
		card.Visible = not group.collapsed
	end

	card.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not isPress(input) or Zenith.values["Ripple Effect"] == false then
			return
		end

		local offset = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y) - card.AbsolutePosition
		local ripple = create("Frame", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(offset.X, offset.Y),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
			BackgroundTransparency = 0.8,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			ZIndex = 0,
		}, card)

		create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, ripple)
		local diameter = math.max(card.AbsoluteSize.X, card.AbsoluteSize.Y) * 2

		tween(ripple, MEDIUM_TWEEN, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(diameter, diameter), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }).Completed:Connect(function()
			ripple:Destroy()
		end)
	end)

	card.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		tween(card, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 })
		tween(stroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Transparency = 0.55 })
	end)

	card.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tween(card, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.65 })
		tween(stroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Transparency = 1 })
	end)

	return card, stroke
end

local function styleButton(button)
	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4) }, button)
	local buttonStroke = addStroke(button, DIM_TEXT_COLOR, 0.6)

	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		tween(buttonStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Color = ACCENT_A, Transparency = 0 })
		tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 30) })
	end)

	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tween(buttonStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Color = DIM_TEXT_COLOR, Transparency = 0.6 })
		tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR })
	end)

	button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
		tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(46, 46, 48) })
	end)

	button.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
		tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 30) })
	end)
end

local function addSection(parent, text)
	local box = create("Frame", {
		Name = text .. " Box",
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 24),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.2,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		LayoutOrder = #Zenith.boxes + 1,
	}, parent)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, box)
	addStroke(box, Color3.fromRGB(105, 105, 110), 0.55)
	create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 170)), Rotation = 90 }, box)
	create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6) }, box)
	create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, box)

	local holder = create("Frame", { Name = text, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, box)
	local marker = create("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 12),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -7),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, holder)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, marker)
	addGradient(marker, 90)
	tweenService:Create(marker, TweenInfo.new(1.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 }):Play()

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, -4),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = string.upper(text),
		TextColor3 = ACCENT_A,
		TextSize = 11,
		Font = BOLD_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
	}, holder)

	local line = create("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -2),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, holder)

	local gradient = addGradient(line, 0)
	gradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.3, 1)

	Zenith.sectionBoxes[parent] = box
	table.insert(Zenith.boxes, box)
	table.insert(Zenith.sections, holder)
end

local function addToggle(parent, text, keybind, default)
	local startState = default == true
	local holder, stroke = createCard("TextButton", parent, text, 28)
	holder.Text = ""
	holder.AutoButtonColor = false

	local box = create("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -8),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, holder)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4) }, box)
	local boxStroke = addStroke(box, startState and ACCENT_A or DIM_TEXT_COLOR, startState and 0 or 0.5)

	local check = create("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = startState and UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10) or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, box)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3) }, check)
	addGradient(check, 45)

	local label = create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, keybind and -100 or -44, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = startState and TEXT_COLOR or OFF_TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
	}, holder)

	local accentBar = create("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -10),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 5),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = startState and 0 or 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, holder)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, accentBar)
	addGradient(accentBar, 90)

	local state = startState
	Zenith.values[text] = state
	Zenith.defaults[text] = state

	Zenith.setters[text] = function(value)
		state = value == true
		Zenith.values[text] = state

		tween(check, POP_TWEEN, { Size = state and UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10) or UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) })
		tween(boxStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Color = state and ACCENT_A or DIM_TEXT_COLOR, Transparency = state and 0 or 0.5 })
		tween(label, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = state and TEXT_COLOR or OFF_TEXT_COLOR })
		tween(accentBar, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundTransparency = state and 0 or 1 })
		emit(text, state)
	end

	if keybind then
		local keybindName = text .. " Keybind"
		local keybindButton = create("TextButton", {
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, 22),
			Position = UDim2.new(1, -58, 0.5, -11),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Text = "[" .. keybind .. "]",
			TextColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
			TextSize = 12,
			Font = TEXT_FONT,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
			AutoButtonColor = false,
		}, holder)

		local function applyKey(keyName)
			local keyCode = nil
			for _, item in Enum.KeyCode:GetEnumItems() do
				if item.Name == keyName then
					keyCode = item
					break
				end
			end

			Zenith.keybinds[text] = keyCode
			Zenith.values[keybindName] = keyCode and keyName or ""
			keybindButton.Text = keyCode and ("[" .. keyName .. "]") or "[None]"
			keybindButton.Visible = Zenith.values["Keybind Hints"] ~= false
		end

		Zenith.defaults[keybindName] = keybind
		Zenith.setters[keybindName] = applyKey
		Zenith.keyLabels[text] = keybindButton
		applyKey(keybind)

		keybindButton.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			tween(keybindButton, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = ACCENT_A })
		end)

		keybindButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			if not Zenith.listening then
				tween(keybindButton, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR })
			end
		end)

		keybindButton.Activated:Connect(function()
			if Zenith.cancelListening then
				Zenith.cancelListening()
			end

			local previousKey = Zenith.values[keybindName]
			keybindButton.Text = "[...]"
			tween(keybindButton, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = ACCENT_A })

			Zenith.cancelListening = function()
				Zenith.listening = nil
				Zenith.cancelListening = nil
				tween(keybindButton, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR })
				applyKey(previousKey)
			end

			Zenith.listening = function(keyCode)
				Zenith.listening = nil
				Zenith.cancelListening = nil
				tween(keybindButton, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR })
				applyKey(keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape and "" or keyCode.Name)
			end
		end)
	end

	holder.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
		tween(holder, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
		tween(stroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Transparency = 0 })
	end)

	holder.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
		tween(holder, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 })
		tween(stroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Transparency = 0.55 })
	end)

	holder.Activated:Connect(function()
		Zenith.setters[text](not state)
		Zenith.announce(text, state)
	end)

	holder.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if Zenith.values["Hover Slide"] ~= false then
			tween(label, FAST_TWEEN, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 0) })
		end
	end)

	holder.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tween(label, FAST_TWEEN, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 0) })
	end)

	table.insert(Zenith.searchables, holder)
	return holder
end

local function addSlider(parent, text, minValue, maxValue, default, decimals)
	local holder = createCard("Frame", parent, text, 44)
	local startAlpha = (default - minValue) / (maxValue - minValue)

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 20),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 3),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
	}, holder)

	local valueLabel = create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 20),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 3),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = tostring(default),
		TextColor3 = OFF_TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = BOLD_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
	}, holder)

	local bar = create("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 6),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 29),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, holder)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, bar)
	addStroke(bar, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.88)

	local fill = create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(startAlpha, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, bar)
	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, fill)
	addGradient(fill, 0)

	local knob = create("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12),
		Position = UDim2.new(startAlpha, 0, 0.5, 0),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, bar)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, knob)
	addStroke(knob, ACCENT_A, 0, 2)
	local knobScale = create("UIScale", {}, knob)

	local hitbox = create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 21), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, holder)

	Zenith.values[text] = default
	Zenith.defaults[text] = default

	local dragging = false

	local function setValue(value)
		local factor = 10 ^ decimals
		value = math.floor(math.clamp(value, minValue, maxValue) * factor + 0.5) / factor
		local snapped = (value - minValue) / (maxValue - minValue)

		tween(fill, SLIDE_TWEEN, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(snapped, 1) })
		tween(knob, SLIDE_TWEEN, { Position = UDim2.new(snapped, 0, 0.5, 0) })
		valueLabel.Text = tostring(value)
		Zenith.values[text] = value
		emit(text, value)
	end

	Zenith.setters[text] = setValue

	local function updateFromX(positionX)
		local alpha = math.clamp((positionX - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / bar.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
		setValue(minValue + (maxValue - minValue) * alpha)
	end

	hitbox.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not isPress(input) then
			return
		end

		dragging = true
		tween(knobScale, POP_TWEEN, { Scale = 1.35 })
		tween(valueLabel, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = ACCENT_A })
		updateFromX(input.Position.X)
	end)

	connectInput(userInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
		if dragging and isMove(input) then
			updateFromX(input.Position.X)
		end
	end)

	connectInput(userInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
		if not dragging or not isPress(input) then
			return
		end

		dragging = false
		tween(knobScale, POP_TWEEN, { Scale = 1 })
		tween(valueLabel, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = OFF_TEXT_COLOR })
	end)

	table.insert(Zenith.searchables, holder)
	return holder
end

local function addCycle(parent, text, options)
	local holder = createCard("Frame", parent, text, 56)

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 3),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
	}, holder)

	local button = create("TextButton", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 24),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 26),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = options[1],
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
	}, holder)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4) }, button)
	local buttonStroke = addStroke(button, DIM_TEXT_COLOR, 0.6)

	local index = 1
	Zenith.values[text] = options[1]
	Zenith.defaults[text] = options[1]

	Zenith.setters[text] = function(value)
		local found = table.find(options, value)
		if not found then
			return
		end

		index = found
		Zenith.values[text] = options[index]
		button.Text = options[index]
		emit(text, options[index])
	end

	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		tween(buttonStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Color = ACCENT_A, Transparency = 0 })
	end)

	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tween(buttonStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Color = DIM_TEXT_COLOR, Transparency = 0.6 })
	end)

	button.Activated:Connect(function()
		index = index % #options + 1
		Zenith.values[text] = options[index]
		button.Text = options[index]
		button.TextTransparency = 1
		tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { TextTransparency = 0 })
		emit(text, options[index])
	end)

	table.insert(Zenith.searchables, holder)
	return holder
end

local function addButton(parent, text, callback)
	local holder = createCard("Frame", parent, text, 34)

	local button = create("TextButton", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 24),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 5),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
	}, holder)

	styleButton(button)

	if callback then
		Zenith.actions[text] = callback
	end

	button.Activated:Connect(function()
		local fn = Zenith.actions[text]
		if not fn then
			return Zenith.log("Warn", "No handler for: " .. text)
		end

		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, err = pcall(fn)
			if not ok then
				Zenith.log("Error", text .. ": " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end)
	end)

	table.insert(Zenith.searchables, holder)
	return holder
end

local function addText(parent, text, placeholder, default)
	local holder = createCard("Frame", parent, text, 34)

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(0.42, -8, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
	}, holder)

	local box = create("TextBox", {
		Size = UDim2.new(0.58, -12, 0, 22),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.42, 0, 0.5, -11),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		PlaceholderText = placeholder or "",
		PlaceholderColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
		Text = default or "",
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
	}, holder)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4) }, box)
	local boxStroke = addStroke(box, DIM_TEXT_COLOR, 0.6)

	box.Focused:Connect(function()
		tween(boxStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Color = ACCENT_A, Transparency = 0 })
	end)

	box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		tween(boxStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Color = DIM_TEXT_COLOR, Transparency = 0.6 })
		emit(text, box.Text)
	end)

	Zenith.values[text] = default or ""
	Zenith.defaults[text] = default or ""

	Zenith.setters[text] = function(value)
		box.Text = tostring(value)
		Zenith.values[text] = box.Text
		emit(text, box.Text)
	end

	box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		Zenith.values[text] = box.Text
	end)

	table.insert(Zenith.searchables, holder)
	return holder
end

local function addKeybind(parent, text, default)
	local holder = createCard("Frame", parent, text, 28)

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
	}, holder)

	local keybindButton = create("TextButton", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 22),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -64, 0.5, -11),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = default and ("[" .. default .. "]") or "[None]",
		TextColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
	}, holder)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4) }, keybindButton)
	addStroke(keybindButton, DIM_TEXT_COLOR, 0.6)

	local keybindName = text .. " Value"
	Zenith.defaults[keybindName] = default or ""
	Zenith.values[keybindName] = default or ""

	local function applyKey(keyName)
		Zenith.values[keybindName] = keyName or ""
		keybindButton.Text = (keyName and keyName ~= "") and ("[" .. keyName .. "]") or "[None]"
		emit(keybindName, keyName or "")
	end

	Zenith.setters[keybindName] = applyKey

	keybindButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if Zenith.cancelListening then
			Zenith.cancelListening()
		end

		local previousKey = Zenith.values[keybindName]
		keybindButton.Text = "[...]"

		Zenith.cancelListening = function()
			Zenith.listening = nil
			Zenith.cancelListening = nil
			applyKey(previousKey)
		end

		Zenith.listening = function(keyCode)
			Zenith.listening = nil
			Zenith.cancelListening = nil
			applyKey(keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape and "" or keyCode.Name)
		end
	end)

	table.insert(Zenith.searchables, holder)
	return holder
end

local function addColor(parent, text, default)
	local holder = createCard("Frame", parent, text, 28)

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -46, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
	}, holder)

	local swatch = create("TextButton", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 18),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0.5, -9),
		BackgroundColor3 = default or COLOR_PRESETS[1],
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = "",
		AutoButtonColor = false,
	}, holder)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4) }, swatch)
	addStroke(swatch, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.5)

	local index = 1
	Zenith.values[text] = default or COLOR_PRESETS[1]
	Zenith.defaults[text] = default or COLOR_PRESETS[1]

	Zenith.setters[text] = function(value)
		swatch.BackgroundColor3 = value
		Zenith.values[text] = value
		emit(text, value)
	end

	swatch.Activated:Connect(function()
		index = index % #COLOR_PRESETS + 1
		Zenith.setters[text](COLOR_PRESETS[index])
	end)

	table.insert(Zenith.searchables, holder)
	return holder
end

local function addList(parent, text, items, height)
	height = height or 90
	local holder = createCard("Frame", parent, text, height + 24)

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 18),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 2),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = BOLD_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
	}, holder)

	local scroller = create("ScrollingFrame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, height),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 22),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.3,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 3,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
	}, holder)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4) }, scroller)
	create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, scroller)
	create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 2), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 2) }, scroller)

	local rowList, rows = {}, {}
	Zenith.values[text] = items[1] or ""
	Zenith.defaults[text] = items[1] or ""
	Zenith.transient[text] = true

	local function highlight()
		for name, row in rows do
			row.BackgroundTransparency = name == Zenith.values[text] and 0.5 or 1
		end
	end

	local function selectItem(name)
		Zenith.values[text] = name
		highlight()
		emit(text, name)
	end

	local function setItems(newItems)
		for _, row in rowList do
			row:Destroy()
		end

		table.clear(rowList)
		table.clear(rows)

		for index, itemName in newItems do
			if index > 600 then
				break
			end

			itemName = tostring(itemName)
			if rows[itemName] then
				continue
			end

			local row = create("TextButton", {
				Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 20),
				BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT_A,
				BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				BorderSizePixel = 0,
				Text = itemName,
				TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
				TextSize = 12,
				Font = TEXT_FONT,
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
				TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
				AutoButtonColor = false,
				LayoutOrder = index,
			}, scroller)

			create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 5), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4) }, row)

			create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3) }, row)
			rows[itemName] = row
			table.insert(rowList, row)

			row.Activated:Connect(function()
				selectItem(itemName)
			end)
		end

		if not rows[Zenith.values[text]] then
			Zenith.values[text] = rowList[1] and rowList[1].Text or ""
		end

		highlight()
	end

	Zenith.setters[text] = selectItem
	Zenith.lists[text] = { set = setItems }
	setItems(items)

	table.insert(Zenith.searchables, holder)
	return holder
end

local function addCounter(parent, text, default)
	local holder = createCard("Frame", parent, text, 26)

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -90, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
	}, holder)

	local valueLabel = create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(74, 20),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -82, 0.5, -10),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = tostring(default or 0),
		TextColor3 = ACCENT_A,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = BOLD_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
	}, holder)

	Zenith.values[text] = default or 0
	Zenith.defaults[text] = default or 0
	Zenith.transient[text] = true

	Zenith.setters[text] = function(value)

		Zenith.values[text] = value

	table.insert(Zenith.searchables, holder)

end

local function addOutput(parent, text, height, maxLines)
	height = height or 100
	local holder = createCard("Frame", parent, text, height + 54)

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 18),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 2),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = BOLD_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
	}, holder)

	local scroller = create("ScrollingFrame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, height),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 22),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.2,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 3,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
	}, holder)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4) }, scroller)
	create("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, scroller)
	create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 3), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 3) }, scroller)

	local api = { maxLines = maxLines or 400, autoScroll = true, lines = {} }
	local order = 0

	function api.push(line)
		order += 1
		line = tostring(line)

		local label = create("TextLabel", {
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Text = line == "" and " " or line,
			TextColor3 = OFF_TEXT_COLOR,
			TextSize = 11,
			Font = CODE_FONT,
			TextWrapped = true,
			RichText = false,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
			LayoutOrder = order,
		}, scroller)

		table.insert(api.lines, label)
		while #api.lines > api.maxLines do
			table.remove(api.lines, 1):Destroy()
		end

		if api.autoScroll then
			task.delay(0.03, function()
				if scroller.Parent then
					scroller.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, scroller.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y)
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	function api.clear()
		for _, label in api.lines do
			label:Destroy()
		end
		table.clear(api.lines)
	end

	function api.setText(source)
		api.clear()
		local previous = api.autoScroll
		api.autoScroll = false

		local all = string.split(tostring(source), "\n")
		for index = 1, math.min(#all, api.maxLines) do
			api.push(all[index])
		end

		api.autoScroll = previous
		scroller.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
	end

	function api.getText()
		local parts = {}
		for _, label in api.lines do
			table.insert(parts, label.Text)
		end
		return table.concat(parts, "\n")
	end

	local copyButton = create("TextButton", {
		Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -12, 0, 22),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, height + 28),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = "Copy",
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
	}, holder)

	local clearButton = create("TextButton", {
		Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -12, 0, 22),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 4, 0, height + 28),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = "Clear",
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
	}, holder)

	styleButton(copyButton)
	styleButton(clearButton)

	copyButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		local copied = Zenith.copy(api.getText())
		Zenith.log(copied and "Info" or "Warn", copied and (text .. " copied.") or "Clipboard is not supported here.")
	end)

	clearButton.Activated:Connect(api.clear)

	Zenith.outputs[text] = api
	Zenith.transient[text] = true
	table.insert(Zenith.searchables, holder)
	return holder
end

local function beginGroup(parent, text, open)
	local group = { cards = {}, collapsed = not open }
	local holder = createCard("Frame", parent, text .. " Options", 30)

	local button = create("TextButton", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 22),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 4),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = (open and "-  Hide " or "+  Show ") .. text,
		TextColor3 = OFF_TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
	}, holder)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4) }, button)
	local buttonStroke = addStroke(button, DIM_TEXT_COLOR, 0.6)

	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		tween(buttonStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Color = ACCENT_A, Transparency = 0 })
	end)

	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tween(buttonStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Color = DIM_TEXT_COLOR, Transparency = 0.6 })
	end)

	button.Activated:Connect(function()
		group.collapsed = not group.collapsed
		button.Text = (group.collapsed and "+  Show " or "-  Hide ") .. text

		for _, card in group.cards do
			card:SetAttribute("GroupHidden", group.collapsed)
			card.Visible = not group.collapsed
		end
	end)

	table.insert(Zenith.searchables, holder)
	Zenith.currentGroup = group
end

local function endGroup()
	Zenith.currentGroup = nil
end

local function createColumn(parent, position)
	local column = create("ScrollingFrame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -6, 1, 0),
		Position = position,
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 3,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = ACCENT_A,
		ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.4,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
	}, parent)

	create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 4), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, column)
	create("UIPadding", { PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6) }, column)

	return column
end

local function makeDraggable(handle, target)
	local dragStart, startPosition

	handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not isPress(input) or Zenith.values["Lock Window"] then
			return
		end

		dragStart = input.Position
		startPosition = target.Position
	end)

	connectInput(userInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
		if not dragStart or not isMove(input) then
			return
		end

		local delta = input.Position - dragStart
		target.Position = UDim2.new(startPosition.X.Scale, startPosition.X.Offset + delta.X, startPosition.Y.Scale, startPosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
	end)

	connectInput(userInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
		if isPress(input) then
			dragStart = nil
		end
	end)
end

local function createTitleButton(parent, text, offset, hoverColor)
	local button = create("TextButton", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 22),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, offset, 0.5, -11),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.4,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		Text = text,
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = BOLD_FONT,
		AutoButtonColor = false,
	}, parent)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5) }, button)

	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = hoverColor, BackgroundTransparency = 0.1 })
	end)

	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR, BackgroundTransparency = 0.4 })
	end)

	button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
		tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.4 })
	end)

	return button
end

local function switchTab(name)
	for pageName, page in Zenith.pages do
		local selected = pageName == name
		page.Visible = selected
		tween(Zenith.tabButtons[pageName], FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = selected and TEXT_COLOR or DIM_TEXT_COLOR })

		if selected then
			page.GroupTransparency = 1
			tween(page, MEDIUM_TWEEN, { GroupTransparency = 0 })
		end
	end

	Zenith.selected = name
	tween(Zenith.indicator, MEDIUM_TWEEN, { Position = UDim2.new((Zenith.tabIndex[name] - 1) / #TAB_NAMES, 6, 0, 3) })
end

Zenith.switchTab = switchTab

-- Step 0 Kernel: Capabilities, Signal, Core lifecycle & Boot.
local flag, num, set
local KernelMATRIX
do
	local V = Zenith.values

	function flag(name, default)
		local v = V[name]
		if v == nil then
			return default
		end
		return v == true
	end

	function num(name, default)
		return tonumber(V[name]) or default
	end

	function set(name, value)
		V[name] = value
		local s = Zenith.setters and Zenith.setters[name]
		if s then
			pcall(s, value)
		end
	end

	Zenith.bootErrors = {}

	local function bootLog(level, msg)
		if level == "Error" then
			table.insert(Zenith.bootErrors, msg)
			if not flag("Show Boot Errors", true) then
				return
			end
		end
		local out = Zenith.outputs and Zenith.outputs["Boot Log"]
		if out then
			out.push(string.format("[%s] %s", level, msg))
		end
		if Zenith.log then
			Zenith.log(level, msg)
		end
	end

	Zenith.connections = Zenith.connections or {}

	local function isConnected(c)
		if typeof(c) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
			return c.Connected
		end
		if type(c) == "table" then
			return c.Connected ~= false
		end
		return false
	end

	local function disconnect(c)
		pcall(function()
			if typeof(c) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
				c:Disconnect()
			elseif type(c) == "table" and c.Disconnect then
				c:Disconnect()
			end
		end)
	end

	function Zenith.refreshConnections()
		local ui = Zenith.uiConnections and #Zenith.uiConnections or 0
		set("Active Connections", #Zenith.connections + ui)
	end

	local function prune()
		for i = #Zenith.connections, 1, -1 do
			if not isConnected(Zenith.connections[i]) then
				table.remove(Zenith.connections, i)
			end
		end
	end

	function Zenith.track(conn, owner)
		if owner then
			table.insert(owner, conn)
		end
		if flag("Track Connections", true) then
			table.insert(Zenith.connections, conn)
		end
		Zenith.refreshConnections()
		return conn
	end

	function Zenith.cleanup()
		local n = 0
		for i = #Zenith.connections, 1, -1 do
			local c = Zenith.connections[i]
			if isConnected(c) then
				n += 1
			end
			disconnect(c)
		end
		table.clear(Zenith.connections)
		Zenith.refreshConnections()
		return n
	end

	local roots = {}
	if typeof(getgenv) == "function" then
		table.insert(roots, getgenv())
	end
	do
		local ok, fe = pcall(getfenv, 0)
		if ok and type(fe) == "table" then
			table.insert(roots, fe)
		end
	end

	local function resolve(path)
		for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
			local cur = root
			for key in string.gmatch(path, "[^%.]+") do
				if type(cur) ~= "table" then
					cur = nil
					break
				end
				local ok, v = pcall(function()
					return cur[key]
				end)
				cur = ok and v or nil
				if cur == nil then
					break
				end
			end
			if type(cur) == "function" then
				return cur
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function sampleScript(class)
		local ps = localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
		local rsOk, rs = pcall(game.GetService, game, "ReplicatedStorage")
		local containers = {}
		if ps then
			table.insert(containers, ps)
		end
		if rsOk and rs then
			table.insert(containers, rs)
		end
		for _, root in ipairs(containers) do
			local ok, list = pcall(root.GetDescendants, root)
			if ok and type(list) == "table" then
				for _, d in ipairs(list) do
					if d:IsA(class) then
						return d
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local upv = 1
	local function probeFn(a)
		upv = upv + 1
		if upv < 0 then
			local function inner()
				return upv
			end
			return inner
		end
		return "zenith" .. tostring(a)
	end

	local function tableProbe(f)
		return type(f()) == "table"
	end

	KernelMATRIX = {
		{ "getscriptbytecode", { "getscriptbytecode", "get_script_bytecode" }, function(f)
			local s = sampleScript("LuaSourceContainer")
			if not s then
				return nil
			end
			return type(f(s)) == "string"
		end },
		{ "decompile", { "decompile" } },
		{ "getscripts", { "getscripts" }, tableProbe },
		{ "getloadedmodules", { "getloadedmodules" }, tableProbe },
		{ "getnilinstances", { "getnilinstances" }, tableProbe },
		{ "getscriptclosure", { "getscriptclosure", "getscriptfunction" }, function(f)
			local s = sampleScript("ModuleScript")
			if not s then
				return nil
			end
			return type(f(s)) == "function"
		end },
		{ "getconstants", { "debug.getconstants", "getconstants" }, function(f)
			local r = f(probeFn)
			return type(r) == "table" and #r > 0
		end },
		{ "getupvalues", { "debug.getupvalues", "getupvalues" }, function(f)
			local r = f(probeFn)
			return type(r) == "table" and #r > 0
		end },
		{ "getprotos", { "debug.getprotos", "getprotos" }, function(f)
			local r = f(probeFn)
			return type(r) == "table" and #r > 0
		end },
		{ "getinfo", { "debug.getinfo", "getinfo" }, function(f)
			return type(f(probeFn)) == "table"
		end },
		{ "debuginfo", { "debug.info" }, function(f)
			return type(f(probeFn, "s")) == "string"
		end },
		{ "writefile", { "writefile" } },
		{ "appendfile", { "appendfile" } },
		{ "readfile", { "readfile" } },
		{ "isfile", { "isfile" } },
		{ "isfolder", { "isfolder" } },
		{ "makefolder", { "makefolder" } },
		{ "listfiles", { "listfiles" } },
		{ "delfile", { "delfile" } },
		{ "setclipboard", { "setclipboard", "toclipboard" } },
		{ "buffer", { "buffer.create" }, function(f)
			return typeof(f(4)) == "buffer"
		end },
		{ "hookfunction", { "hookfunction", "replaceclosure" } },
		{ "hookmetamethod", { "hookmetamethod" } },
		{ "getrawmetatable", { "getrawmetatable", "debug.getmetatable" } },
		{ "getnamecallmethod", { "getnamecallmethod" } },
		{ "checkcaller", { "checkcaller" } },
		{ "newcclosure", { "newcclosure" } },
		{ "getcallingscript", { "getcallingscript" } },
		{ "getcallbackvalue", { "getcallbackvalue", "getcallbackmember" } },
		{ "getcallbackmember", { "getcallbackmember" } },
		{ "getgc", { "getgc" } },
		{ "getconnections", { "getconnections" } },
		{ "gethui", { "gethui" } },
		{ "getscriptfromthread", { "getscriptfromthread" } },
		{ "getfunctionhash", { "getfunctionhash" } },
		{ "isexecutorclosure", { "isexecutorclosure", "is_synapse_function", "isourclosure" } },
		{ "islclosure", { "islclosure" } },

		{ "setstackhidden", { "setstackhidden" } },
		{ "run_on_actor", { "run_on_actor" } },

		{ "create_comm_channel", { "create_comm_channel" } },
		{ "cloneref", { "cloneref" } },
		{ "compareinstances", { "compareinstances" } },
		{ "setthreadidentity", { "setthreadidentity", "set_thread_identity" } },
		{ "getthreadidentity", { "getthreadidentity", "get_thread_identity" } },
		{ "oth_hook", { "oth.hook" } },
		{ "oth_unhook", { "oth.unhook" } },

	function Zenith.probe()
		local caps = { status = {}, via = {}, fn = {}, executor = "unknown" }

		pcall(function()
			if typeof(identifyexecutor) == "function" then
				local n, v = identifyexecutor()
				caps.executor = tostring(n) .. (v and (" " .. tostring(v)) or "")
			end
		end)

		local okCount = 0
		for _, entry in ipairs(KernelMATRIX) do
			local name, aliases, probe = entry[1], entry[2], entry[3]
			local status, via, fn = "missing", nil, nil
			for _, alias in ipairs(aliases) do
				local f = resolve(alias)
				if f then
					fn, via = f, alias
					break
				end
			end
			if fn then
				if probe then
					local ok, res = pcall(probe, fn)
					if not ok or res == false then
						status = "limited"
					elseif res == nil then
						status = "present"
					else
						status = "ok"
					end
				else
					status = "present"
				end
				okCount += 1
			end
			caps.status[name] = status
			caps.via[name] = via
			caps.fn[name] = fn
		end

		Zenith.capabilities = caps

		bootLog("Info", "Executor: " .. caps.executor)
		for _, entry in ipairs(KernelMATRIX) do
			local name = entry[1]
			local st = caps.status[name]
			local line = string.format("%-18s %-8s %s", name, st, caps.via[name] or "-")
			bootLog(st == "missing" and "Warn" or "Info", line)
		end
		bootLog("Info", string.format("Capabilities: %d/%d disponibles", okCount, #KernelMATRIX))

		return caps
	end

	function Zenith.cap(name)
		local c = Zenith.capabilities
		local st = c and c.status[name]
		return st ~= nil and st ~= "missing"
	end

	function Zenith.capFn(name)
		local c = Zenith.capabilities
		return c and c.fn[name]
	end

	function Zenith.need(list)
		local missing = {}
		for _, name in ipairs(list or {}) do
			if not Zenith.cap(name) then
				table.insert(missing, name)
			end
		end
		return #missing == 0, missing
	end

	local Signal = {}
	Signal.__index = Signal
	local registry = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local fired = 0

	local function refreshListeners()
		local n = 0
		for s in pairs(registry) do
			n += #s.listeners
		end
		set("Active Listeners", n)
	end

	local function signalLog(msg)
		if not flag("Signal Debug", false) then
			return
		end
		local out = Zenith.outputs and Zenith.outputs["Signal Log"]
		if out then
			out.push(msg)
		end
	end

	function Signal.new(name)
		local self = setmetatable({ name = name or "Signal", listeners = {}, warned = false }, Signal)
		registry[self] = true
		return self
	end

	function Signal:Connect(fn)
		local item = { fn = fn }
		local listeners = self.listeners
		table.insert(listeners, item)

		if #listeners > num("Max Listeners", 64) and flag("Warn On Leak", true) and not self.warned then
			self.warned = true
			bootLog("Warn", string.format("Posible fuga: %s tiene %d listeners", self.name, #listeners))
		end
		refreshListeners()

		local conn = { Connected = true }
		function conn:Disconnect()
			if not self.Connected then
				return
			end
			self.Connected = false
			local i = table.find(listeners, item)
			if i then
				table.remove(listeners, i)
			end
			refreshListeners()
		end
		return Zenith.track(conn)
	end

	function Signal:Once(fn)
		local conn
		conn = self:Connect(function(...)
			if conn then
				conn:Disconnect()
			end
			fn(...)
		end)
		return conn
	end

	function Signal:Fire(...)
		fired += 1
		set("Signals Fired", fired)
		signalLog(string.format("%s -> %d listeners", self.name, #self.listeners))

		local args = table.pack(...)
		local deferred = string.find(string.lower(tostring(V["Dispatch Mode"] or "")), "defer") ~= nil
		for _, item in ipairs(table.clone(self.listeners)) do
			local function run()
				local ok, err = pcall(item.fn, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
				if not ok then
					bootLog("Error", string.format("%s: %s", self.name, tostring(err)))
				end
			end
			if deferred then
				task.defer(run)
			else
				task.spawn(run)
			end
		end
	end

	function Signal:DisconnectAll()
		table.clear(self.listeners)
		refreshListeners()
	end

	Zenith.Signal = Signal

	Zenith.actions["Dump Signals"] = function()
		local out = Zenith.outputs and Zenith.outputs["Signal Log"]
		local count = 0
		for s in pairs(registry) do
			count += 1
			local msg = string.format("%s: %d listeners", s.name, #s.listeners)
			if out then
				out.push(msg)
			else
				Zenith.log("Info", "[Signal] " .. msg)
			end
		end
		if count == 0 and not out then
			Zenith.log("Info", "[Signal] Sin senales registradas")
		end
	end

	local modules, order = {}, {}
	local started = false

	function Zenith.register(name, spec)
		if modules[name] then
			if flag("Strict Lifecycle", true) then
				bootLog("Warn", name .. " ya estaba registrado")
			end
			return
		end
		modules[name] = {
			requires = spec.requires or {},
			optional = spec.optional or {},
			init = spec.init,
			stop = spec.stop,
			state = "pending",
			owned = {},
		}
		table.insert(order, name)
	end

	local function releaseOwned(name)
		local m = modules[name]
		local leaked = 0
		for i = #m.owned, 1, -1 do
			local c = m.owned[i]
			if isConnected(c) then
				leaked += 1
			end
			disconnect(c)
		end
		table.clear(m.owned)
		prune()
		Zenith.refreshConnections()
		return leaked
	end

	local function stopModule(name)
		local m = modules[name]
		local strict = flag("Strict Lifecycle", true)
		local ok, err = true, nil
		if m.stop then
			ok, err = pcall(m.stop)
		elseif strict and #m.owned > 0 then
			bootLog("Warn", name .. " no define stop pero tiene conexiones")
		end
		if not ok then
			bootLog(strict and "Error" or "Warn", string.format("%s stop fallo: %s", name, tostring(err)))
		end
		local leaked = releaseOwned(name)
		if leaked > 0 and strict then
			bootLog("Warn", string.format("%s dejo %d conexiones abiertas (forzadas)", name, leaked))
		end
		if strict and not ok then
			m.state = "failed"
			return false
		end
		m.state = "pending"
		return true
	end

	local function runInit(name, caps)
		local m = modules[name]
		local retries = math.max(0, math.floor(num("Boot Retries", 1)))
		local timeout = num("Boot Timeout", 10)
		local autoClean = flag("Auto Cleanup On Error", false)
		local ctx = {
			name = name,
			track = function(conn)
				return Zenith.track(conn, m.owned)
			end,
		}
		local lastErr
		for attempt = 0, retries do
			local done, success, err = false, false, nil
			task.spawn(function()
				local ok, e = pcall(m.init, caps, ctx)
				success, done = ok, true
				if not ok then
					err = e
				end
			end)
			local t0 = os.clock()
			while not done and os.clock() - t0 < timeout do
				task.wait()
			end
			if done and success then
				return true
			end
			lastErr = done and err or "timeout"
			bootLog("Warn", string.format("%s intento %d/%d fallo: %s", name, attempt + 1, retries + 1, tostring(lastErr)))
			if autoClean then
				releaseOwned(name)
				if m.stop then
					pcall(m.stop)
				end
			end
		end
		return false, lastErr
	end

	function Zenith.refreshServices()
		local caps = Zenith.capabilities
		local okCount, total = 0, 0
		if caps then
			for _, e in ipairs(KernelMATRIX) do
				total += 1
				if caps.status[e[1]] ~= "missing" then
					okCount += 1
				end
			end
		end
		local listeners = 0
		for s in pairs(registry) do
			listeners += #s.listeners
		end
		local items = {
			string.format("Capabilities: %d/%d", okCount, total),
			string.format("Signal: %d listeners", listeners),
			string.format("Connections: %d modulos / %d GUI", #Zenith.connections, Zenith.uiConnections and #Zenith.uiConnections or 0),
			"Console: " .. (Zenith.log and "activa" or "inactiva"),
		}
		for _, name in ipairs(order) do
			table.insert(items, string.format("%s: %s", name, modules[name].state))
		end
		if Zenith.setItems then
			pcall(Zenith.setItems, "Core Services", items)
		end
	end

	function Zenith.boot()
		local caps = Zenith.capabilities or Zenith.probe()
		local safe = flag("Safe Boot", true)
		local parallel = string.find(string.lower(tostring(V["Load Order"] or "")), "parallel") ~= nil

		local function bootOne(name)
			local m = modules[name]
			if m.state == "loaded" then
				return true
			end
			local ok, missing = Zenith.need(m.requires)
			if not ok then
				m.state = "skipped"
				bootLog("Warn", string.format("%s omitido, falta: %s", name, table.concat(missing, ", ")))
				return true
			end
			local _, optMissing = Zenith.need(m.optional)
			if #optMissing > 0 then
				bootLog("Info", string.format("%s sin opcionales: %s", name, table.concat(optMissing, ", ")))
			end
			local success, err = runInit(name, caps)
			if success then
				m.state = "loaded"
				bootLog("Info", name .. " cargado")
				return true
			end
			m.state = "failed"
			bootLog("Error", string.format("%s fallo: %s", name, tostring(err)))
			return false
		end

		if parallel then
			if not safe then
				bootLog("Warn", "Parallel no puede abortar el boot, solo registra fallos")
			end
			local pending = 0
			for _, name in ipairs(order) do
				pending += 1
				task.spawn(function()
					local ok, e = pcall(bootOne, name)
					if not ok then
						modules[name].state = "failed"
						bootLog("Error", string.format("%s fallo: %s", name, tostring(e)))
					end
					pending -= 1
				end)
			end
			while pending > 0 do
				task.wait()
			end
		else
			for _, name in ipairs(order) do
				if not bootOne(name) and not safe then
					bootLog("Error", "Safe Boot desactivado, boot abortado")
					break
				end
			end
		end

		local loaded = 0
		for _, name in ipairs(order) do
			if modules[name].state == "loaded" then
				loaded += 1
			end
		end
		set("Modules Loaded", loaded)
		bootLog("Info", string.format("Boot: %d/%d modulos (%s)", loaded, #order, parallel and "Parallel" or "Sequential"))
		return loaded
	end

	function Zenith.start()
		if started then
			return
		end
		started = true
		task.spawn(function()
			Zenith.probe()
			Zenith.boot()
			Zenith.refreshServices()
		end)
	end

	Zenith.actions["Refresh Services"] = function()
		Zenith.refreshServices()
	end

	Zenith.actions["Cleanup Connections"] = function()
		local n = Zenith.cleanup()
		for _, name in ipairs(order) do
			table.clear(modules[name].owned)
		end
		Zenith.log("Info", string.format("Cleanup Connections: %d desconectadas (GUI intacta)", n))
		Zenith.refreshServices()
	end

	Zenith.actions["Reload Zenith"] = function()
		local strict = flag("Strict Lifecycle", true)
		for i = #order, 1, -1 do
			local name = order[i]
			local m = modules[name]
			if m.state == "loaded" then
				if not stopModule(name) and strict then
					bootLog("Error", "Reload abortado (Strict Lifecycle): " .. name)
					return
				end
			else
				m.state = "pending"
			end
		end
		Zenith.probe()
		Zenith.boot()
		Zenith.refreshServices()
	end

	local prevUnload = Zenith.hooks["Zenith Unload"]
	Zenith.hooks["Zenith Unload"] = function(...)
		for i = #order, 1, -1 do
			local m = modules[order[i]]
			if m.state == "loaded" then
				stopModule(order[i])
			end
		end
		Zenith.cleanup()
		if prevUnload then
			pcall(prevUnload, ...)
		end
	end
end

do
	Zenith.robloxDefaults = {
		services = "CoreGui, CorePackages, RobloxReplicatedStorage, Chat, VoiceChatService, LocalizationService, SocialService, PolicyService, UGCValidationService, AdService, AnalyticsService, TelemetryService",
		scripts = "PlayerModule, CameraModule, ControlModule, Poppercam, Invisicam, BaseCamera, ClassicCamera, OrbitalCamera, VehicleCamera, LegacyCamera, VRBaseCamera, VRCamera, VRVehicleCamera, MouseLockController, BaseCharacterController, Keyboard, Gamepad, TouchJump, TouchThumbstick, DynamicThumbstick, ClickToMoveController, VehicleController, CameraInput, CameraUtils, CameraUI, CameraToggleStateController, ZoomController, TransparencyController, RbxCharacterSounds, AtomicBinding, ChatScript, BubbleChat, ChatMain, ChatBar, ChatChannel, ChatSettings, ChannelsTab, MessageLabelCreator, MessageLogDisplay, MessageSender, CommandProcessor, ChatInstallVerifier, Animate, Health, Freecam, PlayerScriptsLoader, PlayerListManager, TopBarApp, BackpackScript, EmotesMenu, InspectMenu, SelfView, ExperienceChat",
		remotes = "NewPlayerGroupDetails, UpdatePlayerBlockList, SetPlayerBlockList, SendPlayerBlockList, ShowFriendJoinedPlayerToast, SetDialogInUse, SetPlayerReady, GetServerVersion, ClientLoaded, SetCoreGuiEnabled, AddCoreScriptLocal, UserNotification, SendPlayerProfileSettings, RequestServerStats, DefaultChatSystemChatEvents, SayMessageRequest, MutePlayerRequest, OnMessageDoneFiltering, OnNewMessage, OnNewSystemMessage, OnChannelJoined, OnChannelLeft, OnMuted, OnUnmuted, OnMainChannelSet, GetInitDataRequest, ChannelNameColorUpdated, WhisperChat, SetBlockedUserIdsRequest, CanChatWith, NewFollower, GetFollowRelationships, FollowRelationshipChanged, SetPlayerFollowRelationship, PlayerPolicyInfo, TeleportAsync, RequestDeviceCameraCFrame",
	}

	function Zenith.parseCsvSet(raw)
		local out = {}
		for item in string.gmatch(tostring(raw or ""), "[^,]+") do
			local trimmed = string.lower((string.gsub(item, "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")))
			if trimmed ~= "" then
				out[trimmed] = true
			end
		end
		return out
	end
end

do

	for _, o in ipairs({

		GETUDATAKS = "rA rB kU", SETUDATAKS = "rA rB kU", NAMECALLUDATA = "rA rB kU",
		NEWCLASSMEMBER = "rA iB rC iX", CALLFB = "rA iB iC", CMPPROTO = "rA jD iX", FASTPCALL = "fA iB iC",
		NEWCLASS = "rA rB iC kX", CONSTRUCT = "rA rB iX", FINCONSTRUCT = "rA jC",
		GETPRIVATEFIELD = "rA rB uC iX", SETPRIVATEFIELD = "rA rB uC iX",
	}
	for _, n in ipairs({ "ADD", "SUB", "MUL", "DIV", "MOD", "POW", "AND", "OR", "CONCAT", "IDIV" }) do
		SPEC[n] = "rA rB rC"
	end
	for _, n in ipairs({ "ADDK", "SUBK", "MULK", "DIVK", "MODK", "POWK", "ANDK", "ORK", "IDIVK" }) do
		SPEC[n] = "rA rB kC"
	end
	for _, n in ipairs({ "NOT", "MINUS", "LENGTH" }) do
		SPEC[n] = "rA rB"
	end

	local JD = {}
	for _, n in ipairs({
		"JUMP", "JUMPBACK", "JUMPIF", "JUMPIFNOT", "JUMPIFEQ", "JUMPIFLE", "JUMPIFLT", "JUMPIFNOTEQ",
		"JUMPIFNOTLE", "JUMPIFNOTLT", "FORNPREP", "FORNLOOP", "FORGLOOP", "FORGPREP_INEXT", "FORGPREP_NEXT",
		"FORGPREP", "JUMPXEQKNIL", "JUMPXEQKB", "JUMPXEQKN", "JUMPXEQKS", "CMPPROTO",
	}) do
		JD[n] = true
	end

	local CAPK = { [0] = "VAL", "REF", "UPVAL" }

	local function jumpOf(ins)
		if JD[ins.name] then
			return ins.pc + 1 + ins.D
		end
		if ins.name == "JUMPX" then
			return ins.pc + 1 + ins.E
		end
		if (ins.name == "LOADB" or ins.name == "FINCONSTRUCT") and ins.C ~= 0 then
			return ins.pc + 1 + ins.C
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function fmtNum(v)
		if v ~= v then
			return "nan"
		end
		if v == math.huge then
			return "math.huge"
		end
		if v == -math.huge then
			return "-math.huge"
		end
		if v == math.floor(v) and math.abs(v) < 1e15 then
			return string.format("%d", v)
		end
		local s = string.format("%.14g", v)
		if tonumber(s) ~= v then
			s = string.format("%.17g", v)
		end
		return s
	end

	local ESC = { ["\n"] = "\\n", ["\r"] = "\\r", ["\t"] = "\\t", ['"'] = '\\"', ["\\"] = "\\\\" }
	local function quote(s)
		return '"' .. (string.gsub(s, '[%c"\\]', function(c)
			return ESC[c] or string.format("\\%03d", string.byte(c))
		end)) .. '"'
	end

	local function importPath(p, id)
		local count = rshift(id, 30)
		local parts = {}
		for i = 1, count do
			local k = p.k[band(rshift(id, 30 - 10 * i), 1023) + 1]
			parts[i] = (k and k.t == "string" and k.v) or "?"
		end
		return table.concat(parts, ".")
	end

	local function kstr(p, k, full)
		local t = k.t
		if t == "nil" then
			return "nil"
		elseif t == "boolean" then
			return tostring(k.v)
		elseif t == "number" then
			return fmtNum(k.v)
		elseif t == "string" then
			local s = k.v or ""
			if not full and #s > 160 then
				return quote(string.sub(s, 1, 160)) .. "..."
			end
			return quote(s)
		elseif t == "import" then
			return importPath(p, k.id)
		elseif t == "table" then
			local keys = {}
			for i, idx in ipairs(k.keys) do
				local kk = p.k[idx + 1]
				local ks = kk and kstr(p, kk) or "?"
				if k.vals and k.vals[i] ~= nil and k.vals[i] >= 0 then
					local kv = p.k[k.vals[i] + 1]
					keys[i] = ks .. " = " .. (kv and kstr(p, kv) or "?")
				else
					keys[i] = ks
				end
			end
			return "{" .. table.concat(keys, ", ") .. "}"
		elseif t == "closure" then
			return "closure<P" .. k.proto .. ">"
		elseif t == "vector" then
			return string.format("vector(%s, %s, %s, %s)", fmtNum(k.x), fmtNum(k.y), fmtNum(k.z), fmtNum(k.w))
		elseif t == "integer" then
			return string.format("int64(0x%08x%08x)", k.hi or 0, k.lo or 0)
		elseif t == "class" then
			local cn = k.cname and p.k[k.cname + 1]
			return "class<" .. (cn and kstr(p, cn) or "?") .. ">"
		end
		return "?"
	end

	local function opOf(word, key)
		return band(band(word, 255) * key, 255)
	end

	local function validate(p, key)
		local code, n, pc, last = p.code, #p.code, 1, nil
		while pc <= n do
			local op = opOf(code[pc], key)
			if op > OP_MAX then
				return false, false
			end
			last = op
			pc += AUX[op] and 2 or 1
		end
		if pc ~= n + 1 then
			return false, false
		end
		return true, (n == 0 or last == OP_RETURN or last == OP_JUMPBACK)
	end

	local function detectKey(protos)
		local best, bestScore = 1, -1
		local function scoreKey(key)
			local score = 0
			for _, p in ipairs(protos) do
				local ok, cleanEnd = validate(p, key)
				if ok then
					score += cleanEnd and 2 or 1
				end
			end
			return score
		end

		for _, key in ipairs({ 1, 203, 227 }) do
			local score = scoreKey(key)
			if score > bestScore then
				best, bestScore = key, score
			end
		end
		if bestScore >= #protos * 2 then
			return best
		end

		for key = 1, 255, 2 do
			if key ~= 1 and key ~= 203 and key ~= 227 then
				local score = scoreKey(key)
				if score > bestScore then
					best, bestScore = key, score
				end
			end
		end
		return best
	end

	local function parse(bytes)
		local b = buffer.fromstring(bytes)
		local size = buffer.len(b)
		local pos = 0
		local function u8()
			local v = buffer.readu8(b, pos)
			pos += 1
			return v
		end
		local function u32()
			local v = buffer.readu32(b, pos)
			pos += 4
			return v
		end
		local function i32()
			local v = buffer.readi32(b, pos)
			pos += 4
			return v
		end
		local function f32()
			local v = buffer.readf32(b, pos)
			pos += 4
			return v
		end
		local function f64()
			local v = buffer.readf64(b, pos)
			pos += 8
			return v
		end
		local function raw(n)
			local s = buffer.readstring(b, pos, n)
			pos += n
			return s
		end
		local function varint()
			local result, shift = 0, 0
			while true do
				local byte = u8()
				result += band(byte, 0x7F) * 2 ^ shift
				if byte < 0x80 then
					break
				end
				shift += 7
				if shift > 35 then
					error("varint invalido", 0)
				end
			end
			return result
		end
		local function varint64()
			local result, shift = 0, 0
			while true do
				local byte = u8()
				result += band(byte, 0x7F) * 2 ^ shift
				if byte < 0x80 then
					break
				end
				shift += 7
				if shift > 63 then
					error("varint64 invalido", 0)
				end
			end
			return result
		end

		local version = u8()
		if version == 0 then
			error("el script no compilo: " .. raw(size - pos), 0)
		end
		if (version < 3 or version > 14) and version ~= 100 then
			error("version de bytecode no soportada: " .. version, 0)
		end
		local typesVersion = 0
		if version >= 4 then
			typesVersion = u8()
		end

		local strings = {}
		for i = 1, varint() do
			strings[i] = raw(varint())
		end
		local function sref()
			local id = varint()
			if id == 0 then
				return nil
			end
			return strings[id]
		end

		local userdataTypes
		if typesVersion == 3 then
			userdataTypes = {}
			local idx = u8()
			while idx ~= 0 do
				userdataTypes[idx] = sref()
				idx = u8()
			end
		end

		local protos = {}
		for i = 1, varint() do
			local protoSize, protoStart = nil, nil
			if version >= 12 then
				protoSize = varint()
				protoStart = pos
			end

			local p = { index = i - 1 }
			p.maxstack = u8()
			p.numparams = u8()
			p.nups = u8()
			p.vararg = u8() ~= 0
			if version >= 4 then
				p.flags = u8()
				local ts = varint()
				if ts > 0 then
					p.typeinfo = raw(ts)
				end
			end

			local sizecode = varint()
			local code = table.create(sizecode)
			for j = 1, sizecode do
				code[j] = u32()
			end
			p.code = code

			local ks = {}
			for j = 1, varint() do
				local t = u8()
				local k
				if t == 0 then
					k = { t = "nil" }
				elseif t == 1 then
					k = { t = "boolean", v = u8() ~= 0 }
				elseif t == 2 then
					k = { t = "number", v = f64() }
				elseif t == 3 then
					k = { t = "string", v = sref() or "" }
				elseif t == 4 then
					k = { t = "import", id = u32() }
				elseif t == 5 then
					local keys = {}
					for q = 1, varint() do
						keys[q] = varint()
					end
					k = { t = "table", keys = keys }
				elseif t == 6 then
					k = { t = "closure", proto = varint() }
				elseif t == 7 then
					k = { t = "vector", x = f32(), y = f32(), z = f32(), w = f32() }
				elseif t == 8 then
					local keys, vals = {}, {}
					for q = 1, varint() do
						keys[q] = varint()
						vals[q] = i32()
					end
					k = { t = "table", keys = keys, vals = vals }
				elseif t == 9 then
					k = { t = "integer", lo = u32(), hi = u32() }
				elseif t == 10 then
					local cnid = varint()
					local privF = u8()
					local privM = u8()
					local numProps = varint()
					local numMethods = varint()
					local members = {}
					local totalPublic = math.max(0, (numProps - privF) + (numMethods - privM))
					for q = 1, totalPublic do
						members[q] = varint()
					end
					k = { t = "class", cname = cnid, members = members }
				elseif t == 11 then
					k = { t = "vector", x = f64(), y = f64(), z = f64(), w = f64() }
				else
					error(string.format("constante desconocida %d (funcion %d)", t, i - 1), 0)
				end
				ks[j] = k
			end
			p.k = ks

			p.children = {}
			for j = 1, varint() do
				p.children[j] = varint()
			end
			p.linedefined = varint()
			p.name = sref()

			if u8() ~= 0 then
				local gap = u8()
				local offs, last = {}, 0
				for j = 1, sizecode do
					last = (last + u8()) % 256
					offs[j] = last
				end
				local intervals = sizecode > 0 and (rshift(sizecode - 1, gap) + 1) or 0
				local abs, line = {}, 0
				for j = 0, intervals - 1 do
					line += i32()
					abs[j] = line
				end
				local lines = table.create(sizecode)
				for j = 1, sizecode do
					lines[j] = abs[rshift(j - 1, gap)] + offs[j]
				end
				p.lines = lines
			end

			p.locvars, p.upnames = {}, {}
			if u8() ~= 0 then
				p.hasDebug = true
				for j = 1, varint() do
					local name = sref()
					local s = varint()
					local e = varint()
					p.locvars[j] = { name = name, startpc = s, endpc = e, reg = u8() }
				end
				for j = 1, varint() do
					p.upnames[j] = sref()
				end
			end

			if version >= 11 then
				local fbCount = varint()
				for _ = 1, fbCount do
					u8()
					varint()
				end
			end

			if version >= 12 then
				if band(p.flags or 0, 8) ~= 0 then
					p.cost = varint64()
				end
				if protoSize and protoStart then
					pos = protoStart + protoSize
				end
			end

			protos[i] = p
		end

		local main = varint()
		local bc = {
			version = version,
			typesVersion = typesVersion,
			strings = strings,
			protos = protos,
			main = main,
			size = size,
			userdataTypes = userdataTypes,
			trailing = size - pos,
		}
		bc.key = detectKey(protos)
		return bc
	end

	local function decode(bc, p)
		if p.insn then
			return p.insn
		end
		local code, out, pc, n, key = p.code, {}, 1, #p.code, bc.key
		while pc <= n do
			local w = code[pc]
			local op = opOf(w, key)
			local d = rshift(w, 16)
			if d >= 32768 then
				d -= 65536
			end
			local e = rshift(w, 8)
			if e >= 8388608 then
				e -= 16777216
			end
			local ins = {
				pc = pc - 1,
				op = op,
				name = OPN[op] or "?",
				A = band(rshift(w, 8), 255),
				B = band(rshift(w, 16), 255),
				C = band(rshift(w, 24), 255),
				D = d,
				E = e,
				len = 1,
				line = p.lines and p.lines[pc],
			}
			if AUX[op] then
				ins.aux = code[pc + 1] or 0
				ins.len = 2
			end
			out[#out + 1] = ins
			pc += ins.len
		end
		p.insn = out
		return out
	end

	local function link(bc)
		if bc.linked then
			return
		end
		bc.linked = true
		for _, p in ipairs(bc.protos) do
			local insn = decode(bc, p)
			for i, ins in ipairs(insn) do
				local child
				if ins.name == "NEWCLOSURE" then
					child = p.children[ins.D + 1]
				elseif ins.name == "DUPCLOSURE" then
					local k = p.k[ins.D + 1]
					child = k and k.proto
				end
				local cp = child and bc.protos[child + 1]
				if cp then
					if cp.parent == nil then
						cp.parent = p.index
					end
					if not cp.captures then
						local caps, j = {}, i + 1
						while insn[j] and insn[j].name == "CAPTURE" do
							caps[#caps + 1] = { kind = insn[j].A, index = insn[j].B, pc = insn[j].pc }
							j += 1
						end
						cp.captures = caps
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function localAt(p, reg, pc)
		for _, lv in ipairs(p.locvars) do
			if lv.reg == reg and lv.startpc <= pc and pc <= lv.endpc then
				return lv.name
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function field(ins, f)
		if f == "A" then
			return ins.A
		elseif f == "B" then
			return ins.B
		elseif f == "C" then
			return ins.C
		elseif f == "D" then
			return ins.D
		elseif f == "E" then
			return ins.E
		end
		return ins.aux or 0
	end

	local function fmtIns(bc, p, ins)
		local spec = SPEC[ins.name]
		if not spec then
			return "?", nil
		end
		local parts, notes = {}, {}
		local aux = ins.aux or 0
		for tok in string.gmatch(spec, "%S+") do
			local kind, f = string.sub(tok, 1, 1), string.sub(tok, 2, 2)
			if kind == "r" then
				local v
				if f == "X" then
					v = band(aux, 255)
				elseif f == "Y" then
					v = band(rshift(aux, 8), 255)
				else
					v = field(ins, f)
				end
				parts[#parts + 1] = "R" .. v
			elseif kind == "k" then
				local idx
				if f == "N" then
					idx = band(aux, 0xFFFFFF)
				elseif f == "U" then
					idx = band(aux, 0xFFFF)
				else
					idx = field(ins, f)
				end
				parts[#parts + 1] = "K" .. idx
				local k = p.k[idx + 1]
				if k then
					notes[#notes + 1] = kstr(p, k)
				end
			elseif kind == "i" then
				parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(field(ins, f))
			elseif kind == "n" then
				parts[#parts + 1] = tostring(field(ins, f) + 1)
			elseif kind == "j" then
				local t = jumpOf(ins)
				parts[#parts + 1] = t and string.format("->%04d", t) or "-"
			elseif kind == "u" then
				parts[#parts + 1] = "U" .. ins.B
				local nm = p.upnames[ins.B + 1]
				if nm then
					notes[#notes + 1] = nm
				end
			elseif kind == "p" then
				local gi = p.children[ins.D + 1]
				parts[#parts + 1] = "P" .. tostring(gi)
				local cp = gi and bc.protos[gi + 1]
				if cp then
					notes[#notes + 1] = cp.name or "<anon>"
				end
			elseif kind == "c" then
				parts[#parts + 1] = (CAPK[ins.A] or "?") .. " " .. (ins.A == 2 and "U" or "R") .. ins.B
			elseif kind == "f" then
				parts[#parts + 1] = "fast#" .. ins.A
			elseif kind == "x" then
				if aux >= 0x80000000 then
					parts[#parts + 1] = "NOT"
				end
			elseif kind == "b" then
				parts[#parts + 1] = band(aux, 1) == 1 and "true" or "false"
			end
		end
		local n = ins.name
		if n == "CALL" then
			notes[#notes + 1] = string.format("args=%s results=%s", ins.B == 0 and "multret" or ins.B - 1, ins.C == 0 and "multret" or ins.C - 1)
		elseif n == "RETURN" then
			notes[#notes + 1] = "values=" .. (ins.B == 0 and "multret" or tostring(ins.B - 1))
		elseif n == "GETVARARGS" then
			notes[#notes + 1] = "count=" .. (ins.B == 0 and "multret" or tostring(ins.B - 1))
		elseif n == "NEWTABLE" then
			notes[#notes + 1] = string.format("hash=%d array=%d", ins.B == 0 and 0 or 2 ^ (ins.B - 1), aux)
		elseif n == "SETLIST" then
			notes[#notes + 1] = string.format("count=%s start=%d", ins.C == 0 and "multret" or tostring(ins.C - 1), aux)
		elseif n == "FORGLOOP" then
			notes[#notes + 1] = string.format("vars=%d%s", band(aux, 255), aux >= 0x80000000 and " inext" or "")
		end
		return table.concat(parts, " "), #notes > 0 and table.concat(notes, " | ") or nil
	end

	local function sig(p)
		return string.format(
			"function #%d %s (line %d | params %d | upvalues %d | %s | stack %d | %d words)",
			p.index,
			p.name and ("'" .. p.name .. "'") or "<anon>",
			p.linedefined,
			p.numparams,
			p.nups,
			p.vararg and "vararg" or "fixed",
			p.maxstack,
			#p.code
		)
	end

	local function disasm(bc, p, opts, out)
		local insn = decode(bc, p)
		local targets = {}
		for _, ins in ipairs(insn) do
			local t = jumpOf(ins)
			if t then
				targets[t] = true
			end
		end
		for _, ins in ipairs(insn) do
			local ops, notes = fmtIns(bc, p, ins)
			local ln = opts.lineNumbers and string.format("L%-5s ", tostring(ins.line or "?")) or ""
			local line = string.format("%s%04d  %s%-14s %s", targets[ins.pc] and ">" or " ", ins.pc, ln, ins.name, ops)
			line = string.gsub(line, "%s+$", "")
			if notes and not opts.strip then
				line = string.format("%-56s ; %s", line, notes)
			end
			out[#out + 1] = line
		end
	end

	local function header(cur)
		local bc = cur.bc
		return {
			"-- Script   : " .. cur.label,
			"-- Class    : " .. cur.class,
			string.format(
				"-- Bytecode : %d bytes | v%d | types v%d | opcode key %d | %d funciones | main #%d",
				cur.size,
				bc.version,
				bc.typesVersion,
				bc.key,
				#bc.protos,
				bc.main
			),
			"",
		}
	end

	local function constantsOf(p, out)
		for i, k in ipairs(p.k) do
			out[#out + 1] = string.format("  K%-4d %-8s %s", i - 1, k.t, kstr(p, k, true))
		end
		if #p.k == 0 then
			out[#out + 1] = "  (sin constantes)"
		end
	end

	local function upvaluesOf(bc, p, out)
		if p.nups == 0 then
			out[#out + 1] = "  (sin upvalues)"
			return
		end
		local parent = p.parent ~= nil and bc.protos[p.parent + 1]
		for i = 0, p.nups - 1 do
			local name = p.upnames[i + 1] or "?"
			local c = p.captures and p.captures[i + 1]
			local src = "origen desconocido"
			if c and parent then
				if c.kind == 2 then
					src = string.format("upvalue U%d de #%d", c.index, parent.index)
				else
					local ln = localAt(parent, c.index, c.pc)
					src = string.format("%s R%d de #%d%s", CAPK[c.kind] or "?", c.index, parent.index, ln and (" (" .. ln .. ")") or "")
				end
			end
			out[#out + 1] = string.format("  U%-3d %-20s <- %s", i, name, src)
		end
	end

	local BUILD = {}

	BUILD.bytecode = function(cur, opts)
		local out, bc = header(cur), cur.bc
		link(bc)
		for _, p in ipairs(bc.protos) do
			out[#out + 1] = "== " .. sig(p)
			if opts.includeUpvalues then
				out[#out + 1] = "-- upvalues"
				upvaluesOf(bc, p, out)
			end
			if opts.includeConstants then
				out[#out + 1] = "-- constantes"
				constantsOf(p, out)
			end
			disasm(bc, p, opts, out)
			out[#out + 1] = ""
		end
		return out
	end

	BUILD.constants = function(cur)
		local out = header(cur)
		for _, p in ipairs(cur.bc.protos) do
			out[#out + 1] = "== " .. sig(p)
			constantsOf(p, out)
			out[#out + 1] = ""
		end
		return out
	end

	BUILD.upvalues = function(cur)
		local out, bc = header(cur), cur.bc
		link(bc)
		for _, p in ipairs(bc.protos) do
			out[#out + 1] = "== " .. sig(p)
			upvaluesOf(bc, p, out)
			out[#out + 1] = ""
		end
		return out
	end

	BUILD.protos = function(cur)
		local out, bc, seen = header(cur), cur.bc, {}
		link(bc)
		local function visit(idx, depth)
			local p = bc.protos[idx + 1]
			if not p or seen[idx] then
				return
			end
			seen[idx] = true
			out[#out + 1] = string.rep("  ", depth) .. sig(p)
			for _, c in ipairs(p.children) do
				visit(c, depth + 1)
			end
		end
		visit(bc.main, 0)
		for _, p in ipairs(bc.protos) do
			if not seen[p.index] then
				visit(p.index, 0)
			end
		end
		return out
	end

	local function resolveTarget(bc)
		local t = string.match(tostring(Zenith.values[N.target] or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if t == "" then
			return bc.protos[bc.main + 1]
		end
		local n = tonumber(t)
		if n then
			return bc.protos[n + 1]
		end
		local low = string.lower(t)
		for _, p in ipairs(bc.protos) do
			if p.name and string.lower(p.name) == low then
				return p
			end
		end
		for _, p in ipairs(bc.protos) do
			if p.name and string.find(string.lower(p.name), low, 1, true) then
				return p
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	BUILD["function"] = function(cur, opts)
		local out, bc = header(cur), cur.bc
		link(bc)
		local p = resolveTarget(bc)
		if not p then
			out[#out + 1] = "-- Function Target no encontrado (indice o nombre). Vacio = main."
			return out
		end
		out[#out + 1] = "== " .. sig(p)
		if p.parent ~= nil then
			out[#out + 1] = "-- padre: #" .. p.parent
		end
		if #p.children > 0 then
			local ch = {}
			for i, c in ipairs(p.children) do
				ch[i] = "#" .. c
			end
			out[#out + 1] = "-- hijas: " .. table.concat(ch, ", ")
		end
		out[#out + 1] = ""
		out[#out + 1] = "-- upvalues"
		upvaluesOf(bc, p, out)
		out[#out + 1] = ""
		out[#out + 1] = "-- constantes"
		constantsOf(p, out)
		out[#out + 1] = ""
		out[#out + 1] = "-- codigo"
		disasm(bc, p, opts, out)
		return out
	end

	local CFG = (function()
		-- ============================================================================
		-- Zenith Decompiler (Fase 2 - Paso 1): Basic Block + CFG Builder + Pretty Printer
		--
		-- Contrato de entrada:
		--   insn = { { pc, name, A, B, C, D, E, len, aux, line }, ... }
		--   - ins.pc  : indice de PALABRA base 0 (las palabras AUX NO son entradas).
		--   - ins.D   : 16-bit CON SIGNO.  ins.E : 24-bit CON SIGNO.
		--   - ins.len : 2 si la instruccion consume palabra AUX, si no 1.
		--   - ins.aux : palabra AUX (con o sin signo, ambos soportados).
		--
		-- Semantica de cond (bloques que terminan en COND_JUMP):
		--   La arista "taken" se sigue cuando el predicado cond.op es VERDADERO si
		--   cond.negated == false, o FALSO si cond.negated == true.
		--   JUMPIFNOTLT / JUMPIFNOTLE significan `not (a < b)` / `not (a <= b)` y NO
		--   deben reescribirse como >= / > (NaN).
		-- ============================================================================

		local CFG = {}
		local band = bit32.band

		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- 1. Clasificacion de instrucciones
		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- FASTCALL*: no parten bloques. Si la builtin tiene exito la VM continua en
		-- callPc + 1; el rango [skipFrom, callPc] solo se ejecuta si falla.

		local FASTCALL_OPS = {
			FASTCALL = true,
			FASTCALL1 = true,
			FASTCALL2 = true,
			FASTCALL2K = true,
			FASTCALL3 = true,
			FASTPCALL = true,
		}

		local UNCOND_JUMP = {
			JUMP = true,
			JUMPBACK = true,
			JUMPX = true,
		}

		local COND_JUMP = {
			JUMPIF = true,
			JUMPIFNOT = true,
			JUMPIFEQ = true,
			JUMPIFLE = true,
			JUMPIFLT = true,
			JUMPIFNOTEQ = true,
			JUMPIFNOTLE = true,
			JUMPIFNOTLT = true,
			JUMPIFEQK = true,      -- Luau antiguo
			JUMPIFNOTEQK = true,   -- Luau antiguo
			JUMPXEQKNIL = true,
			JUMPXEQKB = true,
			JUMPXEQKN = true,
			JUMPXEQKS = true,
			CMPPROTO = true,
		}

		-- LOADB A,B,C (C ~= 0) y FINCONSTRUCT (C ~= 0) saltan siempre
		local COND_SKIP_UNCOND = {
			LOADB = true,
			FINCONSTRUCT = true,
		}

		local FOR_GEN_PREP = {
			FORGPREP = true,
			FORGPREP_INEXT = true,
			FORGPREP_NEXT = true,
		}

		local GEN_LOOP = {
			FORGLOOP = true,
			FORGLOOP_INEXT = true,
			FORGLOOP_NEXT = true,
		}

		local FOR_LOOP = {
			FORNLOOP = true,
			FORGLOOP = true,
			FORGLOOP_INEXT = true,
			FORGLOOP_NEXT = true,
		}

		-- Instrucciones cuyo destino es pc + 1 + D
		local JD_DEFAULT = {
			JUMP = true, JUMPBACK = true, JUMPIF = true, JUMPIFNOT = true,
			JUMPIFEQ = true, JUMPIFLE = true, JUMPIFLT = true,
			JUMPIFNOTEQ = true, JUMPIFNOTLE = true, JUMPIFNOTLT = true,
			JUMPIFEQK = true, JUMPIFNOTEQK = true,
			FORNPREP = true, FORNLOOP = true, FORGLOOP = true,
			FORGLOOP_INEXT = true, FORGLOOP_NEXT = true,
			FORGPREP_INEXT = true, FORGPREP_NEXT = true, FORGPREP = true,
			JUMPXEQKNIL = true, JUMPXEQKB = true, JUMPXEQKN = true, JUMPXEQKS = true,
			CMPPROTO = true,
		}

		local function defaultJumpOf(ins)
			if JD_DEFAULT[ins.name] then
				return ins.pc + 1 + (ins.D or 0)
			end
			if ins.name == "JUMPX" then
				return ins.pc + 1 + (ins.E or 0)
			end
			if COND_SKIP_UNCOND[ins.name] and (ins.C or 0) ~= 0 then
				return ins.pc + 1 + ins.C
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function describeCond(ins)
			local n = ins.name
			if not COND_JUMP[n] then
				return nil
			end
			local aux = ins.aux or 0
			local notBit = band(aux, 0x80000000) ~= 0
			local auxReg = band(aux, 255)
			local auxK = band(aux, 0xFFFFFF)

			if n == "JUMPIF" then
				return { op = "truthy", regA = ins.A, negated = false }
			elseif n == "JUMPIFNOT" then
				return { op = "truthy", regA = ins.A, negated = true }
			elseif n == "JUMPIFEQ" then
				return { op = "eq", regA = ins.A, regB = auxReg, negated = false }
			elseif n == "JUMPIFNOTEQ" then
				return { op = "eq", regA = ins.A, regB = auxReg, negated = true }
			elseif n == "JUMPIFLT" then
				return { op = "lt", regA = ins.A, regB = auxReg, negated = false }
			elseif n == "JUMPIFNOTLT" then
				return { op = "lt", regA = ins.A, regB = auxReg, negated = true, nanSensitive = true }
			elseif n == "JUMPIFLE" then
				return { op = "le", regA = ins.A, regB = auxReg, negated = false }
			elseif n == "JUMPIFNOTLE" then
				return { op = "le", regA = ins.A, regB = auxReg, negated = true, nanSensitive = true }
			elseif n == "JUMPIFEQK" then
				return { op = "eqk", regA = ins.A, kIdx = auxK, negated = false }
			elseif n == "JUMPIFNOTEQK" then
				return { op = "eqk", regA = ins.A, kIdx = auxK, negated = true }
			elseif n == "JUMPXEQKNIL" then
				return { op = "eq_nil", regA = ins.A, negated = notBit }
			elseif n == "JUMPXEQKB" then
				return { op = "eq_bool", regA = ins.A, val = band(aux, 1) == 1, negated = notBit }
			elseif n == "JUMPXEQKN" or n == "JUMPXEQKS" then
				return { op = "eq_k", regA = ins.A, kIdx = auxK, negated = notBit }
