			elseif n == "CMPPROTO" then
				return { op = "cmpproto", regA = ins.A, aux = aux, negated = false }
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function isControlFlow(ins, jumpOfFn)
			local name = ins.name
			if name == "RETURN" or UNCOND_JUMP[name] or COND_JUMP[name]
				or FOR_GEN_PREP[name] or name == "FORNPREP" or FOR_LOOP[name] then
				return true
			end
			if COND_SKIP_UNCOND[name] and jumpOfFn(ins) ~= nil then
				return true
			end
			return false
		end

		-- Aristas salientes de la ultima instruccion de un bloque.
		-- Devuelve lista de { targetPc, kind }.
		local function outgoingOf(ins, nextPc, target)
			local name = ins.name

			if name == "RETURN" then
				return {}
			end

			if UNCOND_JUMP[name] then
				if target then
					local kind = (name == "JUMPBACK" or target <= ins.pc) and "jump_back" or "jump"
					return { { targetPc = target, kind = kind } }
				end
				return {}
			end

			if COND_SKIP_UNCOND[name] then
				if target then
					return { { targetPc = target, kind = "jump" } }
				end
				if nextPc then
					return { { targetPc = nextPc, kind = "fallthrough" } }
				end
				return {}
			end

			if FOR_GEN_PREP[name] then
				if target then
					return { { targetPc = target, kind = "for_prep" } }
				end
				return {}
			end

			if name == "FORNPREP" then
				local out = {}
				if nextPc then
					out[#out + 1] = { targetPc = nextPc, kind = "for_enter" }
				end
				if target then
					out[#out + 1] = { targetPc = target, kind = "for_skip" }
				end
				return out
			end

			if FOR_LOOP[name] then
				local out = {}
				if target then
					out[#out + 1] = { targetPc = target, kind = "for_loop" }
				end
				if nextPc then
					out[#out + 1] = { targetPc = nextPc, kind = "for_exit" }
				end
				return out
			end

			if COND_JUMP[name] then
				local out = {}
				if target then
					out[#out + 1] = { targetPc = target, kind = "taken" }
				end
				if nextPc then
					out[#out + 1] = { targetPc = nextPc, kind = "fallthrough" }
				end
				return out
			end

			if nextPc then
				return { { targetPc = nextPc, kind = "fallthrough" } }
			end
			return {}
		end

		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- 2. Construccion de Basic Blocks y CFG
		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		function CFG.build(insn, opts)
			opts = opts or {}
			local jumpOfFn = opts.jumpOf or defaultJumpOf

			local cfg = {
				blocks = {},
				byStartPc = {},
				entry = nil,
				exits = {},
				rpo = {},
				loops = {},            -- de mas interno a mas externo
				fastcalls = {},
				errors = {},           -- problemas graves (destinos invalidos)
				warnings = {},         -- inconsistencias de entrada
				irreducible = false,
				irreducibleEdges = {},
			}

			local n = #insn
			if n == 0 then
				return cfg
			end

			-- pc -> indice, con validacion + cache de saltos
			local pcToIdx = {}
			local jumpTarget = table.create(n)
			local ctrlFlow = table.create(n, false)
			for i, ins in ipairs(insn) do
				if pcToIdx[ins.pc] then
					cfg.warnings[#cfg.warnings + 1] = string.format("pc duplicado: %d", ins.pc)
				else
					pcToIdx[ins.pc] = i
				end
				local nxt = insn[i + 1]
				if nxt then
					if nxt.pc <= ins.pc then
						cfg.warnings[#cfg.warnings + 1] = string.format(
							"pc no creciente: %d -> %d", ins.pc, nxt.pc)
					elseif nxt.pc ~= ins.pc + (ins.len or 1) then
						cfg.warnings[#cfg.warnings + 1] = string.format(
							"pc %d (%s) len=%d pero la siguiente instruccion esta en pc %d",
							ins.pc, tostring(ins.name), ins.len or 1, nxt.pc)
					end
				end
				local t = jumpOfFn(ins)
				jumpTarget[i] = t
				ctrlFlow[i] = isControlFlow(ins, function()
					return t
				end)
			end

			-- FASTCALL ranges
			for _, ins in ipairs(insn) do
				if FASTCALL_OPS[ins.name] and ins.C ~= nil then
					local callPc = ins.pc + 1 + ins.C
					cfg.fastcalls[#cfg.fastcalls + 1] = {
						pc = ins.pc,
						skipFrom = ins.pc + (ins.len or 1),
						callPc = callPc,
						resumePc = callPc + 1,
						op = ins.name,
						fastId = ins.A,
					}
					if not pcToIdx[callPc] then
						cfg.warnings[#cfg.warnings + 1] = string.format(
							"%s en pc %d: callPc %d no existe", ins.name, ins.pc, callPc)
					end
				end
			end

			-- Paso A: leaders
			local isLeader = {}
			isLeader[insn[1].pc] = true
			for i = 1, n do
				if ctrlFlow[i] then
					local target = jumpTarget[i]
					if target and pcToIdx[target] then
						isLeader[target] = true
					end
					local nextIns = insn[i + 1]
					if nextIns then
						isLeader[nextIns.pc] = true
					end
				end
			end

			-- Paso B: cortar bloques
			local blocks = {}
			local curBlock = nil

			local function flushBlock()
				if curBlock and #curBlock.insns > 0 then
					local lastIns = curBlock.insns[#curBlock.insns]
					curBlock.endPc = lastIns.pc
					curBlock.cond = describeCond(lastIns)
					blocks[#blocks + 1] = curBlock
				end
				curBlock = nil
			end

			for i, ins in ipairs(insn) do
				if isLeader[ins.pc] then
					flushBlock()
					curBlock = {
						id = #blocks + 1,
						startPc = ins.pc,
						endPc = ins.pc,
						insns = {},
						succ = {},          -- deduplicado
						pred = {},          -- deduplicado
						edgesOut = {},      -- Edge compartido con edgesIn
						edgesIn = {},
						cond = nil,
						reachable = false,
						rpoIndex = nil,
						idom = nil,
						domChildren = nil,
						domIn = nil,
						domOut = nil,
						ipdom = nil,        -- nil = salida virtual o sin camino a salida
						ipdomIsExit = false,-- true si ipdom es la salida virtual de la funcion
						loopHeader = nil,
						_lastIdx = i,
					}
					cfg.byStartPc[ins.pc] = curBlock
				end

				curBlock.insns[#curBlock.insns + 1] = ins
				curBlock._lastIdx = i

				local nextIns = insn[i + 1]
				if ctrlFlow[i] or (nextIns and isLeader[nextIns.pc]) then
					flushBlock()
				end
			end
			flushBlock()

			-- Paso C: aristas
			local succSeen, predSeen = {}, {}
			for _, b in ipairs(blocks) do
				succSeen[b.id] = {}
				predSeen[b.id] = {}
			end

			for idx, blk in ipairs(blocks) do
				local lastIns = blk.insns[#blk.insns]
				local lastIdx = blk._lastIdx
				blk._lastIdx = nil
				local nextBlk = blocks[idx + 1]
				local nextPc = nextBlk and nextBlk.startPc or nil
				local outs = outgoingOf(lastIns, nextPc, jumpTarget[lastIdx])

				for _, e in ipairs(outs) do
					local targetBlk = cfg.byStartPc[e.targetPc]
					if targetBlk then
						local edge = { from = blk, to = targetBlk, kind = e.kind, isBack = false }
						blk.edgesOut[#blk.edgesOut + 1] = edge
						targetBlk.edgesIn[#targetBlk.edgesIn + 1] = edge

						if not succSeen[blk.id][targetBlk.id] then
							succSeen[blk.id][targetBlk.id] = true
							blk.succ[#blk.succ + 1] = targetBlk
						end
						if not predSeen[targetBlk.id][blk.id] then
							predSeen[targetBlk.id][blk.id] = true
							targetBlk.pred[#targetBlk.pred + 1] = blk
						end
					else
						cfg.errors[#cfg.errors + 1] = string.format(
							"arista sin bloque destino (%s en pc %d -> target pc %d)",
							tostring(lastIns.name), lastIns.pc, e.targetPc)
					end
				end
			end

			cfg.blocks = blocks
			cfg.entry = blocks[1]

			-- Paso D: RPO iterativo
			if cfg.entry then
				local visited, postorder = {}, {}
				local stack = { { blk = cfg.entry, i = 1 } }
				visited[cfg.entry.id] = true
				cfg.entry.reachable = true

				while #stack > 0 do
					local top = stack[#stack]
					local nxt = top.blk.succ[top.i]
					if nxt then
						top.i += 1
						if not visited[nxt.id] then
							visited[nxt.id] = true
							nxt.reachable = true
							stack[#stack + 1] = { blk = nxt, i = 1 }
						end
					else
						postorder[#postorder + 1] = top.blk
						stack[#stack] = nil
					end
				end

				for i = #postorder, 1, -1 do
					local blk = postorder[i]
					blk.rpoIndex = #cfg.rpo + 1
					cfg.rpo[blk.rpoIndex] = blk
				end
			end

			for _, blk in ipairs(blocks) do
				if blk.reachable and #blk.succ == 0 then
					cfg.exits[#cfg.exits + 1] = blk
				end
			end

			-- Paso E
			CFG._computeDominators(cfg)
			CFG._computePostDominators(cfg)
			CFG._findLoops(cfg)

			return cfg
		end

		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- 3. Dominadores, post-dominadores y bucles naturales
		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------

		-- True si 'a' domina a 'b' (ambos alcanzables). O(1).
		function CFG._dominates(a, b)
			if a.domIn == nil or b.domIn == nil then
				return false
			end
			return a.domIn <= b.domIn and b.domOut <= a.domOut
		end
		CFG.dominates = CFG._dominates

		local function intersectBy(b1, b2, domField, rpoField)
			local f1, f2 = b1, b2
			while f1 ~= f2 do
				while f1[rpoField] > f2[rpoField] do
					f1 = f1[domField]
				end
				while f2[rpoField] > f1[rpoField] do
					f2 = f2[domField]
				end
			end
			return f1
		end

		function CFG._computeDominators(cfg)
			local entry = cfg.entry
			if not entry then
				return
			end
			entry.idom = entry

			local changed = true
			while changed do
				changed = false
				for i = 2, #cfg.rpo do
					local b = cfg.rpo[i]
					local newIdom = nil
					for _, p in ipairs(b.pred) do
						if p.reachable and p.idom ~= nil then
							if newIdom == nil then
								newIdom = p
							else
								newIdom = intersectBy(p, newIdom, "idom", "rpoIndex")
							end
						end
					end
					if newIdom and b.idom ~= newIdom then
						b.idom = newIdom
						changed = true
					end
				end
			end

			-- Arbol de dominadores + numeracion enter/exit para consultas O(1)
			for _, b in ipairs(cfg.rpo) do
				b.domChildren = {}
			end
			for i = 2, #cfg.rpo do
				local b = cfg.rpo[i]
				if b.idom then
					local c = b.idom.domChildren
					c[#c + 1] = b
				end
			end

			local t = 0
			entry.domIn = t
			t += 1
			local stack = { { blk = entry, i = 1 } }
			while #stack > 0 do
				local top = stack[#stack]
				local child = top.blk.domChildren[top.i]
				if child then
					top.i += 1
					child.domIn = t
					t += 1
					stack[#stack + 1] = { blk = child, i = 1 }
				else
					top.blk.domOut = t
					t += 1
					stack[#stack] = nil
				end
			end
		end

		function CFG._computePostDominators(cfg)
			if #cfg.rpo == 0 then
				return
			end

			local virtualExit = {
				id = 0,
				pred = table.clone(cfg.exits),
				succ = {},
				revRpoIndex = 1,
				ipdom = nil,
			}
			virtualExit.ipdom = virtualExit

			local isExit = {}
			for _, ex in ipairs(cfg.exits) do
				isExit[ex.id] = true
			end

			-- DFS sobre el grafo inverso desde salidas reales y pseudo-salidas (bucles infinitos)
			local visited, revPost = { [0] = true }, {}
			local function walk(startBlk)
				if visited[startBlk.id] then
					return
				end
				visited[startBlk.id] = true
				local stack = { { blk = startBlk, i = 1 } }
				while #stack > 0 do
					local top = stack[#stack]
					local prev = top.blk.pred[top.i]
					if prev then
						top.i += 1
						if prev.reachable and not visited[prev.id] then
							visited[prev.id] = true
							stack[#stack + 1] = { blk = prev, i = 1 }
						end
					else
						revPost[#revPost + 1] = top.blk
						stack[#stack] = nil
					end
				end
			end

			for _, ex in ipairs(cfg.exits) do
				walk(ex)
			end
			for i = #cfg.rpo, 1, -1 do
				local b = cfg.rpo[i]
				if not visited[b.id] then
					isExit[b.id] = true
					virtualExit.pred[#virtualExit.pred + 1] = b
					walk(b)
				end
			end
			revPost[#revPost + 1] = virtualExit

			local revRpo = {}
			for i = #revPost, 1, -1 do
				local b = revPost[i]
				b.revRpoIndex = #revRpo + 1
				revRpo[b.revRpoIndex] = b
			end

			local changed = true
			while changed do
				changed = false
				for i = 2, #revRpo do
					local b = revRpo[i]
					local newIpdom = isExit[b.id] and virtualExit or nil
					for _, s in ipairs(b.succ) do
						if s.revRpoIndex and s.ipdom ~= nil then
							if newIpdom == nil then
								newIpdom = s
							else
								newIpdom = intersectBy(s, newIpdom, "ipdom", "revRpoIndex")
							end
						end
					end
					if newIpdom and b.ipdom ~= newIpdom then
						b.ipdom = newIpdom
						changed = true
					end
				end
			end

			for _, b in ipairs(revRpo) do
				if b ~= virtualExit then
					if b.ipdom == virtualExit then
						b.ipdom = nil
						b.ipdomIsExit = true
					end
					b.revRpoIndex = nil
				end
			end
		end

		local function lastInsOf(b)
			return b.insns[#b.insns]
		end

		local function isPostTestedLatch(loop, e)
			-- Patron real de Luau para repeat ... until:
			-- cond (dentro del bucle, taken sale del bucle) -> fallthrough -> latch (JUMPBACK -> header)
			if e.kind == "taken" then
				local l = lastInsOf(e.from)
				return l ~= nil and COND_JUMP[l.name] == true
			end
			if e.kind ~= "jump_back" then
				return false
			end
			local latch = e.from
			if #latch.insns ~= 1 or latch.insns[1].name ~= "JUMPBACK" then
				return false
			end
			if #latch.edgesIn ~= 1 then
				return false
			end
			local inEdge = latch.edgesIn[1]
			if inEdge.kind ~= "fallthrough" then
				return false
			end
			local c = inEdge.from
			local cl = lastInsOf(c)
			if not (cl and COND_JUMP[cl.name]) or not loop.body[c.id] then
				return false
			end
			for _, o in ipairs(c.edgesOut) do
				if o.kind == "taken" and loop.body[o.to.id] then
					return false
				end
			end
			-- Si hay debug lines: en `repeat ... until cond`, JUMPBACK comparte linea con el `until`;
			-- en `while c do if y then break end end`, el `if` y el `JUMPBACK` (linea del `while`) difieren.
			local jLine = latch.insns[1].line
			if cl.line ~= nil and jLine ~= nil and cl.line ~= jLine then
				return false
			end
			return true
		end

		local function classifyLoopKind(loop)
			-- generic for: la cabecera ES el bloque del FORGLOOP
			local hl = lastInsOf(loop.header)
			if hl and GEN_LOOP[hl.name] then
				return "generic_for"
			end

			-- numeric for: la cabecera es el inicio del cuerpo; el latch termina en FORNLOOP
			for _, e in ipairs(loop.backEdges) do
				local l = lastInsOf(e.from)
				if l and l.name == "FORNLOOP" and e.kind == "for_loop" then
					return "numeric_for"
				end
			end

			-- repeat ... until: patron real (COND_JUMP -> exit + JUMPBACK) o directo
			if #loop.backEdges > 0 then
				local all = true
				for _, e in ipairs(loop.backEdges) do
					if not isPostTestedLatch(loop, e) then
						all = false
						break
					end
				end
				if all then
					return "repeat_until"
				end
			end

			return "while_or_loop"
		end

		function CFG._findLoops(cfg)
			local byHeader = {}
			local latchSeen = {}

			for _, u in ipairs(cfg.rpo) do
				for _, e in ipairs(u.edgesOut) do
					local h = e.to
					if h.reachable then
						if CFG._dominates(h, u) then
							e.isBack = true
							local loop = byHeader[h.id]
							if not loop then
								loop = {
									header = h,
									latches = {},
									backEdges = {},
									exits = {},
									blocks = {},
									body = { [h.id] = true },
									kind = "while_or_loop",
									parent = nil,
									depth = 1,
								}
								byHeader[h.id] = loop
								latchSeen[h.id] = {}
								cfg.loops[#cfg.loops + 1] = loop
							end
							loop.backEdges[#loop.backEdges + 1] = e
							if not latchSeen[h.id][u.id] then
								latchSeen[h.id][u.id] = true
								loop.latches[#loop.latches + 1] = u
							end

							if not loop.body[u.id] then
								loop.body[u.id] = true
								local stack = { u }
								while #stack > 0 do
									local cur = table.remove(stack)
									for _, p in ipairs(cur.pred) do
										if p.reachable and not loop.body[p.id] then
											loop.body[p.id] = true
											stack[#stack + 1] = p
										end
									end
								end
							end
						elseif h.rpoIndex <= u.rpoIndex then
							-- arista que retrocede en RPO sin dominancia: irreducible
							cfg.irreducible = true
							cfg.irreducibleEdges[#cfg.irreducibleEdges + 1] = e
						end
					end
				end
			end

			-- de mas interno a mas externo
			table.sort(cfg.loops, function(a, b)
				return a.header.rpoIndex > b.header.rpoIndex
			end)

			for i, loop in ipairs(cfg.loops) do
				loop.kind = classifyLoopKind(loop)

				for _, blk in ipairs(cfg.rpo) do
					if loop.body[blk.id] then
						loop.blocks[#loop.blocks + 1] = blk
						if blk.loopHeader == nil then
							blk.loopHeader = loop.header
						end
						for _, e in ipairs(blk.edgesOut) do
							if not loop.body[e.to.id] then
								loop.exits[#loop.exits + 1] = e
							end
						end
					end
				end

				for j = i + 1, #cfg.loops do
					local outer = cfg.loops[j]
					if outer.body[loop.header.id] then
						loop.parent = outer
						break
					end
				end
			end

			for i = #cfg.loops, 1, -1 do
				local loop = cfg.loops[i]
				loop.depth = loop.parent and (loop.parent.depth + 1) or 1
			end
		end

		CFG._describeCond = describeCond

		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- 4. Pretty Printer
		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		local function defaultFmtIns(ins)
			local parts = {}
			if ins.A ~= nil then parts[#parts + 1] = "A=" .. tostring(ins.A) end
			if ins.B ~= nil then parts[#parts + 1] = "B=" .. tostring(ins.B) end
			if ins.C ~= nil then parts[#parts + 1] = "C=" .. tostring(ins.C) end
			if ins.D ~= nil and ins.D ~= 0 then parts[#parts + 1] = "D=" .. tostring(ins.D) end
			if ins.E ~= nil and ins.E ~= 0 then parts[#parts + 1] = "E=" .. tostring(ins.E) end
			if ins.aux ~= nil then parts[#parts + 1] = "aux=" .. tostring(ins.aux) end
			return table.concat(parts, " "), nil
		end

		function CFG.format(cfg, opts)
			opts = opts or {}
			local fmtInsFn = opts.fmtIns or defaultFmtIns
			local out = {}

			local edgeCount, unreachableCount = 0, 0
			for _, b in ipairs(cfg.blocks) do
				edgeCount += #b.edgesOut
				if not b.reachable then
					unreachableCount += 1
				end
			end

			out[#out + 1] = string.format(
				"-- CFG: %d bloques | %d aristas | %d bucles | %d inalcanzables%s",
				#cfg.blocks, edgeCount, #cfg.loops, unreachableCount,
				cfg.irreducible and " | IRREDUCIBLE" or "")

			for _, err in ipairs(cfg.errors) do
				out[#out + 1] = "-- ERROR: " .. err
			end
			for _, w in ipairs(cfg.warnings) do
				out[#out + 1] = "-- WARN: " .. w
			end
			for _, fc in ipairs(cfg.fastcalls) do
				out[#out + 1] = string.format(
					"-- FASTCALL: %s pc=%d id=%s fallback=[%d..%d] resume=%d",
					fc.op, fc.pc, tostring(fc.fastId), fc.skipFrom, fc.callPc, fc.resumePc)
			end

			if #cfg.loops > 0 then
				local loopDesc = {}
				for i, loop in ipairs(cfg.loops) do
					local count = 0
					for _ in pairs(loop.body) do
						count += 1
					end
					local backs = {}
					for _, be in ipairs(loop.backEdges) do
						backs[#backs + 1] = string.format("B%d(%s)", be.from.id, be.kind)
					end
					local exits = {}
					for _, ex in ipairs(loop.exits) do
						exits[#exits + 1] = string.format("B%d->B%d", ex.from.id, ex.to.id)
					end
					loopDesc[i] = string.format(
						"L%d[%s d=%d]: header=B%d bloques=%d back=[%s] exits=[%s]",
						i, loop.kind, loop.depth, loop.header.id, count,
						table.concat(backs, ", "), table.concat(exits, ", "))
				end
				out[#out + 1] = "-- Bucles: " .. table.concat(loopDesc, " ; ")
			end
			out[#out + 1] = ""

			for _, b in ipairs(cfg.blocks) do
				local tags = {}
				if b == cfg.entry then tags[#tags + 1] = "entry" end
				if #b.succ == 0 and b.reachable then tags[#tags + 1] = "exit" end
				if not b.reachable then tags[#tags + 1] = "UNREACHABLE" end
				if b.loopHeader == b then tags[#tags + 1] = "loop_header" end

				local meta = {}
				if b.idom and b ~= cfg.entry then
					meta[#meta + 1] = "idom=B" .. b.idom.id
				end
				if b.ipdom then
					meta[#meta + 1] = "ipdom=B" .. b.ipdom.id
				elseif b.ipdomIsExit then
					meta[#meta + 1] = "ipdom=EXIT"
				end
				if b.loopHeader and b.loopHeader ~= b then
					meta[#meta + 1] = "in_loop=B" .. b.loopHeader.id
				end
				if b.cond then
					meta[#meta + 1] = "cond=" .. b.cond.op .. (b.cond.negated and "(not)" or "")
				end

				local preds = {}
				for _, e in ipairs(b.edgesIn) do
					preds[#preds + 1] = string.format("B%d(%s%s)", e.from.id, e.kind, e.isBack and ",BACK" or "")
				end
				local succs = {}
				for _, e in ipairs(b.edgesOut) do
					succs[#succs + 1] = string.format("B%d(%s%s)", e.to.id, e.kind, e.isBack and ",BACK" or "")
				end

				local tagStr = #tags > 0 and (" [" .. table.concat(tags, ", ") .. "]") or ""
				local metaStr = #meta > 0 and (" {" .. table.concat(meta, ", ") .. "}") or ""
				out[#out + 1] = string.format("B%d (pc %04d..%04d)%s%s", b.id, b.startPc, b.endPc, tagStr, metaStr)
				out[#out + 1] = "  pred: " .. (#preds > 0 and table.concat(preds, ", ") or "(none)")
				out[#out + 1] = "  succ: " .. (#succs > 0 and table.concat(succs, ", ") or "(none)")

				for _, ins in ipairs(b.insns) do
					local lineStr = ins.line ~= nil and string.format("L%-4s ", tostring(ins.line)) or "      "
					local text, note = fmtInsFn(ins)
					local suffix = (note and note ~= "") and (" ; " .. note) or ""
					out[#out + 1] = string.format("    %04d  %s%-14s %s%s",
						ins.pc, lineStr, tostring(ins.name), text or "", suffix)
				end
				out[#out + 1] = ""
			end

			return out
		end

		function CFG.formatString(cfg, opts)
			return table.concat(CFG.format(cfg, opts), "\n")
		end

		return CFG
	end)()

	local Structurer = (function()
		-- ============================================================================
		-- Zenith Decompiler (Fase 2 - Paso 2): Control-Flow Structurer + Pretty Printer
		--
		-- Consume un `cfg` generado por `CFG.build(insn, opts)` y reconstruye un arbol
		-- estructurado de sentencias (`Seq`, `If`, `While`, `RepeatUntil`, `NumericFor`,
		-- `GenericFor`, `Break`, `Continue`, `BlockRef`, `GotoFallback`) sin bucles
		-- infinitos ni cuelgues ante grafos complejos o irreducibles.
		-- ============================================================================

		local Structurer = {}

		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- 1. Utilidades de inspeccion de bloques y condiciones
		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		local PURE_COND_OPS = {
			NOP = true, LOADNIL = true, LOADN = true, LOADK = true, LOADKX = true,
			MOVE = true, GETGLOBAL = true, GETUPVAL = true, GETIMPORT = true,
			GETTABLE = true, GETTABLEKS = true, GETTABLEN = true, GETUDATAKS = true,
			ADD = true, SUB = true, MUL = true, DIV = true, IDIV = true, MOD = true, POW = true,
			ADDK = true, SUBK = true, MULK = true, DIVK = true, IDIVK = true, MODK = true, POWK = true,
			SUBRK = true, DIVRK = true, CONCAT = true, NOT = true, MINUS = true, LENGTH = true,
		}

		local function lastInsOf(blk)
			return blk and blk.insns and blk.insns[#blk.insns] or nil
		end

		local function edgeByKind(blk, kind)
			for _, e in ipairs(blk.edgesOut) do
				if e.kind == kind then
					return e
				end
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function insReadsReg(ins, reg)
			local n = ins.name
			if n == "NOP" or n == "COVERAGE" or n == "PREPVARARGS" or n == "NATIVECALL"
				or n == "LOADNIL" or n == "LOADB" or n == "LOADN" or n == "LOADK" or n == "LOADKX"
				or n == "GETGLOBAL" or n == "GETUPVAL" or n == "GETIMPORT"
				or n == "NEWTABLE" or n == "DUPTABLE" or n == "NEWCLOSURE" or n == "DUPCLOSURE"
				or n == "GETVARARGS" or n == "JUMP" or n == "JUMPBACK" or n == "JUMPX" then
				return false
			end
			if n == "MOVE" or n == "NOT" or n == "MINUS" or n == "LENGTH"
				or n == "GETTABLEKS" or n == "GETTABLEN" or n == "GETUDATAKS"
				or n == "ADDK" or n == "SUBK" or n == "MULK" or n == "DIVK" or n == "IDIVK"
				or n == "MODK" or n == "POWK" or n == "ANDK" or n == "ORK" or n == "NAMECALL" or n == "NAMECALLUDATA" then
				return ins.B == reg
			end
			if n == "SUBRK" or n == "DIVRK" then
				return ins.C == reg
			end
			if n == "GETTABLE" or n == "ADD" or n == "SUB" or n == "MUL" or n == "DIV"
				or n == "IDIV" or n == "MOD" or n == "POW" or n == "AND" or n == "OR" then
				return ins.B == reg or ins.C == reg
			end
			if n == "CONCAT" then
				return reg >= ins.B and reg <= ins.C
			end
			if n == "SETTABLE" then
				return ins.A == reg or ins.B == reg or ins.C == reg
			end
			if n == "SETTABLEKS" or n == "SETTABLEN" or n == "SETUDATAKS" then
				return ins.A == reg or ins.B == reg
			end
			if n == "SETGLOBAL" or n == "SETUPVAL" or n == "JUMPIF" or n == "JUMPIFNOT"
				or n == "JUMPXEQKNIL" or n == "JUMPXEQKB" or n == "JUMPXEQKN" or n == "JUMPXEQKS" then
				return ins.A == reg
			end
			if n == "JUMPIFEQ" or n == "JUMPIFLE" or n == "JUMPIFLT"
				or n == "JUMPIFNOTEQ" or n == "JUMPIFNOTLE" or n == "JUMPIFNOTLT" then
				return ins.A == reg or bit32.band(ins.aux or 0, 255) == reg
			end
			if n == "RETURN" then
				if ins.B == 0 then
					return reg >= ins.A
				end
				return reg >= ins.A and reg <= ins.A + ins.B - 2
			end
			if n == "CALL" or n == "CALLFB" then
				if ins.B == 0 then
					return reg >= ins.A
				end
				return reg >= ins.A and reg <= ins.A + ins.B - 1
			end
			return true
		end

		local function isRegLiveFrom(reg, startBlk)
			if not startBlk then
				return false
			end
			local seen, queue, head = { [startBlk.id] = true }, { startBlk }, 1
			while head <= #queue do
				local b = queue[head]
				head += 1
				local killed = false
				for _, ins in ipairs(b.insns) do
					if insReadsReg(ins, reg) then
						return true
					end
					if PURE_COND_OPS[ins.name] and ins.name ~= "NOP" and ins.A == reg then
						killed = true
						break
					end
				end
				if not killed then
					for _, e in ipairs(b.edgesOut) do
						if not e.virtual and e.to and not seen[e.to.id] then
							seen[e.to.id] = true
							queue[#queue + 1] = e.to
						end
					end
				end
			end
			return false
		end

		local function isPureCondBlock(blk)
			if not blk or not blk.cond then
				return false
			end
			local insns = blk.insns
			local nTaken = edgeByKind(blk, "taken")
			local nFall = edgeByKind(blk, "fallthrough")
			for i = 1, #insns - 1 do
				local ins = insns[i]
				if not PURE_COND_OPS[ins.name] then
					return false
				end
				if ins.name ~= "NOP" then
					if (nTaken and isRegLiveFrom(ins.A, nTaken.to)) or (nFall and isRegLiveFrom(ins.A, nFall.to)) then
						return false
					end
				end
			end
			return true
		end

		local function makeAtom(blk, wantTaken)
			local cond = blk.cond
			local effNeg = (not wantTaken) ~= (cond.negated == true)
			return {
				kind = "atom",
				block = blk,
				cond = cond,
				wantTaken = wantTaken,
				negated = effNeg,
			}
		end

		local function negateCond(c)
			if c.kind == "not" then
				return c.inner
			elseif c.kind == "atom" then
				return {
					kind = "atom",
					block = c.block,
					cond = c.cond,
					wantTaken = not c.wantTaken,
					negated = not c.negated,
				}
			end
			return { kind = "not", inner = c }
		end

		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- 2. Paso 1: Fusion de condiciones short-circuit (and / or)
		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- Dada una cabecera condicional `blk`, intenta fusionar bloques sucesores de
		-- condicion pura en un unico CondExpr + (trueTarget, falseTarget, consumed).
		--
		-- Identidad algebraica en Luau:
		--   `blk` entra en `fall` cuando `onFall(blk)` es cierto, o salta a `taken`.
		--   Si `fall` es un bloque condicional puro `nxt` con un unico predecesor:
		--   - `taken(blk) == taken(nxt)`:
		--       va a `fall(nxt)` ssi `onFall(blk) and onFall(nxt)`, si no `taken(blk)`.
		--   - `taken(blk) == fall(nxt)`:
		--       va a `taken(blk)` ssi `onTaken(blk) or onFall(nxt)`, si no `taken(nxt)`.
		local function resolveShortCircuit(blk, stopSet, loopByHeader)
			local takenEdge = edgeByKind(blk, "taken")
			local fallEdge = edgeByKind(blk, "fallthrough")
			if not (takenEdge and fallEdge) then
				return nil
			end

			local consumed = { [blk.id] = true }
			local trueTarget = fallEdge.to
			local falseTarget = takenEdge.to
			local cond = makeAtom(blk, false) -- verdadero cuando toma fallthrough (entra al then)

			local guard = 0
			while guard < 32 do
				guard += 1
				local nxt = trueTarget
				if not nxt or consumed[nxt.id] or (stopSet and stopSet[nxt.id]) then
					break
				end
				if loopByHeader[nxt.id] or #nxt.pred ~= 1 or not isPureCondBlock(nxt) then
					break
				end

				local nTaken = edgeByKind(nxt, "taken")
				local nFall = edgeByKind(nxt, "fallthrough")
				if not (nTaken and nFall) then
					break
				end

				if falseTarget == nTaken.to then
					-- A and B -> nFall; si falla -> falseTarget
					consumed[nxt.id] = true
					cond = { kind = "and", left = cond, right = makeAtom(nxt, false) }
					trueTarget = nFall.to
				elseif falseTarget == nFall.to and not (stopSet and stopSet[falseTarget.id]) then
					-- (not A) or B -> falseTarget; si falla -> nTaken
					consumed[nxt.id] = true
					cond = { kind = "or", left = negateCond(cond), right = makeAtom(nxt, false) }
					trueTarget = falseTarget
					falseTarget = nTaken.to
					break
				else
					break
				end
			end

			-- Si trueTarget sale del bucle o es un retorno inmediato y falseTarget sigue en el flujo,
			-- mantenemos la polaridad natural; el emisor de If decidira si invertir (if not c).
			return {
				cond = cond,
				trueTarget = trueTarget,
				falseTarget = falseTarget,
				consumed = consumed,
			}
		end

		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- 3. Paso 2: Estructuracion de regiones (loops, if/elseif/else, seq)
		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		local function copySet(src)
			local out = {}
			if src then
				for k, v in pairs(src) do
					out[k] = v
				end
			end
			return out
		end

		local function makeBlockRef(blk, trimLast)
			local count = #blk.insns - (trimLast and 1 or 0)
			return {
				kind = "BlockRef",
				block = blk,
				fromIdx = 1,
				toIdx = math.max(0, count),
			}
		end

		local function appendStmt(seq, node)
			if not node then
				return
			end
			if node.kind == "Seq" then
				for _, child in ipairs(node.body) do
					appendStmt(seq, child)
				end
				return
			end
			if node.kind == "BlockRef" and node.toIdx < node.fromIdx then
				return
			end
			seq[#seq + 1] = node
		end

		function Structurer.structure(cfg)
			local ctx = {
				cfg = cfg,
				loopByHeader = {},
				numericPrepLoops = {}, -- [prepBlock.id] -> loop
				genericPrepLoops = {}, -- [prepBlock.id] -> loop
				repeatUntilBlock = {}, -- [loop] -> condBlock
				repeatLatchBlock = {}, -- [loop] -> latchBlock
				structuredLoops = {},  -- [loop] -> true mientras ya se estructuro su entrada
			}

			-- Indexar bucles por cabecera y detectar bloques prep / until
			for _, loop in ipairs(cfg.loops) do
				ctx.loopByHeader[loop.header.id] = loop

				if loop.kind == "numeric_for" then
					for _, p in ipairs(loop.header.pred) do
						local last = lastInsOf(p)
						if last and last.name == "FORNPREP" then
							ctx.numericPrepLoops[p.id] = loop
						end
					end
				elseif loop.kind == "generic_for" then
					for _, p in ipairs(loop.header.pred) do
						local last = lastInsOf(p)
						if last and (last.name == "FORGPREP" or last.name == "FORGPREP_INEXT" or last.name == "FORGPREP_NEXT") then
							ctx.genericPrepLoops[p.id] = loop
						end
					end
				elseif loop.kind == "repeat_until" then
					local latch = loop.latches[1]
					if latch then
						ctx.repeatLatchBlock[loop] = latch
						if latch.edgesIn[1] and latch.edgesIn[1].kind == "fallthrough" then
							ctx.repeatUntilBlock[loop] = latch.edgesIn[1].from
						else
							ctx.repeatUntilBlock[loop] = latch
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local function isInLoop(blk, loopCtx)
				if not loopCtx or not blk then
					return true
				end
				return loopCtx.body[blk.id] == true
			end

			local function isLoopExitTarget(target, loopCtx)
				if not loopCtx or not target then
					return false
				end
				return not loopCtx.body[target.id]
			end

			local function structureLoop(loop, stopSet, outerLoopCtx)
				local stmts = {}
				local innerStop = copySet(stopSet)

				-- Salidas del bucle actuan como limites de region
				local exitTargets = {}
				for _, ex in ipairs(loop.exits) do
					innerStop[ex.to.id] = true
					exitTargets[ex.to.id] = true
				end

				local afterLoop = nil
				if #loop.exits > 0 then
					-- Elegimos como continuacion tras el bucle el primer target de salida por RPO (o for_exit)
					for _, ex in ipairs(loop.exits) do
						if ex.kind == "for_exit" then
							afterLoop = ex.to
							break
						end
					end
					if not afterLoop then
						afterLoop = loop.exits[1].to
					end
				end

				-- Marcar este bucle como activo mientras estructuramos su interior
				local prevLoopActive = ctx.structuredLoops[loop]
				ctx.structuredLoops[loop] = true

				local kind = loop.kind
				if kind == "numeric_for" then
					-- En numeric_for, el latch termina en FORNLOOP -> loop.header
					local latchStop = copySet(innerStop)
					latchStop[loop.header.id] = true
					local body = ctx.structureRegion(loop.header, latchStop, loop, {}, true)
					ctx.structuredLoops[loop] = prevLoopActive
					return {
						kind = "NumericFor",
						loop = loop,
						header = loop.header,
						latch = loop.latches[1],
						body = body,
					}, afterLoop
				elseif kind == "generic_for" then
					-- En generic_for, loop.header ES FORGLOOP; el cuerpo arranca en for_loop edge
					local bodyStart = edgeByKind(loop.header, "for_loop")
					local latchStop = copySet(innerStop)
					latchStop[loop.header.id] = true
					local body = bodyStart and ctx.structureRegion(bodyStart.to, latchStop, loop, {}) or { kind = "Seq", body = {} }
					ctx.structuredLoops[loop] = prevLoopActive
					return {
						kind = "GenericFor",
						loop = loop,
						header = loop.header,
						body = body,
					}, afterLoop
				elseif kind == "repeat_until" then
					local untilBlk = ctx.repeatUntilBlock[loop]
					local latchBlk = ctx.repeatLatchBlock[loop]
					local bodyStop = copySet(innerStop)
					if untilBlk and untilBlk ~= loop.header then
						bodyStop[untilBlk.id] = true
					end
					if latchBlk then
						bodyStop[latchBlk.id] = true
					end

					local body = { kind = "Seq", body = {} }
					local condExpr = nil

					if untilBlk == loop.header then
						-- Caso de 1 solo bloque (ej. repeat_simple: B2 tiene cuerpo + JUMPIFLE -> salida)
						appendStmt(body.body, makeBlockRef(loop.header, true))
						condExpr = makeAtom(loop.header, true)
					else
						local innerBody = ctx.structureRegion(loop.header, bodyStop, loop, {})
						appendStmt(body.body, innerBody)
						-- Si la ultima sentencia antes de `untilBlk` es un Continue por fallthrough natural, quitarlo
						local lastNode = body.body[#body.body]
						if lastNode and lastNode.kind == "Continue" and lastNode.target == untilBlk then
							body.body[#body.body] = nil
						end
						if untilBlk then
							-- Las instrucciones antes del salto final de untilBlk forman parte del cuerpo
							appendStmt(body.body, makeBlockRef(untilBlk, true))
							condExpr = makeAtom(untilBlk, true)
						end
					end

					ctx.structuredLoops[loop] = prevLoopActive
					return {
						kind = "RepeatUntil",
						loop = loop,
						header = loop.header,
						untilBlock = untilBlk,
						cond = condExpr,
						body = body,
					}, afterLoop
				else
					-- while_or_loop: distinguir `while cond do` vs `while true do`
					local h = loop.header
					local sc = h.cond and resolveShortCircuit(h, nil, ctx.loopByHeader) or nil
					local latchStop = copySet(innerStop)
					latchStop[h.id] = true

					if sc and (not loop.body[sc.falseTarget.id] or not loop.body[sc.trueTarget.id]) then
						-- Uno de los dos caminos de la cabecera sale del bucle: es `while cond do ... end`
						local enterBlk, condExpr, exitBlk
						if loop.body[sc.trueTarget.id] then
							enterBlk = sc.trueTarget
							exitBlk = sc.falseTarget
							condExpr = sc.cond
						else
							enterBlk = sc.falseTarget
							exitBlk = sc.trueTarget
							condExpr = negateCond(sc.cond)
						end

						-- Prefijo dentro de `h` (evaluacion de la condicion) si existe
						local condSetup = makeBlockRef(h, true)
						local visitedInCond = copySet(sc.consumed)
						local body = ctx.structureRegion(enterBlk, latchStop, loop, visitedInCond)
						ctx.structuredLoops[loop] = prevLoopActive

						return {
							kind = "While",
							loop = loop,
							header = h,
							condSetup = (condSetup.toIdx >= condSetup.fromIdx) and condSetup or nil,
							cond = condExpr,
							body = body,
						}, afterLoop or exitBlk
					else
						-- Bucle sin test de salida en cabecera (`while true do ... end`)
						local body = ctx.structureRegion(h, latchStop, loop, {}, true)
						ctx.structuredLoops[loop] = prevLoopActive
						return {
							kind = "While",
							loop = loop,
							header = h,
							cond = { kind = "true" },
							body = body,
						}, afterLoop
					end
				end
			end

			-- Emite un edge target dentro de una region respetando break/continue/stopSet
			local function emitTarget(target, stopSet, loopCtx, visited, skipLoopCheck)
				if not target then
					return { kind = "Seq", body = {} }
				end
				if loopCtx and isLoopExitTarget(target, loopCtx) then
					return { kind = "Break", target = target }
				end
				if loopCtx and loopCtx.kind == "repeat_until" and target == ctx.repeatUntilBlock[loopCtx] and target ~= loopCtx.header then
					return { kind = "Continue", target = target }
				end
				if loopCtx and target == loopCtx.header then
					return { kind = "Continue", target = target }
				end
				if stopSet and stopSet[target.id] then
					return { kind = "Seq", body = {} }
				end
				return ctx.structureRegion(target, stopSet, loopCtx, visited, skipLoopCheck)
			end

			function ctx.structureRegion(startBlk, stopSet, loopCtx, visited, skipHeaderLoopEntry)
				local out = {}
				local cur = startBlk
				visited = visited or {}

				local guard = 0
				while cur and guard < 10000 do
					guard += 1

					local firstEntry = (guard == 1 and skipHeaderLoopEntry)
					if not firstEntry and stopSet and stopSet[cur.id] then
						break
					end
					if loopCtx and isLoopExitTarget(cur, loopCtx) then
						appendStmt(out, { kind = "Break", target = cur })
						break
					end

					-- Si entramos a un bucle (que no esta ya activo en loopCtx)
					local enteringLoop = ctx.loopByHeader[cur.id]
					if enteringLoop and not skipHeaderLoopEntry and not ctx.structuredLoops[enteringLoop] then
						local loopNode, nextAfter = structureLoop(enteringLoop, stopSet, loopCtx)
						appendStmt(out, loopNode)
						cur = nextAfter
						continue
					end
					skipHeaderLoopEntry = false

					-- Si alcanzamos el latch de `repeat_until` (JUMPBACK -> header), terminamos el cuerpo
					if loopCtx and loopCtx.kind == "repeat_until" and cur == ctx.repeatLatchBlock[loopCtx] then
						break
					end

					-- Evitar bucles infinitos en grafos irreducibles o visitados
					if visited[cur.id] then
						appendStmt(out, { kind = "GotoFallback", target = cur })
						break
					end
					visited[cur.id] = true

					-- Bloque preparatorio de `for` numerico (FORNPREP)
					local numLoop = ctx.numericPrepLoops[cur.id]
					if numLoop and not ctx.structuredLoops[numLoop] then
						local prepRef = makeBlockRef(cur, true)
						appendStmt(out, prepRef)
						local loopNode, nextAfter = structureLoop(numLoop, stopSet, loopCtx)
						loopNode.prep = cur
						appendStmt(out, loopNode)
						cur = nextAfter
						continue
					end

					-- Bloque preparatorio de `for` generico (FORGPREP*)
					local genLoop = ctx.genericPrepLoops[cur.id]
					if genLoop and not ctx.structuredLoops[genLoop] then
						local prepRef = makeBlockRef(cur, true)
						appendStmt(out, prepRef)
						local loopNode, nextAfter = structureLoop(genLoop, stopSet, loopCtx)
						loopNode.prep = cur
						appendStmt(out, loopNode)
						cur = nextAfter
						continue
					end

					-- Latch de numeric_for (FORNLOOP): emitir instrucciones previas y terminar iteracion
					local last = lastInsOf(cur)
					if last and last.name == "FORNLOOP" and loopCtx and loopCtx.kind == "numeric_for" then
						appendStmt(out, makeBlockRef(cur, true))
						break
					end

					-- Salida normal (RETURN / sin sucesores)
					if #cur.edgesOut == 0 then
						appendStmt(out, makeBlockRef(cur, false))
						break
					end

					-- Bloque lineal con 1 sucesor
					if #cur.edgesOut == 1 then
						local e = cur.edgesOut[1]
						local trimLast = (last and (last.name == "JUMP" or last.name == "JUMPBACK" or last.name == "JUMPX"))
						appendStmt(out, makeBlockRef(cur, trimLast))

						if e.isBack then
							if loopCtx and e.to == loopCtx.header then
								-- Fin natural de iteracion o `continue` explicito si no es ultimo bloque
								if cur.edgesOut[1].kind == "jump_back" and loopCtx.kind ~= "while_or_loop" then
									appendStmt(out, { kind = "Continue", target = e.to })
								end
							else
								appendStmt(out, { kind = "GotoFallback", target = e.to })
							end
							break
						end

						if loopCtx and loopCtx.kind == "repeat_until" and e.to == ctx.repeatUntilBlock[loopCtx] and e.kind == "jump" then
							appendStmt(out, { kind = "Continue", target = e.to })
							break
						end

						if stopSet and stopSet[e.to.id] then
							break
						end
						if loopCtx and isLoopExitTarget(e.to, loopCtx) then
							appendStmt(out, { kind = "Break", target = e.to })
							break
						end
						cur = e.to
						continue
					end

					-- Bloque condicional (If / ElseIf / Else)
					if cur.cond then
						local sc = resolveShortCircuit(cur, stopSet, ctx.loopByHeader)
						if sc then
							-- Instrucciones previas a la condicion en `cur`
							appendStmt(out, makeBlockRef(cur, true))
							for cid in pairs(sc.consumed) do
								visited[cid] = true
							end

							-- Determinar punto de convergencia (join) usando post-dominador
							local joinBlk = cur.ipdom
							if joinBlk and stopSet and stopSet[joinBlk.id] then
								-- joinBlk esta dentro o justo en el borde
							end
							if joinBlk and loopCtx and isLoopExitTarget(joinBlk, loopCtx) then
								-- Si ipdom esta fuera del bucle, las ramas pueden contener `break`
								joinBlk = nil
							end

							local branchStop = copySet(stopSet)
							if joinBlk then
								branchStop[joinBlk.id] = true
							end

							local branches = {}
							local elseBody = nil

							local thenNode = emitTarget(sc.trueTarget, branchStop, loopCtx, copySet(visited))
							branches[1] = { cond = sc.cond, body = thenNode }

							-- Detectar cadena elseif / else (permitir Break/Continue aunque fTarget este en stopSet)
							local fTarget = sc.falseTarget
							local fIsLoopCtrl = loopCtx and (isLoopExitTarget(fTarget, loopCtx) or fTarget == loopCtx.header or (loopCtx.kind == "repeat_until" and fTarget == ctx.repeatUntilBlock[loopCtx]))
							if fTarget and fTarget ~= joinBlk and (fIsLoopCtrl or not (stopSet and stopSet[fTarget.id])) then
								local fNode = emitTarget(fTarget, branchStop, loopCtx, copySet(visited))
								if fNode.kind == "Seq" and #fNode.body == 1 then
									fNode = fNode.body[1]
								end
								if fNode.kind == "If" then
									for _, br in ipairs(fNode.branches) do
										branches[#branches + 1] = br
									end
									elseBody = fNode.elseBody
								elseif fNode.kind ~= "Seq" or #fNode.body > 0 then
									elseBody = fNode
								end
							end

							-- Si `then` quedo vacio y `else` tiene cuerpo, invertimos `if not cond then elseBody end`
							if #branches == 1 and elseBody ~= nil and branches[1].body.kind == "Seq" and #branches[1].body.body == 0 then
								branches[1] = { cond = negateCond(branches[1].cond), body = elseBody }
								elseBody = nil
							end

							-- Si `else` es Break/Continue y no hay joinBlk, emitir guardia limpia:
							-- `if not cond then break/continue end` seguido del cuerpo `then`
							if #branches == 1 and joinBlk == nil and elseBody ~= nil and (elseBody.kind == "Break" or elseBody.kind == "Continue") then
								local guardThen = branches[1].body
								appendStmt(out, {
									kind = "If",
									header = cur,
									branches = { { cond = negateCond(branches[1].cond), body = elseBody } },
									elseBody = nil,
									join = nil,
								})
								appendStmt(out, guardThen)
								break
							end

							appendStmt(out, {
								kind = "If",
								header = cur,
								branches = branches,
								elseBody = elseBody,
								join = joinBlk,
							})

							if joinBlk and not (stopSet and stopSet[joinBlk.id]) and isInLoop(joinBlk, loopCtx) then
								cur = joinBlk
								continue
							end
							break
						end
					end

					-- Fallback seguro si una bifurcacion no es condicional estandar
					appendStmt(out, makeBlockRef(cur, false))
					for _, e in ipairs(cur.edgesOut) do
						appendStmt(out, { kind = "GotoFallback", target = e.to })
					end
					break
				end

				return { kind = "Seq", body = out }
			end

			if not cfg.entry then
				return { kind = "Seq", body = {} }
			end

			return ctx.structureRegion(cfg.entry, {}, nil, {})
		end

		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- 4. Paso 3: Pretty Printer legible del AST estructurado
		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		local function fmtCond(c)
			if not c then
				return "<?>"
			end
			if c.kind == "true" then
				return "true"
			elseif c.kind == "not" then
				return "not (" .. fmtCond(c.inner) .. ")"
			elseif c.kind == "and" then
				return "(" .. fmtCond(c.left) .. " and " .. fmtCond(c.right) .. ")"
			elseif c.kind == "or" then
				return "(" .. fmtCond(c.left) .. " or " .. fmtCond(c.right) .. ")"
			elseif c.kind == "atom" then
				local prefix = c.negated and "not " or ""
				local tag = c.cond.op
				if c.cond.regA ~= nil then
					tag ..= "(R" .. c.cond.regA .. (c.cond.regB and (",R" .. c.cond.regB) or "") .. ")"
				end
				return string.format("%s%s@B%d", prefix, tag, c.block.id)
			end
			return "<?>"
		end

		function Structurer.format(tree)
			local out = {}

			local function emit(node, indent)
				if not node then
					return
				end
				local pad = string.rep("  ", indent)
				local k = node.kind

				if k == "Seq" then
					for _, child in ipairs(node.body) do
						emit(child, indent)
					end
				elseif k == "BlockRef" then
					local b = node.block
					local names = {}
					for i = node.fromIdx, node.toIdx do
						local ins = b.insns[i]
						if ins then
							names[#names + 1] = ins.name
						end
					end
					if #names > 0 then
						out[#out + 1] = string.format("%sB%d [%s]", pad, b.id, table.concat(names, ", "))
					end
				elseif k == "Break" then
					out[#out + 1] = string.format("%sbreak -- -> B%d", pad, node.target.id)
				elseif k == "Continue" then
					out[#out + 1] = string.format("%scontinue -- -> B%d", pad, node.target.id)
				elseif k == "GotoFallback" then
					out[#out + 1] = string.format("%s-- [unstructured jump -> B%d]", pad, node.target.id)
				elseif k == "If" then
					for i, br in ipairs(node.branches) do
						local kw = (i == 1) and "if" or "elseif"
						out[#out + 1] = string.format("%s%s %s then", pad, kw, fmtCond(br.cond))
						emit(br.body, indent + 1)
					end
					if node.elseBody then
						out[#out + 1] = pad .. "else"
						emit(node.elseBody, indent + 1)
					end
					out[#out + 1] = pad .. "end"
				elseif k == "While" then
					if node.condSetup then
						emit(node.condSetup, indent)
					end
					out[#out + 1] = string.format("%swhile %s do", pad, fmtCond(node.cond))
					emit(node.body, indent + 1)
					out[#out + 1] = pad .. "end"
				elseif k == "RepeatUntil" then
					out[#out + 1] = pad .. "repeat"
					emit(node.body, indent + 1)
					out[#out + 1] = string.format("%suntil %s", pad, fmtCond(node.cond))
				elseif k == "NumericFor" then
					out[#out + 1] = string.format("%sfor_num (header=B%d) do", pad, node.header.id)
					emit(node.body, indent + 1)
					out[#out + 1] = pad .. "end"
				elseif k == "GenericFor" then
					out[#out + 1] = string.format("%sfor_gen (header=B%d) do", pad, node.header.id)
					emit(node.body, indent + 1)
					out[#out + 1] = pad .. "end"
				end
			end

			emit(tree, 0)
			return out
		end

		function Structurer.formatString(tree)
			return table.concat(Structurer.format(tree), "\n")
		end

		return Structurer
	end)()

	local Lifter = (function()
		-- ============================================================================
		-- Zenith Decompiler (Fase 2 - Paso 3): Expression Lifter + Luau Source Emitter
		--
		-- Consume `(bc, proto, cfg, tree)` de Fase 1 + CFG + Structurer y reconstruye
		-- codigo fuente Luau legible y semanticamente fiel, incluyendo:
		--   - Nombres de parametros, locales (`locvars`) y upvalues (`upnames`/`captures`)
		--   - Plegado de expresiones con precedencia minima de parentesis
		--   - Constructores de tablas (`NEWTABLE` / `DUPTABLE` / `SETTABLE*` / `SETLIST`)
		--   - `NAMECALL` (`obj:Method(...)`), `CALL` / `CALLFB`, `multret`, `varargs`
		--   - Closures anidados recursivos (`NEWCLOSURE` / `DUPCLOSURE`)
		--   - Bucles `while`, `repeat ... until`, `for i = ...`, `for k, v in ...`
		--   - `if / elseif / else` con condiciones compuestas (`and` / `or` / `not`)
		-- ============================================================================

		local Lifter = {}

		local band, rshift = bit32.band, bit32.rshift

		local KEYWORDS = {
			["and"] = true, ["break"] = true, ["do"] = true, ["else"] = true, ["elseif"] = true,
			["end"] = true, ["false"] = true, ["for"] = true, ["function"] = true, ["if"] = true,
			["in"] = true, ["local"] = true, ["nil"] = true, ["not"] = true, ["or"] = true,
			["repeat"] = true, ["return"] = true, ["then"] = true, ["true"] = true, ["until"] = true,
			["while"] = true,
		}

		local PREC = {
			OR = 1,
			AND = 2,
			CMP = 3,
			CONCAT = 4,
			ADD = 5,
			MUL = 6,
			UNARY = 7,
			POW = 8,
			ATOM = 9,
		}

		local BINOP_INFO = {
			ADD = { op = "+", prec = PREC.ADD, compound = "+=" },
			ADDK = { op = "+", prec = PREC.ADD, compound = "+=" },
			SUB = { op = "-", prec = PREC.ADD, compound = "-=" },
			SUBK = { op = "-", prec = PREC.ADD, compound = "-=" },
			SUBRK = { op = "-", prec = PREC.ADD },
			MUL = { op = "*", prec = PREC.MUL, compound = "*=" },
			MULK = { op = "*", prec = PREC.MUL, compound = "*=" },
			DIV = { op = "/", prec = PREC.MUL, compound = "/=" },
			DIVK = { op = "/", prec = PREC.MUL, compound = "/=" },
			DIVRK = { op = "/", prec = PREC.MUL },
			IDIV = { op = "//", prec = PREC.MUL, compound = "//=" },
			IDIVK = { op = "//", prec = PREC.MUL, compound = "//=" },
			MOD = { op = "%", prec = PREC.MUL, compound = "%=" },
			MODK = { op = "%", prec = PREC.MUL, compound = "%=" },
			POW = { op = "^", prec = PREC.POW, right = true, compound = "^=" },
			POWK = { op = "^", prec = PREC.POW, right = true, compound = "^=" },
			AND = { op = "and", prec = PREC.AND },
			ANDK = { op = "and", prec = PREC.AND },
			OR = { op = "or", prec = PREC.OR },
			ORK = { op = "or", prec = PREC.OR },
			CONCAT = { op = "..", prec = PREC.CONCAT, right = true },
		}

		local ESC = { ["\n"] = "\\n", ["\r"] = "\\r", ["\t"] = "\\t", ['"'] = '\\"', ["\\"] = "\\\\" }

		local function isValidIdent(s)
			return type(s) == "string" and not KEYWORDS[s] and string.match(s, "^[%a_][%w_]*$") ~= nil
		end

		local function quoteStr(s)
			return '"' .. (string.gsub(s or "", '[%c"\\]', function(c)
				return ESC[c] or string.format("\\%03d", string.byte(c))
			end)) .. '"'
		end

		local function fmtNum(v)
			if v ~= v then
				return "0/0"
			end
			if v == math.huge then
				return "math.huge"
			end
			if v == -math.huge then
				return "-math.huge"
			end
			if v == math.floor(v) and math.abs(v) < 1e15 then
				return string.format("%d", v)
			end
			local s = string.format("%.14g", v)
			if tonumber(s) ~= v then
				s = string.format("%.17g", v)
			end
			return s
		end

		local function importPath(p, id)
			local count = rshift(id, 30)
			local parts = {}
			for i = 1, count do
				local k = p.k[band(rshift(id, 30 - 10 * i), 1023) + 1]
				parts[i] = (k and k.t == "string" and k.v) or "?"
			end
			return table.concat(parts, ".")
		end

		local function mkExpr(text, prec, extra)
			local e = { text = text, prec = prec or PREC.ATOM }
			if extra then
				for k, v in pairs(extra) do
					e[k] = v
				end
			end
			return e
		end

		local function wrapExpr(e, minPrec, isRight)
			if not e then
				return "nil"
			end
			local p = e.prec or PREC.ATOM
			if p < minPrec or (isRight and p == minPrec) then
				return "(" .. e.text .. ")"
			end
			return e.text
		end

		local function wrapPrefix(e)
			if not e then
				return "nil"
			end
			if (e.prec or PREC.ATOM) < PREC.ATOM or e.isTable or e.isLiteral or e.isFunctionExpr then
				return "(" .. e.text .. ")"
			end
			return e.text
		end

		local function constToExpr(p, k)
			if not k then
				return mkExpr("nil", PREC.ATOM, { isLiteral = true })
			end
			local t = k.t
			if t == "nil" then
				return mkExpr("nil", PREC.ATOM, { isLiteral = true })
			elseif t == "boolean" then
				return mkExpr(tostring(k.v), PREC.ATOM, { isLiteral = true })
			elseif t == "number" then
				local txt = fmtNum(k.v)
				local prec = (string.sub(txt, 1, 1) == "-") and PREC.UNARY or PREC.ATOM
				return mkExpr(txt, prec, { isLiteral = true, numVal = k.v })
			elseif t == "string" then
				return mkExpr(quoteStr(k.v or ""), PREC.ATOM, { isLiteral = true, strVal = k.v })
			elseif t == "import" then
				return mkExpr(importPath(p, k.id), PREC.ATOM, { pure = true })
			elseif t == "vector" then
				if (k.w or 0) == 0 then
					return mkExpr(string.format("Vector3.new(%s, %s, %s)", fmtNum(k.x), fmtNum(k.y), fmtNum(k.z)), PREC.ATOM)
				end
				return mkExpr(string.format("vector.create(%s, %s, %s, %s)", fmtNum(k.x), fmtNum(k.y), fmtNum(k.z), fmtNum(k.w)), PREC.ATOM)
			elseif t == "integer" then
				return mkExpr(string.format("0x%08x%08x", k.hi or 0, k.lo or 0), PREC.ATOM, { isLiteral = true })
			elseif t == "table" then
				-- DUPTABLE puede traer keys+vals constantes (v100 / type 8) o solo template (type 5)
				local fields = {}
				for i, idx in ipairs(k.keys or {}) do
					local kk = p.k[idx + 1]
					local kv = (k.vals and k.vals[i] ~= nil and k.vals[i] >= 0) and p.k[k.vals[i] + 1] or nil
					local valTxt = kv and constToExpr(p, kv).text or "nil"
					if kk and kk.t == "string" and isValidIdent(kk.v) then
						fields[#fields + 1] = kk.v .. " = " .. valTxt
					elseif kk then
						fields[#fields + 1] = "[" .. constToExpr(p, kk).text .. "] = " .. valTxt
					end
				end
				return mkExpr(#fields > 0 and ("{ " .. table.concat(fields, ", ") .. " }") or "{}", PREC.ATOM, { isTable = true })
			end
			return mkExpr("nil", PREC.ATOM)
		end

		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- Analisis de lectura/escritura de registros y liveness por bloque
		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		local function insReadsWrites(ins)
			local n = ins.name
			local reads, writes = {}, {}
			local function r(x)
				if x ~= nil then
					reads[#reads + 1] = x
				end
			end
			local function w(x)
				if x ~= nil then
					writes[#writes + 1] = x
				end
			end

			if n == "NOP" or n == "BREAK" or n == "COVERAGE" or n == "PREPVARARGS"
				or n == "NATIVECALL" or n == "JUMP" or n == "JUMPBACK" or n == "JUMPX"
				or n == "CAPTURE" or n == "CLOSEUPVALS" or n == "FASTCALL" or n == "FASTPCALL" then
				return reads, writes
			elseif n == "FASTCALL1" then
				r(ins.B)
			elseif n == "FASTCALL2" then
				r(ins.B)
				r(band(ins.aux or 0, 255))
			elseif n == "FASTCALL2K" then
				r(ins.B)
			elseif n == "FASTCALL3" then
				r(ins.B)
				r(band(ins.aux or 0, 255))
				r(band(rshift(ins.aux or 0, 8), 255))
			elseif n == "LOADNIL" or n == "LOADB" or n == "LOADN" or n == "LOADK" or n == "LOADKX"
				or n == "GETGLOBAL" or n == "GETUPVAL" or n == "GETIMPORT" or n == "NEWTABLE"
				or n == "DUPTABLE" or n == "NEWCLOSURE" or n == "DUPCLOSURE" then
				w(ins.A)
			elseif n == "GETVARARGS" then
				local count = (ins.B == 0) and 1 or (ins.B - 1)
				for i = 0, count - 1 do
					w(ins.A + i)
				end
			elseif n == "MOVE" or n == "NOT" or n == "MINUS" or n == "LENGTH"
				or n == "GETTABLEKS" or n == "GETTABLEN" or n == "GETUDATAKS"
				or n == "ADDK" or n == "SUBK" or n == "MULK" or n == "DIVK" or n == "IDIVK"
				or n == "MODK" or n == "POWK" or n == "ANDK" or n == "ORK" then
				r(ins.B)
				w(ins.A)
			elseif n == "SUBRK" or n == "DIVRK" then
				r(ins.C)
				w(ins.A)
			elseif n == "GETTABLE" or n == "ADD" or n == "SUB" or n == "MUL" or n == "DIV"
				or n == "IDIV" or n == "MOD" or n == "POW" or n == "AND" or n == "OR" then
				r(ins.B)
				r(ins.C)
				w(ins.A)
			elseif n == "CONCAT" then
				for reg = ins.B, ins.C do
					r(reg)
				end
				w(ins.A)
			elseif n == "NAMECALL" or n == "NAMECALLUDATA" then
				r(ins.B)
				w(ins.A)
				w(ins.A + 1)
			elseif n == "CALL" or n == "CALLFB" then
				r(ins.A)
				if ins.B > 1 then
					for reg = ins.A + 1, ins.A + ins.B - 1 do
						r(reg)
					end
				elseif ins.B == 0 then
					r(ins.A + 1)
				end
				if ins.C > 1 then
					for reg = ins.A, ins.A + ins.C - 2 do
						w(reg)
					end
				elseif ins.C == 0 then
					w(ins.A)
				end
			elseif n == "RETURN" then
				if ins.B > 1 then
					for reg = ins.A, ins.A + ins.B - 2 do
						r(reg)
					end
				elseif ins.B == 0 then
					r(ins.A)
				end
			elseif n == "SETGLOBAL" or n == "SETUPVAL" then
				r(ins.A)
			elseif n == "SETTABLE" then
				r(ins.A)
				r(ins.B)
				r(ins.C)
			elseif n == "SETTABLEKS" or n == "SETTABLEN" or n == "SETUDATAKS" then
				r(ins.A)
				r(ins.B)
			elseif n == "SETLIST" then
				r(ins.A)
				if ins.C > 1 then
					for reg = ins.B, ins.B + ins.C - 2 do
						r(reg)
					end
				elseif ins.C == 0 then
					r(ins.B)
				end
			elseif n == "JUMPIF" or n == "JUMPIFNOT"
				or n == "JUMPXEQKNIL" or n == "JUMPXEQKB" or n == "JUMPXEQKN" or n == "JUMPXEQKS" then
				r(ins.A)
			elseif n == "JUMPIFEQ" or n == "JUMPIFLE" or n == "JUMPIFLT"
				or n == "JUMPIFNOTEQ" or n == "JUMPIFNOTLE" or n == "JUMPIFNOTLT" then
				r(ins.A)
				r(band(ins.aux or 0, 255))
			elseif n == "FORNPREP" then
				r(ins.A)
				r(ins.A + 1)
				r(ins.A + 2)
				w(ins.A + 2)
			elseif n == "FORNLOOP" then
				r(ins.A)
				r(ins.A + 1)
				r(ins.A + 2)
				w(ins.A + 2)
			elseif n == "FORGPREP" or n == "FORGPREP_INEXT" or n == "FORGPREP_NEXT" then
				r(ins.A)
				r(ins.A + 1)
				r(ins.A + 2)
			elseif n == "FORGLOOP" then
				local nvars = band(ins.aux or 0, 255)
				for i = 0, math.max(1, nvars) - 1 do
					w(ins.A + 3 + i)
				end
			end
			return reads, writes
		end

		local function computeLiveness(cfg)
			local use, def, liveIn, liveOut = {}, {}, {}, {}
			for _, b in ipairs(cfg.blocks) do
				local u, d = {}, {}
				for _, ins in ipairs(b.insns) do
					local reads, writes = insReadsWrites(ins)
					for _, r in ipairs(reads) do
						if not d[r] then
							u[r] = true
						end
					end
					for _, w in ipairs(writes) do
						d[w] = true
					end
				end
				use[b.id] = u
				def[b.id] = d
				liveIn[b.id] = {}
				liveOut[b.id] = {}
			end

			local changed = true
			while changed do
				changed = false
				for i = #cfg.blocks, 1, -1 do
					local b = cfg.blocks[i]
					local outSet = {}
					for _, e in ipairs(b.edgesOut) do
						if not e.virtual and e.to then
							for r in pairs(liveIn[e.to.id]) do
								outSet[r] = true
							end
						end
					end
					local inSet = {}
					for r in pairs(use[b.id]) do
						inSet[r] = true
					end
					for r in pairs(outSet) do
						if not def[b.id][r] then
							inSet[r] = true
						end
					end
					for r in pairs(outSet) do
						if not liveOut[b.id][r] then
							changed = true
							break
						end
					end
					if not changed then
						for r in pairs(inSet) do
							if not liveIn[b.id][r] then
								changed = true
								break
							end
						end
					end
					liveOut[b.id] = outSet
					liveIn[b.id] = inSet
				end
			end

			return liveIn, liveOut
		end

		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- Resolucion de nombres de variables (parametros, locales, upvalues, temps)
		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		local function findLocVar(p, reg, pc)
			local best
			for _, lv in ipairs(p.locvars or {}) do
				if lv.reg == reg and lv.startpc <= pc and pc <= lv.endpc then
					if not best or lv.startpc >= best.startpc then
						best = lv
					end
				end
			end
			return best
		end

		local function sanitizeIdent(name, fallback)
			if isValidIdent(name) then
				return name
			end
			if type(name) == "string" and name ~= "" then
				local cleaned = string.gsub(name, "[^%w_]", "_")
				if string.match(cleaned, "^%d") then
					cleaned = "_" .. cleaned
				end
				if isValidIdent(cleaned) then
					return cleaned
				end
			end
			return fallback
		end

		local function isLoopHeaderLocvar(loopVars, reg)
			return loopVars[reg] == true
		end

		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		-- Lifter principal por proto
		-- ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
		function Lifter.liftProto(bc, p, cfg, tree, opts, scopeCtx)
			opts = opts or {}
			local CFG = opts.CFG
			local Structurer = opts.Structurer
			local indentUnit = opts.indent or "\t"
			local insn = cfg.insns or (p.insn) or {}
			local liveIn, liveOut = computeLiveness(cfg)

			-- Detectar registros de bucles for (no declarar con `local` previo ni forzar persistencia en control regs)
			local forLoopRegs = {}
			local forCtrlRegs = {}
			local prepToForVars = {} -- [baseReg] -> nvars
			for _, ins in ipairs(insn) do
				if ins.name == "FORNPREP" then
					forCtrlRegs[ins.A] = true
					forCtrlRegs[ins.A + 1] = true
					forLoopRegs[ins.A + 2] = true
				elseif ins.name == "FORGLOOP" then
					forCtrlRegs[ins.A] = true
					forCtrlRegs[ins.A + 1] = true
					forCtrlRegs[ins.A + 2] = true
					local nvars = math.max(1, band(ins.aux or 0, 255))
					for i = 0, nvars - 1 do
						forLoopRegs[ins.A + 3 + i] = true
					end
					prepToForVars[ins.A] = math.max(prepToForVars[ins.A] or 0, nvars)
				elseif ins.name == "FORGPREP" or ins.name == "FORGPREP_INEXT" or ins.name == "FORGPREP_NEXT" then
					forCtrlRegs[ins.A] = true
					forCtrlRegs[ins.A + 1] = true
					forCtrlRegs[ins.A + 2] = true
				end
			end

			-- Registros capturados por closures hijos
			local capturedRegs = {}
			for _, cpIdx in ipairs(p.children or {}) do
				local cp = bc.protos[cpIdx + 1]
				if cp and cp.captures then
					for _, cap in ipairs(cp.captures) do
						if cap.kind == 0 or cap.kind == 1 then
							capturedRegs[cap.index] = true
						end
					end
				end
			end

			-- Contar escrituras y bloques de lectura por registro para decidir registros materializados
			local writeCount, multiBlockReg = {}, {}
			for _, b in ipairs(cfg.blocks) do
				for r in pairs(liveIn[b.id]) do
					if not forCtrlRegs[r] then
						multiBlockReg[r] = true
					end
				end
				for r in pairs(liveOut[b.id]) do
					if not forCtrlRegs[r] then
						multiBlockReg[r] = true
					end
				end
				for _, ins in ipairs(b.insns) do
					local _, writes = insReadsWrites(ins)
					for _, r in ipairs(writes) do
						writeCount[r] = (writeCount[r] or 0) + 1
					end
				end
			end

			-- Nombres unicos en la funcion
			local usedNames = {}
			local function allocName(preferred, fallback)
				local base = sanitizeIdent(preferred, fallback)
				if not usedNames[base] then
					usedNames[base] = true
					return base
				end
				local idx = 2
				while usedNames[base .. "_" .. idx] do
					idx += 1
				end
				local chosen = base .. "_" .. idx
				usedNames[chosen] = true
				return chosen
			end

			-- Parametros
			local paramNames = {}
			local paramRegName = {}
			for r = 0, p.numparams - 1 do
				local lv = findLocVar(p, r, 0)
				local nm = allocName(lv and lv.name, "p" .. (r + 1))
				paramNames[#paramNames + 1] = nm
				paramRegName[r] = nm
			end

			-- Upvalues de esta funcion
			local upvalNames = {}
			for i = 0, p.nups - 1 do
				local uname = p.upnames and p.upnames[i + 1]
				if not uname and p.captures and p.captures[i + 1] and scopeCtx then
					local cap = p.captures[i + 1]
					if cap.kind == 2 then
						uname = scopeCtx.upvalNames and scopeCtx.upvalNames[cap.index]
					else
						uname = scopeCtx.regVarName and scopeCtx.regVarName[cap.index]
					end
				end
				upvalNames[i] = sanitizeIdent(uname, "upval" .. (i + 1))
			end

			-- Nombres de registros por (reg, locvar) o fallback `v<r>`
			local locvarName = {} -- [lv] -> uniqueName
			local singleLocvarByReg = {}
			local countLocvarByReg = {}
			for _, lv in ipairs(p.locvars or {}) do
				if lv.reg < p.numparams and lv.startpc == 0 then
					locvarName[lv] = paramRegName[lv.reg]
				else
					locvarName[lv] = allocName(lv.name, "v" .. lv.reg)
				end
				countLocvarByReg[lv.reg] = (countLocvarByReg[lv.reg] or 0) + 1
				singleLocvarByReg[lv.reg] = lv
			end

			local fallbackRegName = {}
			local function regVar(reg, pc)
				if reg < p.numparams and paramRegName[reg] and (not pc or not findLocVar(p, reg, pc)) then
					return paramRegName[reg]
				end
				local lv = pc and findLocVar(p, reg, pc)
				if lv and locvarName[lv] then
					return locvarName[lv]
				end
				if countLocvarByReg[reg] == 1 and not forLoopRegs[reg] and singleLocvarByReg[reg] then
					return locvarName[singleLocvarByReg[reg]]
				end
				if not fallbackRegName[reg] then
					fallbackRegName[reg] = allocName(nil, "v" .. reg)
				end
				return fallbackRegName[reg]
			end

			-- Determinar que registros son variables persistentes (vs temps inlineables)
			local persistentRegs = {}
			for r = 0, p.numparams - 1 do
				persistentRegs[r] = true
			end
			for _, lv in ipairs(p.locvars or {}) do
				if not forLoopRegs[lv.reg] then
					persistentRegs[lv.reg] = true
				end
			end
			for r in pairs(capturedRegs) do
				persistentRegs[r] = true
			end
			for r in pairs(multiBlockReg) do
				if not forLoopRegs[r] then
					persistentRegs[r] = true
				end
			end

			-- Escaneo previo del arbol para detectar registros que requieren hoisting (`local r` antes de if/loop)
			local hoistedBeforeNode = {} -- [node] -> { varName, ... }
			local declaredGlobalVars = {} -- [varName] -> true
			for r = 0, p.numparams - 1 do
				declaredGlobalVars[paramRegName[r]] = true
			end

			local function collectNodeReadWrite(node, readsOut, writesOut, firstWriteNode)
				if not node then
					return
				end
				local k = node.kind
				if k == "Seq" then
					for _, ch in ipairs(node.body) do
						collectNodeReadWrite(ch, readsOut, writesOut, firstWriteNode)
					end
				elseif k == "BlockRef" then
					local b = node.block
					for i = node.fromIdx, node.toIdx do
						local ins = b.insns[i]
						if ins then
							local rs, ws = insReadsWrites(ins)
							for _, r in ipairs(rs) do
								readsOut[r] = true
							end
							for _, w in ipairs(ws) do
								writesOut[w] = true
								if not firstWriteNode[w] then
									firstWriteNode[w] = ins
								end
							end
						end
					end
				elseif k == "If" then
					local function scanCond(c)
						if not c then return end
						if c.kind == "atom" and c.block then
							for _, ins in ipairs(c.block.insns) do
								local rs = insReadsWrites(ins)
								for _, r in ipairs(rs) do
									readsOut[r] = true
								end
							end
						elseif c.kind == "not" then
							scanCond(c.inner)
						elseif c.kind == "and" or c.kind == "or" then
							scanCond(c.left)
							scanCond(c.right)
						end
					end
					for _, br in ipairs(node.branches) do
						scanCond(br.cond)
						collectNodeReadWrite(br.body, readsOut, writesOut, firstWriteNode)
					end
					if node.elseBody then
						collectNodeReadWrite(node.elseBody, readsOut, writesOut, firstWriteNode)
					end
				elseif k == "While" then
					if node.condSetup then
						collectNodeReadWrite(node.condSetup, readsOut, writesOut, firstWriteNode)
					end
					collectNodeReadWrite(node.body, readsOut, writesOut, firstWriteNode)
				elseif k == "RepeatUntil" or k == "NumericFor" or k == "GenericFor" then
					collectNodeReadWrite(node.body, readsOut, writesOut, firstWriteNode)
				end
			end

			local function planHoisting(seqNode, activeDeclared)
				if not seqNode or seqNode.kind ~= "Seq" then
					return
				end
				local body = seqNode.body
				local suffixReads = {} -- [idx] -> set of regs read in body[idx+1 .. #body]
				local acc = {}
				for i = #body, 1, -1 do
					local copy = {}
					for r in pairs(acc) do
						copy[r] = true
					end
					suffixReads[i] = copy
					local rSet, wSet, fw = {}, {}, {}
					collectNodeReadWrite(body[i], rSet, wSet, fw)
					for r in pairs(rSet) do
						acc[r] = true
					end
				end

				local curDeclared = {}
				for k, v in pairs(activeDeclared) do
					curDeclared[k] = v
				end

				for i, ch in ipairs(body) do
					if ch.kind == "BlockRef" then
						for j = ch.fromIdx, ch.toIdx do
							local ins = ch.block.insns[j]
							if ins then
								local _, ws = insReadsWrites(ins)
								for _, r in ipairs(ws) do
									if persistentRegs[r] then
										curDeclared[regVar(r, ins.pc + ins.len)] = true
									end
								end
							end
						end
					elseif ch.kind == "If" or ch.kind == "While" or ch.kind == "RepeatUntil" or ch.kind == "NumericFor" or ch.kind == "GenericFor" then
						local rSet, wSet, fw = {}, {}, {}
						collectNodeReadWrite(ch, rSet, wSet, fw)
						local hoistList = {}
						local sortedRegs = {}
						for r in pairs(wSet) do
							if persistentRegs[r] and not forLoopRegs[r] and suffixReads[i][r] then
								sortedRegs[#sortedRegs + 1] = r
							end
						end
						table.sort(sortedRegs)
						for _, r in ipairs(sortedRegs) do
							local ins = fw[r]
							local vn = regVar(r, ins and (ins.pc + ins.len) or nil)
							if not curDeclared[vn] then
								curDeclared[vn] = true
								hoistList[#hoistList + 1] = vn
							end
						end
						if #hoistList > 0 then
							hoistedBeforeNode[ch] = hoistList
						end

						-- Recursivamente planear dentro de sub-bloques
						if ch.kind == "If" then
							for _, br in ipairs(ch.branches) do
								planHoisting(br.body, curDeclared)
							end
							if ch.elseBody then
								planHoisting(ch.elseBody, curDeclared)
							end
						elseif ch.body and ch.body.kind == "Seq" then
							planHoisting(ch.body, curDeclared)
						end
					end
				end
			end
			planHoisting(tree, declaredGlobalVars)

			-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
			-- Estado de expresiones en registros durante la emision
			-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
			local lines = {}
			local indentLevel = scopeCtx and scopeCtx.indent or 0
			local function emitLine(s)
				lines[#lines + 1] = string.rep(indentUnit, indentLevel) .. s
			end

			local regExpr = {}       -- [reg] -> expr
			local pendingTable = {}  -- [reg] -> { array = {}, hash = {}, seqIdx = 0 }
			local pendingNamecall    -- { reg = A, obj = expr, method = str }
			local pendingMultret     -- { reg = A, expr = expr }
			local activeForVars = {} -- [reg] -> varName (en cabecera de for)
			local declaredVars = {}
			for k, v in pairs(declaredGlobalVars) do
				declaredVars[k] = v
			end

			local function tableToExpr(tb)
				local items = {}
				local maxArr = 0
				for idx in pairs(tb.array) do
					if idx > maxArr then
						maxArr = idx
					end
				end
				local contiguous = 0
				for i = 1, maxArr do
					if tb.array[i] ~= nil then
						contiguous = i
						items[#items + 1] = tb.array[i]
					else
						break
					end
				end
				for _, kv in ipairs(tb.hash) do
					if isValidIdent(kv.rawKey) then
						items[#items + 1] = kv.rawKey .. " = " .. kv.val
					else
						items[#items + 1] = "[" .. kv.key .. "] = " .. kv.val
					end
				end
				for i = contiguous + 1, maxArr do
					if tb.array[i] ~= nil then
						items[#items + 1] = "[" .. i .. "] = " .. tb.array[i]
					end
				end
				return mkExpr(#items > 0 and ("{ " .. table.concat(items, ", ") .. " }") or "{}", PREC.ATOM, { isTable = true })
			end

			local function materializePendingTable(r, pc)
				local tb = pendingTable[r]
				if not tb then
					return nil
				end
				pendingTable[r] = nil
				local e = tableToExpr(tb)
				if persistentRegs[r] then
					local vn = regVar(r, pc)
					if not declaredVars[vn] then
						declaredVars[vn] = true
						emitLine(string.format("local %s = %s", vn, e.text))
						e = mkExpr(vn, PREC.ATOM, { varName = vn })
					end
				end
				regExpr[r] = e
				return e
			end

			local function snapshotState()
				local snap = { regExpr = {}, pendingTable = {} }
				for k, v in pairs(regExpr) do
					snap.regExpr[k] = v
				end
				for k, v in pairs(pendingTable) do
					snap.pendingTable[k] = v
				end
				return snap
			end

			local function restoreState(snap)
				regExpr = {}
				for k, v in pairs(snap.regExpr) do
					regExpr[k] = v
				end
				pendingTable = {}
				for k, v in pairs(snap.pendingTable) do
					pendingTable[k] = v
				end
			end

			local function readReg(r, pc, preferVar)
				if pendingTable[r] then
					materializePendingTable(r, pc)
				end
				if activeForVars[r] then
					return mkExpr(activeForVars[r], PREC.ATOM, { varName = activeForVars[r] })
				end
				if preferVar and persistentRegs[r] then
					local vn = regVar(r, pc)
					if declaredVars[vn] then
						return mkExpr(vn, PREC.ATOM, { varName = vn })
					end
				end
				if regExpr[r] then
					if not persistentRegs[r] then
						regExpr[r].consumed = true
					end
					return regExpr[r]
				end
				local vn = regVar(r, pc)
				return mkExpr(vn, PREC.ATOM, { varName = vn })
			end

			-- Cuando una variable o estado mutable cambia, asegurar que temps pendientes que dependan de ello
			-- no se reordenen ilegalmente.
			local tmpCounter = 0
			local function flushConflictingTemps(writtenReg, isSideEffect)
				local regs = {}
				for r, e in pairs(regExpr) do
					if r ~= writtenReg and not persistentRegs[r] and e and not e.isLiteral and not e.consumed then
						if isSideEffect and e.hasSideEffect then
							regs[#regs + 1] = r
						elseif writtenReg ~= nil and e.deps and e.deps[writtenReg] then
							regs[#regs + 1] = r
						end
					end
				end
				table.sort(regs)
				for _, r in ipairs(regs) do
					local e = regExpr[r]
					tmpCounter += 1
					local tn = allocName(nil, "tmp" .. tmpCounter)
					emitLine(string.format("local %s = %s", tn, e.text))
					declaredVars[tn] = true
					regExpr[r] = mkExpr(tn, PREC.ATOM, { varName = tn })
				end
			end

			local function assignReg(r, expr, pc, ins)
				if pendingTable[r] then
					pendingTable[r] = nil
				end
				flushConflictingTemps(r, expr.hasSideEffect)

				-- Detectar compound assignment: `i += 1` cuando el destino es una variable declarada
				local lv = pc and findLocVar(p, r, pc + (ins and ins.len or 1))
				local vn = regVar(r, pc and (pc + (ins and ins.len or 1)) or nil)
				local isNamedOrPersist = persistentRegs[r] or (lv ~= nil)

				if not isNamedOrPersist and not expr.forceEmit then
					-- Temporal puro en bloque: mantener en `regExpr` para plegar en su consumidor
					regExpr[r] = expr
					return
				end

				-- Variable persistente o con local debug:
				if declaredVars[vn] then
					if expr.compoundOp and expr.compoundLhs == vn then
						emitLine(string.format("%s %s %s", vn, expr.compoundOp, expr.compoundRhs))
					else
						emitLine(string.format("%s = %s", vn, expr.text))
					end
				else
					declaredVars[vn] = true
					if expr.isFunctionStmt then
						emitLine(string.format("local function %s%s", vn, expr.funcBody))
					else
						emitLine(string.format("local %s = %s", vn, expr.text))
					end
				end
				regExpr[r] = mkExpr(vn, PREC.ATOM, { varName = vn })
			end

			local function formatChildFunction(childProto, isStmt)
				local childCtx = {
					indent = indentLevel + 1,
					upvalNames = upvalNames,
					regVarName = {},
				}
				for r = 0, p.maxstack - 1 do
					local e = regExpr[r]
					if e and e.varName then
						childCtx.regVarName[r] = e.varName
					elseif persistentRegs[r] then
						childCtx.regVarName[r] = regVar(r)
					end
				end
				local cCfg = CFG.build(bc.protos[childProto.index + 1] and (function()
					local ins = childProto.insn
					return ins
				end)() or {}, { jumpOf = opts.jumpOf })
				local cTree = Structurer.structure(cCfg)
				local cLines = Lifter.liftProto(bc, childProto, cCfg, cTree, opts, childCtx)
				local sigParams = table.concat(cLines.params, ", ")
				if childProto.vararg then
					sigParams = (#sigParams > 0) and (sigParams .. ", ...") or "..."
				end
				local headerArgs = "(" .. sigParams .. ")"
				if #cLines.body == 0 then
					local inline = headerArgs .. " end"
					if isStmt then
						return mkExpr("function" .. inline, PREC.ATOM, { isFunctionStmt = true, funcBody = inline, isFunctionExpr = true })
					end
					return mkExpr("function" .. inline, PREC.ATOM, { isFunctionExpr = true })
				end
				local buf = { headerArgs }
				for _, ln in ipairs(cLines.body) do
					buf[#buf + 1] = ln
				end
				buf[#buf + 1] = string.rep(indentUnit, indentLevel) .. "end"
				local bodyStr = table.concat(buf, "\n")
				if isStmt then
					return mkExpr("function" .. bodyStr, PREC.ATOM, { isFunctionStmt = true, funcBody = bodyStr, isFunctionExpr = true })
				end
				return mkExpr("function" .. bodyStr, PREC.ATOM, { isFunctionExpr = true })
			end

			local function buildDeps(...)
				local d = {}
				for i = 1, select("#", ...) do
					local r = select(i, ...)
					if r ~= nil then
						d[r] = true
						local e = regExpr[r]
						if e and e.deps then
							for dr in pairs(e.deps) do
								d[dr] = true
							end
						end
					end
				end
				return d
			end

			local function liftIns(ins)
				local n = ins.name
				local pc = ins.pc
				local aux = ins.aux or 0

				if n == "NOP" or n == "BREAK" or n == "COVERAGE" or n == "PREPVARARGS"
					or n == "NATIVECALL" or n == "CLOSEUPVALS" or n == "CAPTURE"
					or n == "FASTCALL" or n == "FASTCALL1" or n == "FASTCALL2"
					or n == "FASTCALL2K" or n == "FASTCALL3" or n == "FASTPCALL"
					or n == "JUMP" or n == "JUMPBACK" or n == "JUMPX" then
					return
				end

				if n == "LOADNIL" then
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr("nil", PREC.ATOM, { isLiteral = true }), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "LOADB" then
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr(ins.B ~= 0 and "true" or "false", PREC.ATOM, { isLiteral = true }), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "LOADN" then
					local txt = tostring(ins.D)
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr(txt, ins.D < 0 and PREC.UNARY or PREC.ATOM, { isLiteral = true, numVal = ins.D }), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "LOADK" then
					assignReg(ins.A, constToExpr(p, p.k[ins.D + 1]), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "LOADKX" then
					assignReg(ins.A, constToExpr(p, p.k[aux + 1]), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "MOVE" then
					local src = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr(src.text, src.prec, { deps = buildDeps(ins.B), varName = src.varName }), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "GETGLOBAL" then
					local k = p.k[aux + 1]
					local name = (k and k.t == "string" and k.v) or "?"
					local expr = isValidIdent(name) and mkExpr(name, PREC.ATOM) or mkExpr("_ENV[" .. quoteStr(name) .. "]", PREC.ATOM)
					assignReg(ins.A, expr, pc, ins)
				elseif n == "SETGLOBAL" then
					local k = p.k[aux + 1]
