					local name = (k and k.t == "string" and k.v) or "?"
					local val = readReg(ins.A, pc, true)
					flushConflictingTemps(nil, true)
					if val.isFunctionStmt and isValidIdent(name) then
						emitLine("function " .. name .. val.funcBody)
					elseif isValidIdent(name) then
						emitLine(name .. " = " .. val.text)
					else
						emitLine("_ENV[" .. quoteStr(name) .. "] = " .. val.text)
					end
				elseif n == "GETUPVAL" then
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr(upvalNames[ins.B] or ("upval" .. (ins.B + 1)), PREC.ATOM), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "SETUPVAL" then
					local uname = upvalNames[ins.B] or ("upval" .. (ins.B + 1))
					local val = readReg(ins.A, pc, true)
					flushConflictingTemps(nil, true)
					if val.compoundOp and val.compoundLhs == uname then
						emitLine(string.format("%s %s %s", uname, val.compoundOp, val.compoundRhs))
					else
						emitLine(string.format("%s = %s", uname, val.text))
					end
				elseif n == "GETIMPORT" then
					assignReg(ins.A, constToExpr(p, p.k[ins.D + 1]), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "GETVARARGS" then
					if ins.B == 0 then
						pendingMultret = { reg = ins.A, expr = mkExpr("...", PREC.ATOM) }
						regExpr[ins.A] = mkExpr("...", PREC.ATOM)
					elseif ins.B == 2 then
						assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr("...", PREC.ATOM), pc, ins)
					else
						local vars = {}
						for i = 0, ins.B - 2 do
							local vn = regVar(ins.A + i, pc + ins.len)
							vars[#vars + 1] = vn
							declaredVars[vn] = true
							regExpr[ins.A + i] = mkExpr(vn, PREC.ATOM, { varName = vn })
						end
						emitLine("local " .. table.concat(vars, ", ") .. " = ...")
					end
				elseif n == "NEWTABLE" then
					pendingTable[ins.A] = { array = {}, hash = {} }
					regExpr[ins.A] = mkExpr("{}", PREC.ATOM, { isTable = true })
				elseif n == "DUPTABLE" then
					local k = p.k[ins.D + 1]
					local tb = { array = {}, hash = {} }
					if k and k.t == "table" and k.vals then
						for i, idx in ipairs(k.keys or {}) do
							local kk = p.k[idx + 1]
							local kv = (k.vals[i] ~= nil and k.vals[i] >= 0) and p.k[k.vals[i] + 1] or nil
							if kk and kv then
								tb.hash[#tb.hash + 1] = {
									rawKey = (kk.t == "string") and kk.v or nil,
									key = constToExpr(p, kk).text,
									val = constToExpr(p, kv).text,
								}
							end
						end
					end
					pendingTable[ins.A] = tb
					regExpr[ins.A] = tableToExpr(tb)
				elseif n == "SETLIST" then
					local tb = pendingTable[ins.A]
					local startIdx = aux
					if tb then
						if ins.C == 0 then
							if pendingMultret and pendingMultret.reg == ins.B then
								tb.array[startIdx] = pendingMultret.expr.text
								pendingMultret.expr.consumed = true
								regExpr[pendingMultret.reg] = nil
								pendingMultret = nil
							else
								tb.array[startIdx] = readReg(ins.B, pc, true).text
							end
						else
							for i = 0, ins.C - 2 do
								tb.array[startIdx + i] = readReg(ins.B + i, pc, true).text
							end
						end
						regExpr[ins.A] = tableToExpr(tb)
					else
						local tbl = readReg(ins.A, pc, true)
						local mrText
						if ins.C == 0 and pendingMultret and pendingMultret.reg == ins.B then
							mrText = pendingMultret.expr.text
							pendingMultret.expr.consumed = true
							regExpr[pendingMultret.reg] = nil
							pendingMultret = nil
						end
						flushConflictingTemps(nil, true)
						if mrText then
							emitLine(string.format("%s[%d] = %s", wrapPrefix(tbl), startIdx, mrText))
						else
							for i = 0, ins.C - 2 do
								local val = readReg(ins.B + i, pc, true)
								emitLine(string.format("%s[%d] = %s", wrapPrefix(tbl), startIdx + i, val.text))
							end
						end
					end
				elseif n == "GETTABLEKS" or n == "GETUDATAKS" then
					local tbl = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
					local k = p.k[aux + 1]
					local key = (k and k.t == "string" and k.v) or "?"
					local access = isValidIdent(key)
						and (wrapPrefix(tbl) .. "." .. key)
						or (wrapPrefix(tbl) .. "[" .. quoteStr(key) .. "]")
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr(access, PREC.ATOM, { deps = buildDeps(ins.B) }), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "SETTABLEKS" or n == "SETUDATAKS" then
					local k = p.k[aux + 1]
					local key = (k and k.t == "string" and k.v) or "?"
					local val = readReg(ins.A, pc, true)
					local tb = pendingTable[ins.B]
					if tb then
						tb.hash[#tb.hash + 1] = { rawKey = key, key = quoteStr(key), val = val.text }
						regExpr[ins.B] = tableToExpr(tb)
					else
						local tbl = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
						flushConflictingTemps(nil, true)
						if isValidIdent(key) then
							emitLine(string.format("%s.%s = %s", wrapPrefix(tbl), key, val.text))
						else
							emitLine(string.format("%s[%s] = %s", wrapPrefix(tbl), quoteStr(key), val.text))
						end
					end
				elseif n == "GETTABLEN" then
					local tbl = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr(string.format("%s[%d]", wrapPrefix(tbl), ins.C + 1), PREC.ATOM, { deps = buildDeps(ins.B) }), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "SETTABLEN" then
					local val = readReg(ins.A, pc, true)
					local idx = ins.C + 1
					local tb = pendingTable[ins.B]
					if tb then
						tb.array[idx] = val.text
						regExpr[ins.B] = tableToExpr(tb)
					else
						local tbl = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
						flushConflictingTemps(nil, true)
						emitLine(string.format("%s[%d] = %s", wrapPrefix(tbl), idx, val.text))
					end
				elseif n == "GETTABLE" then
					local tbl = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
					local key = readReg(ins.C, pc, true)
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr(string.format("%s[%s]", wrapPrefix(tbl), key.text), PREC.ATOM, { deps = buildDeps(ins.B, ins.C) }), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "SETTABLE" then
					local val = readReg(ins.A, pc, true)
					local key = readReg(ins.C, pc, true)
					local tb = pendingTable[ins.B]
					if tb then
						tb.hash[#tb.hash + 1] = { key = key.text, val = val.text }
						regExpr[ins.B] = tableToExpr(tb)
					else
						local tbl = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
						flushConflictingTemps(nil, true)
						emitLine(string.format("%s[%s] = %s", wrapPrefix(tbl), key.text, val.text))
					end
				elseif n == "NAMECALL" or n == "NAMECALLUDATA" then
					local obj = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
					local k = p.k[aux + 1]
					local method = (k and k.t == "string" and k.v) or "?"
					pendingNamecall = { reg = ins.A, obj = obj, method = method }
				elseif n == "CALL" or n == "CALLFB" then
					local args = {}
					local isMethod = pendingNamecall and pendingNamecall.reg == ins.A
					local startArg = isMethod and (ins.A + 2) or (ins.A + 1)

					if ins.B == 0 then
						-- Usa resultados multret previos a partir de `startArg` (o del ultimo reg antes de multret)
						local mr = pendingMultret
						if mr then
							for r = startArg, mr.reg - 1 do
								args[#args + 1] = readReg(r, pc, true).text
							end
							args[#args + 1] = mr.expr.text
							mr.expr.consumed = true
							regExpr[mr.reg] = nil
							pendingMultret = nil
						else
							args[#args + 1] = readReg(startArg, pc, true).text
						end
					else
						local lastArg = ins.A + ins.B - 1
						for r = startArg, lastArg do
							args[#args + 1] = readReg(r, pc, true).text
						end
					end

					local callText
					if isMethod then
						local nc = pendingNamecall
						pendingNamecall = nil
						if isValidIdent(nc.method) then
							callText = string.format("%s:%s(%s)", wrapPrefix(nc.obj), nc.method, table.concat(args, ", "))
						else
							callText = string.format("%s[%s](%s, %s)", wrapPrefix(nc.obj), quoteStr(nc.method), nc.obj.text, table.concat(args, ", "))
						end
					else
						local fn = readReg(ins.A, pc, true)
						callText = string.format("%s(%s)", wrapPrefix(fn), table.concat(args, ", "))
					end

					local callExpr = mkExpr(callText, PREC.ATOM, { hasSideEffect = true, isCall = true })
					flushConflictingTemps(nil, true)

					if ins.C == 1 then
						-- 0 resultados: sentencia directa
						emitLine(callText)
					elseif ins.C == 0 then
						-- multret: guardar en pendingMultret (y si es destino persistente, materializar)
						pendingMultret = { reg = ins.A, expr = callExpr }
						regExpr[ins.A] = callExpr
					elseif ins.C == 2 then
						-- 1 resultado
						assignReg(ins.A, callExpr, pc, ins)
					else
						-- C >= 3: N resultados (e.g. `local a, b = f()` o `for k, v in pairs(t)`)
						local nres = ins.C - 1
						if nres == 3 and (ins.A == ins.A) then
							-- Guardar primero por si el siguiente bloque/instruccion es FORGPREP
							regExpr[ins.A] = mkExpr(callText, PREC.ATOM, { isGenCall = true, numResults = 3, hasSideEffect = true })
						end
						-- Verificar si es inmediatamente consumido por FORGPREP en este bloque
						local nextIns
						for idx, candidate in ipairs(insn) do
							if candidate == ins then
								nextIns = insn[idx + 1]
								break
							end
						end
						if nextIns and (nextIns.name == "FORGPREP" or nextIns.name == "FORGPREP_INEXT" or nextIns.name == "FORGPREP_NEXT") and nextIns.A == ins.A then
							regExpr[ins.A] = mkExpr(callText, PREC.ATOM, { isGenCall = true })
						else
							local lhs, allNew = {}, true
							for i = 0, nres - 1 do
								local vn = regVar(ins.A + i, pc + ins.len)
								lhs[#lhs + 1] = vn
								if declaredVars[vn] then
									allNew = false
								end
							end
							for _, vn in ipairs(lhs) do
								declaredVars[vn] = true
							end
							for i = 0, nres - 1 do
								regExpr[ins.A + i] = mkExpr(lhs[i + 1], PREC.ATOM, { varName = lhs[i + 1] })
							end
							emitLine((allNew and "local " or "") .. table.concat(lhs, ", ") .. " = " .. callText)
						end
					end
				elseif n == "RETURN" then
					if ins.B == 1 then
						flushConflictingTemps(nil, true)
						emitLine("return")
					elseif ins.B == 0 then
						local vals = {}
						if pendingMultret then
							local mr = pendingMultret
							for r = ins.A, mr.reg - 1 do
								vals[#vals + 1] = readReg(r, pc, true).text
							end
							vals[#vals + 1] = mr.expr.text
							mr.expr.consumed = true
							regExpr[mr.reg] = nil
							pendingMultret = nil
						else
							vals[#vals + 1] = readReg(ins.A, pc, true).text
						end
						flushConflictingTemps(nil, true)
						emitLine("return " .. table.concat(vals, ", "))
					else
						local vals = {}
						for r = ins.A, ins.A + ins.B - 2 do
							vals[#vals + 1] = readReg(r, pc, true).text
						end
						flushConflictingTemps(nil, true)
						emitLine("return " .. table.concat(vals, ", "))
					end
				elseif n == "NEWCLOSURE" then
					local gi = p.children and p.children[ins.D + 1]
					local cp = gi and bc.protos[gi + 1]
					if cp then
						local lv = findLocVar(p, ins.A, pc + ins.len)
						local wantStmt = (lv ~= nil) or persistentRegs[ins.A]
						assignReg(ins.A, formatChildFunction(cp, wantStmt), pc, ins)
					end
				elseif n == "DUPCLOSURE" then
					local k = p.k[ins.D + 1]
					local cp = k and k.proto and bc.protos[k.proto + 1]
					if cp then
						local lv = findLocVar(p, ins.A, pc + ins.len)
						local wantStmt = (lv ~= nil) or persistentRegs[ins.A]
						assignReg(ins.A, formatChildFunction(cp, wantStmt), pc, ins)
					end
				elseif n == "NOT" then
					local rhs = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr("not " .. wrapExpr(rhs, PREC.UNARY), PREC.UNARY, { deps = buildDeps(ins.B) }), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "MINUS" then
					local rhs = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
					local opStr = (rhs.prec <= PREC.UNARY) and ("-(" .. rhs.text .. ")") or ("-" .. rhs.text)
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr(opStr, PREC.UNARY, { deps = buildDeps(ins.B) }), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "LENGTH" then
					local rhs = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr("#" .. wrapExpr(rhs, PREC.UNARY), PREC.UNARY, { deps = buildDeps(ins.B) }), pc, ins)
				elseif n == "CONCAT" then
					local parts = {}
					local deps = {}
					for r = ins.B, ins.C do
						local e = readReg(r, pc, true)
						parts[#parts + 1] = wrapExpr(e, PREC.CONCAT, r == ins.B)
						deps[r] = true
					end
					assignReg(ins.A, mkExpr(table.concat(parts, " .. "), PREC.CONCAT, { deps = deps }), pc, ins)
				elseif BINOP_INFO[n] then
					local info = BINOP_INFO[n]
					local lhs, rhs, deps
					if n == "SUBRK" or n == "DIVRK" then
						lhs = constToExpr(p, p.k[ins.B + 1])
						rhs = readReg(ins.C, pc, true)
						deps = buildDeps(ins.C)
					elseif string.sub(n, -1) == "K" then
						lhs = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
						rhs = constToExpr(p, p.k[ins.C + 1])
						deps = buildDeps(ins.B)
					else
						lhs = readReg(ins.B, pc, true)
						rhs = readReg(ins.C, pc, true)
						deps = buildDeps(ins.B, ins.C)
					end
					local lTxt = wrapExpr(lhs, info.prec, info.right)
					local rTxt = wrapExpr(rhs, info.prec, not info.right)
					local expr = mkExpr(string.format("%s %s %s", lTxt, info.op, rTxt), info.prec, { deps = deps })
					if info.compound and lhs.varName then
						expr.compoundOp = info.compound
						expr.compoundLhs = lhs.varName
						expr.compoundRhs = rTxt
					end
					assignReg(ins.A, expr, pc, ins)
				end
			end

			-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
			-- Evaluacion de condiciones estructuradas (`atom`, `and`, `or`, `not`)
			-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
			local function evalAtom(atom)
				local blk = atom.block
				local c = atom.cond
				local snap = snapshotState()

				-- Si este bloque tiene instrucciones previas al salto que no se emitieron (e.g. RHS de `and`/`or` o `elseif`),
				-- levantarlas en estado temporal para resolver sus operandos.
				if blk and #blk.insns > 1 then
					-- Solo re-evaluar si aun no estan en regExpr o si pertenecen a un bloque consumido en short-circuit
					for i = 1, #blk.insns - 1 do
						local ins = blk.insns[i]
						if ins.name ~= "NOP" and ins.name ~= "CAPTURE" then
							-- Para atomos puros, evaluar en un regExpr aislado sin emitir sentencias globales
							local saveEmit = emitLine
							emitLine = function() end
							liftIns(ins)
							emitLine = saveEmit
						end
					end
				end

				local lastIns = blk and blk.insns[#blk.insns]
				local pc = lastIns and lastIns.pc or 0
				local neg = atom.negated == true
				local res

				if c.op == "truthy" then
					local a = readReg(c.regA, pc, true)
					if neg then
						res = mkExpr("not " .. wrapExpr(a, PREC.UNARY), PREC.UNARY)
					else
						res = a
					end
				elseif c.op == "eq" then
					local a = readReg(c.regA, pc, true)
					local b = readReg(c.regB, pc, true)
					local op = neg and "~=" or "=="
					res = mkExpr(string.format("%s %s %s", wrapExpr(a, PREC.CMP), op, wrapExpr(b, PREC.CMP)), PREC.CMP)
				elseif c.op == "lt" then
					local a = readReg(c.regA, pc, true)
					local b = readReg(c.regB, pc, true)
					if neg then
						-- not (a < b) -> a >= b
						res = mkExpr(string.format("%s >= %s", wrapExpr(a, PREC.CMP), wrapExpr(b, PREC.CMP)), PREC.CMP)
					else
						res = mkExpr(string.format("%s < %s", wrapExpr(a, PREC.CMP), wrapExpr(b, PREC.CMP)), PREC.CMP)
					end
				elseif c.op == "le" then
					local a = readReg(c.regA, pc, true)
					local b = readReg(c.regB, pc, true)
					if neg then
						res = mkExpr(string.format("%s > %s", wrapExpr(a, PREC.CMP), wrapExpr(b, PREC.CMP)), PREC.CMP)
					else
						res = mkExpr(string.format("%s <= %s", wrapExpr(a, PREC.CMP), wrapExpr(b, PREC.CMP)), PREC.CMP)
					end
				elseif c.op == "eq_nil" then
					local a = readReg(c.regA, pc, true)
					local op = neg and "~=" or "=="
					res = mkExpr(string.format("%s %s nil", wrapExpr(a, PREC.CMP), op), PREC.CMP)
				elseif c.op == "eq_b" then
					local a = readReg(c.regA, pc, true)
					local op = neg and "~=" or "=="
					res = mkExpr(string.format("%s %s %s", wrapExpr(a, PREC.CMP), op, tostring(c.val)), PREC.CMP)
				elseif c.op == "eq_k" then
					local a = readReg(c.regA, pc, true)
					local kidx = c.kIdx or c.kidx
					if kidx == nil and lastIns then
						kidx = band(lastIns.aux or 0, 0xFFFFFF)
					end
					local k = p.k[(kidx or 0) + 1]
					local kExpr = constToExpr(p, k)
					local op = neg and "~=" or "=="
					res = mkExpr(string.format("%s %s %s", wrapExpr(a, PREC.CMP), op, wrapExpr(kExpr, PREC.CMP)), PREC.CMP)
				else
					res = mkExpr("true", PREC.ATOM)
				end

				restoreState(snap)
				return res
			end

			local function evalCond(cond)
				if not cond then
					return mkExpr("true", PREC.ATOM)
				end
				if cond.kind == "true" then
					return mkExpr("true", PREC.ATOM)
				elseif cond.kind == "atom" then
					return evalAtom(cond)
				elseif cond.kind == "not" then
					local inner = evalCond(cond.inner)
					return mkExpr("not " .. wrapExpr(inner, PREC.UNARY), PREC.UNARY)
				elseif cond.kind == "and" then
					local l = evalCond(cond.left)
					local r = evalCond(cond.right)
					return mkExpr(string.format("%s and %s", wrapExpr(l, PREC.AND), wrapExpr(r, PREC.AND)), PREC.AND)
				elseif cond.kind == "or" then
					local l = evalCond(cond.left)
					local r = evalCond(cond.right)
					return mkExpr(string.format("%s or %s", wrapExpr(l, PREC.OR), wrapExpr(r, PREC.OR)), PREC.OR)
				end
				return mkExpr("true", PREC.ATOM)
			end

			-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
			-- Emisor recursivo sobre el AST estructurado
			-- ------------------------------------------------------------------------
			local function emitNode(node, isTrailingMain)
				if not node then
					return
				end
				if hoistedBeforeNode[node] then
					emitLine("local " .. table.concat(hoistedBeforeNode[node], ", "))
					for _, vn in ipairs(hoistedBeforeNode[node]) do
						declaredVars[vn] = true
					end
				end

				local k = node.kind
				if k == "Seq" then
					for idx, ch in ipairs(node.body) do
						emitNode(ch, isTrailingMain and idx == #node.body)
					end
				elseif k == "BlockRef" then
					local b = node.block
					for i = node.fromIdx, node.toIdx do
						local ins = b.insns[i]
						if ins then
							-- Suprimir `return` final vacio del prototipo principal o cuerpo de funcion
							if isTrailingMain and i == node.toIdx and ins.name == "RETURN" and ins.B == 1 then
								-- nada que emitir
							else
								liftIns(ins)
							end
						end
					end
				elseif k == "Break" then
					emitLine("break")
				elseif k == "Continue" then
					emitLine("continue")
				elseif k == "GotoFallback" then
					emitLine(string.format("-- [unstructured jump -> B%d]", node.target.id))
				elseif k == "If" then
					local branches = {}
					for _, br in ipairs(node.branches) do
						branches[#branches + 1] = br
					end
					local tailElse = node.elseBody
					while tailElse do
						local innerIf
						if tailElse.kind == "If" and not hoistedBeforeNode[tailElse] then
							innerIf = tailElse
						elseif tailElse.kind == "Seq" and #tailElse.body == 1 and tailElse.body[1].kind == "If" and not hoistedBeforeNode[tailElse.body[1]] then
							innerIf = tailElse.body[1]
						end
						if not innerIf then
							break
						end
						for _, br in ipairs(innerIf.branches) do
							branches[#branches + 1] = br
						end
						tailElse = innerIf.elseBody
					end
					for i, br in ipairs(branches) do
						local kw = (i == 1) and "if" or "elseif"
						local condE = evalCond(br.cond)
						emitLine(string.format("%s %s then", kw, condE.text))
						local savedDecl = {}
						for dk, dv in pairs(declaredVars) do savedDecl[dk] = dv end
						local snap = snapshotState()
						indentLevel += 1
						emitNode(br.body, false)
						indentLevel -= 1
						declaredVars = savedDecl
						restoreState(snap)
					end
					if tailElse then
						emitLine("else")
						local savedDecl = {}
						for dk, dv in pairs(declaredVars) do savedDecl[dk] = dv end
						local snap = snapshotState()
						indentLevel += 1
						emitNode(tailElse, false)
						indentLevel -= 1
						declaredVars = savedDecl
						restoreState(snap)
					end
					emitLine("end")
				elseif k == "While" then
					if node.condSetup then
						-- Si condSetup tiene una unica expresion/llamada que alimenta `cond`, intentar integrarla en `while <cond> do`
						local hasSideAssign = false
						for i = node.condSetup.fromIdx, node.condSetup.toIdx do
							local ins = node.condSetup.block.insns[i]
							if ins and persistentRegs[ins.A] and ins.name ~= "NOP" then
								hasSideAssign = true
								break
							end
						end
						if not hasSideAssign then
							local saveEmit = emitLine
							emitLine = function() end
							emitNode(node.condSetup, false)
							emitLine = saveEmit
							local condE = evalCond(node.cond)
							emitLine(string.format("while %s do", condE.text))
						else
							emitLine("while true do")
							indentLevel += 1
							emitNode(node.condSetup, false)
							local condE = evalCond(node.cond)
							emitLine(string.format("if not (%s) then break end", condE.text))
							indentLevel -= 1
						end
					else
						local condE = evalCond(node.cond)
						emitLine(string.format("while %s do", condE.text))
					end
					local savedDecl = {}
					for dk, dv in pairs(declaredVars) do savedDecl[dk] = dv end
					indentLevel += 1
					emitNode(node.body, false)
					indentLevel -= 1
					declaredVars = savedDecl
					emitLine("end")
				elseif k == "RepeatUntil" then
					emitLine("repeat")
					local savedDecl = {}
					for dk, dv in pairs(declaredVars) do savedDecl[dk] = dv end
					indentLevel += 1
					emitNode(node.body, false)
					indentLevel -= 1
					local condE = evalCond(node.cond)
					declaredVars = savedDecl
					emitLine(string.format("until %s", condE.text))
				elseif k == "NumericFor" then
					local prepBlk = node.prep or node.prepBlock
					local prepIns = prepBlk and prepBlk.insns[#prepBlk.insns]
					local baseReg = prepIns and prepIns.A or 0
					local limitE = readReg(baseReg, prepIns and prepIns.pc, false)
					local stepE = readReg(baseReg + 1, prepIns and prepIns.pc, false)
					local initE = readReg(baseReg + 2, prepIns and prepIns.pc, false)
					local loopPc = node.header and node.header.startPc or (prepIns and prepIns.pc + 1) or 0
					local lv = findLocVar(p, baseReg + 2, loopPc)
					local idxName = lv and locvarName[lv] or allocName(nil, "i" .. (baseReg + 2))
					if stepE.numVal == 1 then
						emitLine(string.format("for %s = %s, %s do", idxName, initE.text, limitE.text))
					else
						emitLine(string.format("for %s = %s, %s, %s do", idxName, initE.text, limitE.text, stepE.text))
					end
					local prevFor = activeForVars[baseReg + 2]
					local savedDecl = {}
					for dk, dv in pairs(declaredVars) do savedDecl[dk] = dv end
					activeForVars[baseReg + 2] = idxName
					declaredVars[idxName] = true
					indentLevel += 1
					emitNode(node.body, false)
					indentLevel -= 1
					activeForVars[baseReg + 2] = prevFor
					declaredVars = savedDecl
					emitLine("end")
				elseif k == "GenericFor" then
					local prepBlk = node.prep or node.prepBlock
					local prepIns = prepBlk and prepBlk.insns[#prepBlk.insns]
					local baseReg = prepIns and prepIns.A or 0
					local nvars = prepToForVars[baseReg] or 2
					local loopPc = node.header and node.header.startPc or (prepIns and prepIns.pc + 1) or 0
					local varNames, prevFor = {}, {}
					for i = 0, nvars - 1 do
						local r = baseReg + 3 + i
						local lv = findLocVar(p, r, loopPc)
						local vn = lv and locvarName[lv] or allocName(nil, "v" .. r)
						varNames[#varNames + 1] = vn
						prevFor[r] = activeForVars[r]
						activeForVars[r] = vn
					end
					local iterE = regExpr[baseReg] or readReg(baseReg, prepIns and prepIns.pc, false)
					local iterText = iterE.text
					if not iterE.isGenCall and regExpr[baseReg + 1] then
						local parts = { iterE.text, regExpr[baseReg + 1].text }
						if regExpr[baseReg + 2] then
							parts[#parts + 1] = regExpr[baseReg + 2].text
						end
						iterText = table.concat(parts, ", ")
					end
					emitLine(string.format("for %s in %s do", table.concat(varNames, ", "), iterText))
					local savedDecl = {}
					for dk, dv in pairs(declaredVars) do savedDecl[dk] = dv end
					for _, vn in ipairs(varNames) do
						declaredVars[vn] = true
					end
					indentLevel += 1
					emitNode(node.body, false)
					indentLevel -= 1
					for i = 0, nvars - 1 do
						activeForVars[baseReg + 3 + i] = prevFor[baseReg + 3 + i]
					end
					declaredVars = savedDecl
					emitLine("end")
				end
			end

			emitNode(tree, true)

			return {
				params = paramNames,
				body = lines,
				upvalNames = upvalNames,
			}
		end

		function Lifter.decompile(bc, opts)
			opts = opts or {}
			local CFG = opts.CFG
			local Structurer = opts.Structurer
			assert(CFG and Structurer, "Lifter.decompile requiere opts.CFG y opts.Structurer")

			local mainProto = bc.protos[bc.main + 1]
			assert(mainProto, "Proto principal no encontrado")

			local cfg = CFG.build(mainProto.insn or {}, { jumpOf = opts.jumpOf })
			local tree = Structurer.structure(cfg)
			local res = Lifter.liftProto(bc, mainProto, cfg, tree, opts)
			return res.body
		end

		function Lifter.decompileString(bc, opts)
			return table.concat(Lifter.decompile(bc, opts), "\n")
		end

		return Lifter
	end)()

	BUILD.source = function(cur, opts)
		local bc = cur.bc
		link(bc)
		local out = opts.strip and {} or header(cur)
		local target = string.match(tostring(Zenith.values[N.target] or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		local bodyLines
		if target ~= "" then
			local p = resolveTarget(bc)
			if not p then
				out[#out + 1] = "-- Function Target no encontrado (indice o nombre). Vacio = main."
				return out
			end
			local cfg = CFG.build(decode(bc, p), { jumpOf = jumpOf })
			local tree = Structurer.structure(cfg)
			local lifted = Lifter.liftProto(bc, p, cfg, tree, { CFG = CFG, Structurer = Structurer, jumpOf = jumpOf })
			local sigP = table.concat(lifted.params, ", ")
			if p.vararg and p.index ~= bc.main then
				sigP = (#sigP > 0) and (sigP .. ", ...") or "..."
			end
			bodyLines = { "local function " .. (p.name or ("fn_" .. p.index)) .. "(" .. sigP .. ")" }
			for _, ln in ipairs(lifted.body) do
				bodyLines[#bodyLines + 1] = "\t" .. ln
			end
			bodyLines[#bodyLines + 1] = "end"
		else
			bodyLines = Lifter.decompile(bc, { CFG = CFG, Structurer = Structurer, jumpOf = jumpOf })
		end
		for _, ln in ipairs(bodyLines) do
			out[#out + 1] = ln
		end
		return out
	end

	BUILD["strings & refs"] = function(cur)
		local bc = cur.bc
		link(bc)
		local out = header(cur)
		local importsSeen, imports = {}, {}
		local specialSeen, specials = {}, {}
		local strSeen, strs = {}, {}

		for _, p in ipairs(bc.protos) do
			local fnTag = "#" .. p.index .. (p.name and (" (" .. p.name .. ")") or "")
			for _, k in ipairs(p.k) do
				if k.t == "import" then
					local name = kstr(p, k, true)
					if not importsSeen[name] then
						importsSeen[name] = fnTag
						imports[#imports + 1] = name
					end
				elseif k.t == "string" and type(k.v) == "string" and k.v ~= "" then
					local s = k.v
					if not strSeen[s] then
						strSeen[s] = fnTag
						strs[#strs + 1] = s
					end
					if not specialSeen[s] and (
						string.find(s, "rbxassetid://", 1, true)
						or string.find(s, "http://", 1, true)
						or string.find(s, "https://", 1, true)
						or string.find(s, "Remote", 1, true)
						or string.find(s, "FireServer", 1, true)
						or string.find(s, "InvokeServer", 1, true)
						or string.find(s, "OnClientEvent", 1, true)
						or string.find(s, "Bindable", 1, true)
					) then
						specialSeen[s] = fnTag
						specials[#specials + 1] = s
					end
				end
			end
		end

		table.sort(imports)
		table.sort(specials)
		table.sort(strs)

		out[#out + 1] = string.format("-- Imports / Globals (%d)", #imports)
		for _, item in ipairs(imports) do
			out[#out + 1] = string.format("  %-36s [%s]", item, importsSeen[item])
		end
		out[#out + 1] = ""
		out[#out + 1] = string.format("-- Remotes / Assets / URLs (%d)", #specials)
		for _, item in ipairs(specials) do
			local q = string.gsub(string.gsub(item, "\n", "\\n"), "\r", "\\r")
			out[#out + 1] = string.format("  %q [%s]", q, specialSeen[item])
		end
		out[#out + 1] = ""
		out[#out + 1] = string.format("-- Todas las Strings Unicas (%d)", #strs)
		for _, item in ipairs(strs) do
			local q = string.gsub(string.gsub(item, "\n", "\\n"), "\r", "\\r")
			if #q > 160 then
				q = string.sub(q, 1, 160) .. "..."
			end
			out[#out + 1] = string.format("  %q [%s]", q, strSeen[item])
		end
		return out
	end

	BUILD["script info"] = function(cur)
		local bc = cur.bc
		link(bc)
		local totalInsn, totalK, totalUps, debugProtos = 0, 0, 0, 0
		for _, p in ipairs(bc.protos) do
			totalInsn += #p.code
			totalK += #p.k
			totalUps += p.nups
			if p.hasDebug then
				debugProtos += 1
			end
		end
		local out = header(cur)
		out[#out + 1] = "-- Resumen Estructural"
		out[#out + 1] = string.format("  Protos totales      : %d (con debug info: %d)", #bc.protos, debugProtos)
		out[#out + 1] = string.format("  Instrucciones total : %d words", totalInsn)
		out[#out + 1] = string.format("  Constantes total    : %d", totalK)
		out[#out + 1] = string.format("  Upvalues total      : %d", totalUps)

		local getClo = Zenith.cap("getscriptclosure") and Zenith.capFn("getscriptclosure")
		local getHash = Zenith.cap("getfunctionhash") and Zenith.capFn("getfunctionhash")
		if getClo and cur.inst then
			local okC, fn = pcall(getClo, cur.inst)
			if okC and type(fn) == "function" then
				out[#out + 1] = "  Script Closure      : OK"
				if getHash then
					local okH, h = pcall(getHash, fn)
					if okH and h then
						out[#out + 1] = "  Function Hash       : " .. tostring(h)
					end
				end
			else
				out[#out + 1] = "  Script Closure      : no disponible"
			end
		end
		return out
	end

	local Index = { entries = {}, filteredEntries = {}, byLabel = {}, shown = {}, labelOf = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) }

	local function pathOf(inst)
		local parts, cur, guard = {}, inst, 0
		while cur and cur ~= game and guard < 200 do
			table.insert(parts, 1, cur.Name)
			cur = cur.Parent
			guard += 1
		end
		return table.concat(parts, "."), cur ~= game
	end

	local function resolvePathInstance(rawPath)
		local trimmed = string.match(tostring(rawPath or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if trimmed == "" then
			return nil, "Script Path vacio"
		end
		if Index.byLabel[trimmed] then
			return Index.byLabel[trimmed], trimmed
		end

		local path = string.gsub(trimmed, "^game%.", "")
		local cur = game
		local first = true
		for seg in string.gmatch(path, "[^%.]+") do
			if first and seg == "LocalPlayer" then
				cur = localPlayer
			else
				local ok, child = pcall(cur.FindFirstChild, cur, seg)
				if ok and child then
					cur = child
				elseif first then
					local oks, svc = pcall(game.GetService, game, seg)
					cur = (oks and svc) or nil
				else
					cur = nil
				end
			end
			if not cur then
				return nil, "No encontrado: " .. trimmed
			end
			first = false
		end

		local ok, isScript = pcall(cur.IsA, cur, "LuaSourceContainer")
		if not ok or not isScript then
			return nil, "No es un script: " .. trimmed
		end
		local label = Index.labelOf[cur] or pathOf(cur)
		return cur, label
	end

	local function isCorePath(path)
		return string.sub(path, 1, 8) == "CoreGui."
			or string.sub(path, 1, 13) == "CorePackages."
			or string.sub(path, 1, 14) == "[nil] CoreGui."
			or string.sub(path, 1, 19) == "[nil] CorePackages."
	end

	local function isRobloxFilteredEntry(e, includeCore, ignoreInternals, ignorePlayerMod, svcSet, scrSet)
		if not ignoreInternals then
			return false
		end
		local clean = string.gsub(e.lower, "^%[nil%]%s+", "")
		clean = string.gsub(clean, "%s+#%d+$", "")
		local svc = string.match(clean, "^([^%.]+)")
		if svc and svcSet[svc] then
			if not (includeCore and (svc == "coregui" or svc == "corepackages")) then
				return true
			end
		end
		for seg in string.gmatch(clean, "[^%.]+") do
			if scrSet[seg] then
				return true
			end
		end
		if ignorePlayerMod then
			if string.find(clean, "playermodule", 1, true)
				or string.find(clean, "chatscript", 1, true)
				or string.find(clean, "bubblechat", 1, true)
				or string.find(clean, "rbxcharactersounds", 1, true)
				or string.find(clean, "defaultchatsystemchatevents", 1, true)
				or string.find(clean, "experiencechat", 1, true) then
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	function Index.apply()
		local filter = tostring(Zenith.values[N.class] or "All")
		local needle = string.lower(tostring(Zenith.values[N.search] or ""))
		local includeCore = flag(N.coreScripts, false)
		local ignoreInternals = flag(N.ignoreInternals, true)
		local ignorePlayerMod = flag(N.ignorePlayerMod, true)
		local svcSet = Zenith.parseCsvSet(Zenith.values[N.ignoreServices] or (Zenith.robloxDefaults and Zenith.robloxDefaults.services))
		local scrSet = Zenith.parseCsvSet(Zenith.values[N.ignoreScripts] or (Zenith.robloxDefaults and Zenith.robloxDefaults.scripts))
		table.clear(Index.shown)
		table.clear(Index.filteredEntries)
		local total = 0
		for _, e in ipairs(Index.entries) do
			if (includeCore or not e.isCore)
				and not isRobloxFilteredEntry(e, includeCore, ignoreInternals, ignorePlayerMod, svcSet, scrSet)
				and (filter == "All" or e.class == filter)
				and (needle == "" or string.find(e.lower, needle, 1, true)) then
				total += 1
				Index.filteredEntries[total] = e
				if total <= MAX_SHOWN then
					Index.shown[total] = e.label
				end
			end
		end
		set(N.scriptsFound, total)
		if Zenith.setItems then
			pcall(Zenith.setItems, N.list, Index.shown)
		end
		Zenith.log(
			"Info",
			string.format(
				"Scripts: %d indexados | %d tras filtro%s",
				#Index.entries,
				total,
				total > MAX_SHOWN and (" (mostrando " .. MAX_SHOWN .. ", refina con Script Search)") or ""
			)
		)
	end

	function Index.scan()
		if Index.scanning then
			return
		end
		Index.scanning = true
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			local includeNil = flag(N.nilScripts, false)
			local seen, found, order = {}, {}, 0

			local function add(inst)
				if typeof(inst) ~= "Instance" or seen[inst] then
					return
				end
				local okc, isScript = pcall(inst.IsA, inst, "LuaSourceContainer")
				if not okc or not isScript then
					return
				end
				seen[inst] = true
				local okp, base, detached = pcall(pathOf, inst)
				if not okp or (detached and not includeNil) then
					return
				end
				order += 1
				found[#found + 1] = {
					inst = inst,
					base = detached and ("[nil] " .. base) or base,
					class = inst.ClassName,
					n = order,
				}
			end

			local function each(list)
				if type(list) ~= "table" then
					return
				end
				for i, v in ipairs(list) do
					add(v)
					if i % 3000 == 0 then
						task.wait()
					end
				end
			end

			for _, capName in ipairs({ "getscripts", "getloadedmodules" }) do
				local f = Zenith.cap(capName) and Zenith.capFn(capName)
				if f then
					local okf, list = pcall(f)
					if okf then
						each(list)
					end
				end
			end
			local okd, desc = pcall(game.GetDescendants, game)
			if okd then
				each(desc)
			end

			if includeNil then
				local f = Zenith.cap("getnilinstances") and Zenith.capFn("getnilinstances")
				if f then
					local okn, list = pcall(f)
					if okn and type(list) == "table" then
						for i, inst in ipairs(list) do
							if typeof(inst) == "Instance" then
								add(inst)
								local okg, d = pcall(inst.GetDescendants, inst)
								if okg then
									each(d)
								end
							end
							if i % 500 == 0 then
								task.wait()
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			table.sort(found, function(a, b)
				if a.base ~= b.base then
					return a.base < b.base
				end
				return a.n < b.n
			end)

			table.clear(Index.byLabel)
			local entries = {}
			for _, e in ipairs(found) do
				local label = e.base
				if Index.byLabel[label] then
					local n = 2
					repeat
						label = string.format("%s #%d", e.base, n)
						n += 1
					until not Index.byLabel[label]
				end
				Index.byLabel[label] = e.inst
				Index.labelOf[e.inst] = label
				entries[#entries + 1] = {
					label = label,
					inst = e.inst,
					class = e.class,
					lower = string.lower(label),
					isCore = isCorePath(label),
				}
			end
			Index.entries = entries
		end)
		Index.scanning = false
		if not ok then
			Zenith.log("Error", "Scan Scripts: " .. tostring(err))
			return
		end
		Index.apply()
	end

	local Dec = {
		Index = Index,
		parse = parse,
		decode = decode,
		link = link,
		kstr = kstr,
		jumpOf = jumpOf,
		OPN = OPN,
		SPEC = SPEC,
		CFG = CFG,
		Structurer = Structurer,
		Lifter = Lifter,
	}
	Zenith.Decompiler = Dec

	local cache, cacheOrder = {}, {}
	local decompiledCount = 0

	local function parseCached(bytes)
		local hit = cache[bytes]
		if hit then
			return hit
		end
		local bc = parse(bytes)
		cache[bytes] = bc
		table.insert(cacheOrder, bytes)
		if #cacheOrder > 24 then
			cache[table.remove(cacheOrder, 1)] = nil
		end
		return bc
	end
	Dec.parseCached = parseCached

	local function publish(lines, ext)
		Dec.lines, Dec.ext = lines, ext or "txt"
		local out = Zenith.outputs and Zenith.outputs[N.out]
		if not out then
			return
		end
		local query = string.lower(string.match(tostring(Zenith.values[N.outputSearch] or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$"))
		local viewLines = lines
		if query ~= "" then
			viewLines = {}
			for idx, ln in ipairs(lines) do
				if string.find(string.lower(ln), query, 1, true) then
					viewLines[#viewLines + 1] = string.format("[%d] %s", idx, ln)
				end
			end
			if #viewLines == 0 then
				viewLines = { "-- Sin coincidencias en Output Search para: " .. query }
			end
		end
		local max = math.max(1, math.floor(num(N.maxLines, 600)))
		local text
		if #viewLines > max then
			text = table.concat(viewLines, "\n", 1, max)
				.. string.format("\n-- ... %d lineas mas (Save/Copy Decompiled guarda el texto completo)", #viewLines - max)
		else
			text = table.concat(viewLines, "\n")
		end
		out.setText(text)
	end
	Dec.publish = publish

	local function viewOpts()
		return {
			lineNumbers = flag(N.lineNumbers, true),
			includeConstants = flag(N.includeConstants, false),
			includeUpvalues = flag(N.includeUpvalues, false),
			strip = flag(N.strip, false),
		}
	end

	local function tryNativeDecompile(inst, reason)
		local nativeDec = Zenith.cap("decompile") and Zenith.capFn("decompile")
		if not nativeDec or typeof(inst) ~= "Instance" then
			return nil
		end
		local ok, src = pcall(nativeDec, inst)
		if ok and type(src) == "string" and src ~= "" then
			local out = {
				"-- [Fallback] Executor decompile(): " .. tostring(reason),
				"",
			}
			for _, ln in ipairs(string.split((string.gsub(src, "\r\n", "\n")), "\n")) do
				out[#out + 1] = ln
			end
			return out
		end
		return nil
	end

	function Dec.render()
		local cur = Dec.current
		if not cur then
			return
		end
		if cur.static then
			publish(cur.static, cur.staticExt or "txt")
			return
		end
		local mode = string.lower(tostring(Zenith.values[N.mode] or "bytecode"))
		if not cur.bc then
			local fb = tryNativeDecompile(cur.inst, "sin bytecode")
			if fb then
				publish(fb, "lua")
			else
				publish({ "-- Sin bytecode y decompile() no disponible o fallo" })
			end
			return
		end
		local builder = BUILD[mode]
		if not builder then
			publish({ "-- Mode '" .. mode .. "' no esta disponible todavia (Fase 2). Cambia Decompile Mode a Bytecode, Function, Constants, Upvalues o Protos." })
			return
		end
		local ok, res = pcall(builder, cur, viewOpts())
		if ok and type(res) == "table" then
			publish(res, mode == "source" and "lua" or "txt")
			return
		end
		local reason = ok and "resultado invalido" or res
		if mode == "source" then
			local fb = tryNativeDecompile(cur.inst, reason)
			if fb then
				publish(fb, "lua")
				return
			end
		end
		publish({ "-- Error al generar la vista: " .. tostring(reason) })
	end

	local function fetch(inst)
		local f = Zenith.capFn("getscriptbytecode")
		if not f then
			return nil, "getscriptbytecode no disponible"
		end
		local ok, bc = pcall(f, inst)
		if not ok then
			return nil, tostring(bc)
		end
		if type(bc) ~= "string" or #bc == 0 then
			return nil, "bytecode vacio"
		end
		return bc
	end

	function Dec.loadInstance(inst, label)
		if not inst then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Decompiler: script no valido")
			return
		end
		local bytes, err = fetch(inst)
		if not bytes then
			local fb = tryNativeDecompile(inst, err)
			if fb then
				decompiledCount += 1
				set(N.decompiled, decompiledCount)
				Dec.current = { label = label, inst = inst, bc = nil, size = 0, class = inst.ClassName }
				publish(fb, "lua")
				return
			end
			Dec.current = nil
			publish({ "-- " .. label, "-- No se pudo obtener el bytecode: " .. tostring(err) })
			return
		end
		local ok, bc = pcall(parseCached, bytes)
		if not ok then
			local fb = tryNativeDecompile(inst, bc)
			if fb then
				decompiledCount += 1
				set(N.decompiled, decompiledCount)
				Dec.current = { label = label, inst = inst, bc = nil, size = #bytes, class = inst.ClassName }
				publish(fb, "lua")
				return
			end
			Dec.current = nil
			publish({ "-- " .. label, "-- Error al parsear el bytecode: " .. tostring(bc) })
			return
		end
		decompiledCount += 1
		set(N.decompiled, decompiledCount)
		Dec.current = { label = label, inst = inst, bc = bc, size = #bytes, class = inst.ClassName }
		Dec.render()
	end

	function Dec.load(label)
		local inst = label and Index.byLabel[label]
		if not inst then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Decompiler: selecciona un script de la lista")
			return
		end
		set(N.path, label)
		Dec.loadInstance(inst, label)
	end

	function Dec.loadPath(rawPath)
		local inst, labelOrErr = resolvePathInstance(rawPath)
		if not inst then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Decompile Path: " .. tostring(labelOrErr))
			publish({ "-- " .. tostring(labelOrErr) })
			return
		end
		Dec.loadInstance(inst, labelOrErr)
	end

	local function sanitize(s)
		return string.sub(string.gsub(s, "[^%w%._%-]", "_"), 1, 100)
	end

	local function ensureFolders(pathParts)
		local mk, isf = Zenith.capFn("makefolder"), Zenith.capFn("isfolder")
		if not mk then
			return
		end
		local cur = ""
		for _, part in ipairs(pathParts) do
			cur = (cur == "") and part or (cur .. "/" .. part)
			if not (isf and isf(cur)) then
				pcall(mk, cur)
			end
		end
	end

	local function bytesToHex(bytes, limit)
		local n = math.min(#bytes, limit or 2048)
		local out = table.create(n)
		for i = 1, n do
			out[i] = string.format("%02X", string.byte(bytes, i))
		end
		if #bytes > n then
			out[#out + 1] = string.format("...(+%d bytes)", #bytes - n)
		end
		return table.concat(out)
	end

	local function anonymizeSource(text)
		local lp = playersService and playersService.LocalPlayer
		if not lp then
			return text
		end
		local uname = tostring(lp.Name or "")
		if uname ~= "" then
			local lowText = string.lower(text)
			local lowName = string.lower(uname)
			local out, pos = {}, 1
			while true do
				local s, e = string.find(lowText, lowName, pos, true)
				if not s then
					out[#out + 1] = string.sub(text, pos)
					break
				end
				out[#out + 1] = string.sub(text, pos, s - 1)
				out[#out + 1] = "Player"
				pos = e + 1
			end
			text = table.concat(out)
		end
		local uid = tonumber(lp.UserId)
		if uid and uid > 0 then
			local uidStr = tostring(uid)
			local out, pos = {}, 1
			while true do
				local s, e = string.find(text, uidStr, pos, true)
				if not s then
					out[#out + 1] = string.sub(text, pos)
					break
				end
				out[#out + 1] = string.sub(text, pos, s - 1)
				out[#out + 1] = "1"
				pos = e + 1
			end
			text = table.concat(out)
		end
		return text
	end

	local function runWithTimeout(timeoutSec, fn)
		local done, ok, res = false, false, nil
		task.spawn(function()
			ok, res = pcall(fn)
			done = true
		end)
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while not done and (timeoutSec <= 0 or os.clock() - t0 < timeoutSec) do
			task.wait()
		end
		if not done then
			return false, "timeout (" .. tostring(timeoutSec) .. "s)"
		end
		return ok, res
	end

	local scriptBytecodeCache = {}

	local function decompileEntryToText(inst, label, timeoutSec, saveBc, anon)
		local bytes, bcErr = fetch(inst)
		local cacheHit = false
		local text

		if bytes and scriptBytecodeCache[bytes] then
			text = scriptBytecodeCache[bytes]
			cacheHit = true
		else
			local okRun, res = runWithTimeout(timeoutSec, function()
				if bytes then
					local okP, bc = pcall(parseCached, bytes)
					if okP then
						local cur = { label = label, inst = inst, bc = bc, size = #bytes, class = inst.ClassName }
						local okS, lines = pcall(BUILD.source, cur, viewOpts())
						if okS and type(lines) == "table" then
							return table.concat(lines, "\n")
						end
					end
				end
				local fb = tryNativeDecompile(inst, bcErr or "fallback")
				if fb then
					return table.concat(fb, "\n")
				end
				return nil
			end)
			if okRun and type(res) == "string" and res ~= "" then
				text = res
				if bytes then
					scriptBytecodeCache[bytes] = text
				end
			else
				text = string.format("-- %s\n-- No se pudo decompilar: %s", tostring(label), tostring(res or bcErr or "desconocido"))
			end
		end

		if saveBc and bytes then
			text = text .. string.format("\n\n-- [ZENITH_BYTECODE_SIZE=%d]\n-- [ZENITH_BYTECODE_HEX=%s]", #bytes, bytesToHex(bytes, 4096))
		end
		if anon then
			text = anonymizeSource(text)
		end
		return text, cacheHit, (bytes ~= nil)
	end

	local function copyFull()
		if not Dec.current or not Dec.lines then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Copy Decompiled: no hay nada decompilado")
			return
		end
		local text = table.concat(Dec.lines, "\n")
		if flag(N.anonymizeDump, false) then
			text = anonymizeSource(text)
		end
		local ok = Zenith.copy(text)
		Zenith.log(ok and "Info" or "Warn", ok and string.format("Decompiled copiado (%d lineas)", #Dec.lines) or "Clipboard no soportado")
	end

	local function saveFull()
		if not Dec.current or not Dec.lines then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Save Decompiled: no hay nada decompilado")
			return
		end
		local text = table.concat(Dec.lines, "\n")
		if flag(N.anonymizeDump, false) then
			text = anonymizeSource(text)
		end
		local name = sanitize(Dec.current.label) .. "." .. (Dec.ext or "txt")
		if Zenith.cap("writefile") then
			ensureFolders({ "Zenith", "Zenith/Decompiled" })
			local path = "Zenith/Decompiled/" .. name
			local ok, err = pcall(Zenith.capFn("writefile"), path, text)
			if ok then
				Zenith.log("Info", string.format("Guardado: %s (%d lineas)", path, #Dec.lines))
				return
			end
			Zenith.log("Warn", "writefile fallo: " .. tostring(err))
		end
		if Zenith.copy then
			Zenith.copy(text)
			Zenith.log("Info", "Texto completo copiado al portapapeles")
		end
	end

	local function getExportTargets()
		if #Index.entries == 0 then
			Index.scan()
		end
		return Index.filteredEntries
	end

	local function dumpAllScripts()
		if not Zenith.cap("writefile") then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Dump All Scripts requiere writefile en el executor")
			return
		end
		local entries = getExportTargets()
		if #entries == 0 then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Dump All Scripts: no hay scripts tras filtros")
			return
		end
		local placeId = tostring(game.PlaceId or 0)
		local baseDir = "Zenith/Dump/" .. placeId
		ensureFolders({ "Zenith", "Zenith/Dump", baseDir })

		local timeoutSec = math.max(1, num(N.decompileTimeout, 10))
		local saveBc = flag(N.saveBytecode, false)
		local anon = flag(N.anonymizeDump, false)
		local wf = Zenith.capFn("writefile")

		local okCount, cacheHits, failCount = 0, 0, 0
		for i, e in ipairs(entries) do
			local segs = {}
			for seg in string.gmatch(string.gsub(e.label, "^%[nil%]%s+", "NilScripts."), "[^%.]+") do
				segs[#segs + 1] = sanitize(seg)
			end
			if #segs == 0 then
				segs[1] = "Script_" .. i
			end
			local folders = { "Zenith", "Zenith/Dump", placeId }
			for j = 1, #segs - 1 do
				folders[#folders + 1] = segs[j]
			end
			ensureFolders(folders)

			local relPath = table.concat(segs, "/") .. ".lua"
			local fullPath = baseDir .. "/" .. relPath
			local src, hit, gotBc = decompileEntryToText(e.inst, e.label, timeoutSec, saveBc, anon)
			if hit then
				cacheHits += 1
			end
			if gotBc or not string.find(src, "-- No se pudo decompilar:", 1, true) then
				okCount += 1
			else
				failCount += 1
			end
			pcall(wf, fullPath, src)
			if i % 15 == 0 then
				task.wait()
			end
		end

		local execName = (Zenith.capabilities and Zenith.capabilities.executor) or "unknown"
		local readme = table.concat({
			"Zenith Script Dump Summary",
			"==========================",
			"PlaceId   : " .. placeId,
			"GameId    : " .. tostring(game.GameId or 0),
			"Executor  : " .. tostring(execName),
			"Timestamp : " .. tostring(os.date("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")),
			"Total     : " .. tostring(#entries),
			"Recovered : " .. tostring(okCount),
			"CacheHits : " .. tostring(cacheHits),
			"Failed    : " .. tostring(failCount),
		}, "\n")
		pcall(wf, baseDir .. "/_README.txt", readme)
		Zenith.log("Info", string.format("Dump All completo: %d scripts (%d cache hits, %d fallos) -> %s", okCount, cacheHits, failCount, baseDir))
	end

	local function xmlClean(s)
		local str = string.gsub(tostring(s or ""), "%c", function(c)
			if c == "\t" or c == "\n" or c == "\r" then
				return c
			end
			return ""
		end)
		if type(utf8) == "table" and type(utf8.len) == "function" then
			local okLen = utf8.len(str)
			if not okLen then
				local clean = {}
				local i, n = 1, #str
				while i <= n do
					local okChar, pos = utf8.len(str, i, i)
					if okChar then
						clean[#clean + 1] = string.sub(str, i, i)
						i += 1
					else
						local matched = false
						for len = 2, 4 do
							if i + len - 1 <= n and utf8.len(str, i, i + len - 1) then
								clean[#clean + 1] = string.sub(str, i, i + len - 1)
								i += len
								matched = true
								break
							end
						end
						if not matched then
							clean[#clean + 1] = "?"
							i += 1
						end
					end
				end
				str = table.concat(clean)
			end
		end
		return str
	end

	local function xmlEscape(s)
		return (string.gsub(xmlClean(s), "[&<>\"']", {
			["&"] = "&amp;",
			["<"] = "&lt;",
			[">"] = "&gt;",
			['"'] = "&quot;",
			["'"] = "&apos;",
		}))
	end

	local function cdataSafe(s)
		return (string.gsub(xmlClean(s), "]]>", "]]]]><![CDATA[>"))
	end

	local function exportScriptsRbxmx()
		local entries = getExportTargets()
		if #entries == 0 then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Export .rbxmx: no hay scripts tras filtros")
			return
		end
		local timeoutSec = math.max(1, num(N.decompileTimeout, 10))
		local saveBc = flag(N.saveBytecode, false)
		local anon = flag(N.anonymizeDump, false)

		local root = { name = "Zenith_Scripts_" .. tostring(game.PlaceId or 0), class = "Folder", children = {}, order = {} }
		for _, e in ipairs(entries) do
			local clean = string.gsub(e.label, "^%[nil%]%s+", "NilScripts.")
			clean = string.gsub(clean, "%s+#%d+$", "")
			local segs = {}
			for seg in string.gmatch(clean, "[^%.]+") do
				segs[#segs + 1] = seg
			end
			local curNode = root
			for j = 1, math.max(0, #segs - 1) do
				local segName = segs[j]
				if not curNode.children[segName] then
					local child = { name = segName, class = "Folder", children = {}, order = {} }
					curNode.children[segName] = child
					curNode.order[#curNode.order + 1] = child
				end
				curNode = curNode.children[segName]
			end
			local leafName = segs[#segs] or e.inst.Name or "Script"
			local src = decompileEntryToText(e.inst, e.label, timeoutSec, saveBc, anon)
			local sNode = {
				name = leafName,
				class = (e.class == "LocalScript" or e.class == "ModuleScript" or e.class == "Script") and e.class or "ModuleScript",
				source = src,
				children = {},
				order = {},
			}
			curNode.order[#curNode.order + 1] = sNode
		end

		local xml = { '<roblox xmlns:xmime="http://www.w3.org/2005/05/xmlmime" xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance" xsi:noNamespaceSchemaLocation="http://www.roblox.com/roblox.xsd" version="4">' }
		local referent = 0
		local function emitNode(node, indent)
			referent += 1
			local pad = string.rep("\t", indent)
			xml[#xml + 1] = string.format('%s<Item class="%s" referent="RBX%d">', pad, xmlEscape(node.class), referent)
			xml[#xml + 1] = string.format('%s\t<Properties>', pad)
			xml[#xml + 1] = string.format('%s\t\t<string name="Name">%s</string>', pad, xmlEscape(node.name))
			if node.source ~= nil then
				xml[#xml + 1] = string.format('%s\t\t<ProtectedString name="Source"><![CDATA[%s]]></ProtectedString>', pad, cdataSafe(node.source))
			end
			xml[#xml + 1] = string.format('%s\t</Properties>', pad)
			for _, ch in ipairs(node.order) do
				emitNode(ch, indent + 1)
			end
			xml[#xml + 1] = pad .. "</Item>"
		end
		emitNode(root, 1)
		xml[#xml + 1] = "</roblox>"

		local fullXml = table.concat(xml, "\n")
		local fileName = "Zenith/Zenith_Scripts_" .. tostring(game.PlaceId or 0) .. ".rbxmx"
		if Zenith.cap("writefile") then
			ensureFolders({ "Zenith" })
			local ok, err = pcall(Zenith.capFn("writefile"), fileName, fullXml)
			if ok then
				Zenith.log("Info", string.format("Exportado .rbxmx (%d scripts) -> %s", #entries, fileName))
				return fullXml
			end
			Zenith.log("Warn", "writefile .rbxmx fallo: " .. tostring(err))
		end
		if Zenith.copy then
			Zenith.copy(fullXml)
			Zenith.log("Info", string.format("XML .rbxmx (%d scripts) copiado al portapapeles", #entries))
		end
		return fullXml
	end

	local exportBusy = false
	local MAX_EXPORT_INSTANCES = 12000

	local function buildPlaceXml()
		local timeoutSec = math.max(1, num(N.decompileTimeout, 10))
		local saveBc = flag(N.saveBytecode, false)
		local anon = flag(N.anonymizeDump, false)
		local ignoreInternals = flag(N.ignoreInternals, true)
		local svcNames = { "Workspace", "ReplicatedStorage", "Lighting", "StarterGui", "StarterPack", "StarterPlayer" }
		local xml = { '<roblox xmlns:xmime="http://www.w3.org/2005/05/xmlmime" xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance" xsi:noNamespaceSchemaLocation="http://www.roblox.com/roblox.xsd" version="4">' }
		local referent, instCount, scriptCount, truncated = 0, 0, 0, false
		local VALUE_CLASSES = { StringValue = "string", IntValue = "int", NumberValue = "double", BoolValue = "bool" }

		local function emitInst(inst, indent, isRootSvc)
			if typeof(inst) ~= "Instance" then
				return
			end
			if instCount >= MAX_EXPORT_INSTANCES then
				truncated = true
				return
			end
			if not isRootSvc then
				local okA, arch = pcall(function() return inst.Archivable end)
				if okA and arch == false then
					return
				end
			end
			local okN, iName = pcall(function() return inst.Name end)
			local okC, iClass = pcall(function() return inst.ClassName end)
			iName = tostring(okN and iName or "Instance")
			iClass = tostring(okC and iClass or "Folder")
			if ignoreInternals and (iClass == "Camera" or iClass == "Terrain" or iClass == "Player") then
				return
			end

			referent += 1
			instCount += 1
			if instCount % 250 == 0 then
				task.wait()
			end
			local pad = string.rep("\t", indent)
			xml[#xml + 1] = string.format('%s<Item class="%s" referent="RBX%d">', pad, xmlEscape(iClass), referent)
			xml[#xml + 1] = pad .. "\t<Properties>"
			xml[#xml + 1] = string.format('%s\t\t<string name="Name">%s</string>', pad, xmlEscape(iName))
			local isScrOk, isScr = pcall(inst.IsA, inst, "LuaSourceContainer")
			if isScrOk and isScr then
				scriptCount += 1
				local lbl = Index.labelOf[inst] or iName
				local src = decompileEntryToText(inst, lbl, timeoutSec, saveBc, anon)
				xml[#xml + 1] = string.format('%s\t\t<ProtectedString name="Source"><![CDATA[%s]]></ProtectedString>', pad, cdataSafe(src))
			elseif VALUE_CLASSES[iClass] then
				local okV, val = pcall(function() return inst.Value end)
				if okV and val ~= nil then
					local tag = VALUE_CLASSES[iClass]
					xml[#xml + 1] = string.format('%s\t\t<%s name="Value">%s</%s>', pad, tag, xmlEscape(tostring(val)), tag)
				end
			end
			xml[#xml + 1] = pad .. "\t</Properties>"

			local okCh, ch = pcall(inst.GetChildren, inst)
			if okCh and type(ch) == "table" then
				for _, child in ipairs(ch) do
					emitInst(child, indent + 1, false)
				end
			end
			xml[#xml + 1] = pad .. "</Item>"
		end

		for _, svcName in ipairs(svcNames) do
			local okS, svc = pcall(game.GetService, game, svcName)
			if okS and svc then
				emitInst(svc, 1, true)
			end
		end
		xml[#xml + 1] = "</roblox>"
		return table.concat(xml, "\n"), instCount, scriptCount, truncated
	end

	local function exportPlaceRbxmx()
		if exportBusy then
			return Zenith.log("Warn", "Export Place: ya hay una exportacion en curso")
		end
		exportBusy = true
		local ok, fullXml, instCount, scriptCount, truncated = pcall(buildPlaceXml)
		exportBusy = false
		if not ok then
			Zenith.log("Error", "Export Place fallo: " .. tostring(fullXml))
			return nil
		end
		if truncated then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Export Place: limite de " .. MAX_EXPORT_INSTANCES .. " instancias alcanzado (exportacion parcial)")
		end

		local fileName = "Zenith/Zenith_Place_" .. tostring(game.PlaceId or 0) .. ".rbxmx"
		if Zenith.cap("writefile") then
			ensureFolders({ "Zenith" })
			local okW, errW = pcall(Zenith.capFn("writefile"), fileName, fullXml)
			if okW then
				Zenith.log("Info", string.format("Export Place .rbxmx: %d instancias (%d scripts) -> %s", instCount, scriptCount, fileName))
				return fullXml
			end
			Zenith.log("Warn", "writefile Place .rbxmx fallo: " .. tostring(errW))
		end
		if Zenith.copy(fullXml) then
			Zenith.log("Info", string.format("Export Place .rbxmx (%d instancias) copiado al portapapeles", instCount))
		else
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Export Place: ni writefile ni portapapeles disponibles")
		end
		return fullXml
	end

	function Zenith.decompileInstance(inst)
		if typeof(inst) ~= "Instance" then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Decompile Caller: script origen no es una Instance valida")
			return false
		end
		if Zenith.switchTab then
			pcall(Zenith.switchTab, "Decompiler")
		end
		local label = Index.labelOf[inst]
		if not label then
			local okp, base, detached = pcall(pathOf, inst)
			label = okp and (detached and ("[nil] " .. base) or base) or tostring(inst.Name or "Script")
		end
		set(N.path, label)
		Dec.loadInstance(inst, label)
		return true
	end

	local XREF_CACHE_MAX = 500
	local xrefCache, xrefCount = {}, 0

	local function buildXrefIndex(bytes)
		local bc = parseCached(bytes)
		if not bc then
			return nil
		end
		link(bc)
		local imports, strings = {}, {}
		for _, p in ipairs(bc.protos) do
			local fnTag = "#" .. tostring(p.index) .. (p.name and (" (" .. p.name .. ")") or "")
			for _, k in ipairs(p.k) do
				if k.t == "import" then
					local nm = kstr(p, k, true)
					imports[#imports + 1] = { val = nm, low = string.lower(nm), fnTag = fnTag }
				elseif k.t == "string" and type(k.v) == "string" and k.v ~= "" then
					strings[#strings + 1] = { val = k.v, low = string.lower(k.v), fnTag = fnTag }
				end
			end
		end
		return { imports = imports, strings = strings }
	end

	local function getXrefIndex(bytes)
		local hit = xrefCache[bytes]
		if hit then
			return hit
		end
		local ok, res = pcall(buildXrefIndex, bytes)
		if not ok or not res then
			return nil
		end
		if xrefCount >= XREF_CACHE_MAX then
			xrefCache, xrefCount = {}, 0
		end
		xrefCache[bytes] = res
		xrefCount += 1
		return res
	end

	local PREVIEW_ESC = { ["\n"] = "\\n", ["\r"] = "\\r", ["\t"] = "\\t", ['"'] = '\\"', ["\\"] = "\\\\" }
	local function quoteShort(s)
		return '"' .. (string.gsub(s, '[%c"\\]', function(c)
			return PREVIEW_ESC[c] or string.format("\\%03d", string.byte(c))
		end)) .. '"'
	end

	local searchBusy = false
	function Dec.globalSearch(rawQuery)
		local query = string.match(tostring(rawQuery or Zenith.values[N.globalSearch] or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if query == "" then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Global Search: escribe un texto o nombre de Remote")
			return 0
		end
		if searchBusy then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Global Search: ya hay una busqueda en curso")
			return 0
		end
		local entries = table.clone(getExportTargets())
		if #entries == 0 then
			publish({ "-- Global Search: no hay scripts tras filtros" }, "txt")
			return 0
		end

		searchBusy = true
		local needle = string.lower(query)
		local out = {
			"-- Zenith Global Bytecode XRef Search",
			"-- Query: " .. query .. " | Scripts escaneados: " .. tostring(#entries),
			"",
		}
		local matchedLabels, totalHits, skippedNoBc = {}, 0, 0

		local okRun, errRun = pcall(function()
			for i, e in ipairs(entries) do
				local bytes = fetch(e.inst)
				if not bytes then
					skippedNoBc += 1
				else
					local idx = getXrefIndex(bytes)
					if idx then
						local scriptHits = {}
						for _, imp in ipairs(idx.imports) do
							if string.find(imp.low, needle, 1, true) then
								scriptHits[#scriptHits + 1] = string.format("  [IMPORT %s] %s", imp.fnTag, imp.val)
							end
						end
						for _, st in ipairs(idx.strings) do
							if string.find(st.low, needle, 1, true) then
								local cut = #st.val > 140
								local preview = quoteShort(string.sub(st.val, 1, 140)) .. (cut and "..." or "")
								scriptHits[#scriptHits + 1] = string.format("  [STRING %s] %s", st.fnTag, preview)
							end
						end
						if #scriptHits > 0 then
							matchedLabels[#matchedLabels + 1] = e.label
							totalHits += #scriptHits
							if #out < 450 then
								out[#out + 1] = string.format("== %s (%d hits)", e.label, #scriptHits)
								for k = 1, math.min(#scriptHits, 12) do
									out[#out + 1] = scriptHits[k]
								end
								if #scriptHits > 12 then
									out[#out + 1] = string.format("  ... (+%d hits mas en este script)", #scriptHits - 12)
								end
								out[#out + 1] = ""
							end
						end
					end
				end
				if i % 40 == 0 then
					task.wait()
				end
			end
		end)
		searchBusy = false
		if not okRun then
			Zenith.log("Error", "Global Search fallo: " .. tostring(errRun))
			return 0
		end

		out[3] = string.format("-- Coincidencias: %d hits en %d scripts (%d sin bytecode omitidos)", totalHits, #matchedLabels, skippedNoBc)
		if #matchedLabels == 0 then
			out[#out + 1] = "-- Sin coincidencias para: " .. query
		else
			if #out >= 450 then
				out[#out + 1] = "-- (salida truncada: usa un termino mas especifico)"
			end
			table.clear(Index.shown)
			for idx = 1, math.min(#matchedLabels, MAX_SHOWN) do
				Index.shown[idx] = matchedLabels[idx]
			end
			set(N.scriptsFound, #matchedLabels)
			if Zenith.setItems then
				pcall(Zenith.setItems, N.list, table.clone(Index.shown))
			end
		end

		Dec.current = { label = "GlobalSearch_" .. sanitize(query), inst = nil, bc = nil, size = 0, class = "XRef", static = out, staticExt = "txt" }
		publish(out, "txt")
		Zenith.log("Info", string.format("Global Search '%s': %d hits en %d scripts", query, totalHits, #matchedLabels))
		return #matchedLabels, totalHits
	end

	local gcBusy = false
	function Dec.gcSearch(rawQuery)
		local query = string.match(tostring(rawQuery or Zenith.values[N.globalSearch] or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if query == "" then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "GC Search: escribe un texto en Global Search")
			return 0
		end
		if gcBusy then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "GC Search: ya hay un escaneo en curso")
			return 0
		end
		local getGC = Zenith.capFn("getgc")
		if not getGC then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "GC Search requiere getgc() en el executor")
			publish({ "-- GC Closure Search requiere getgc() en el executor" }, "txt")
			return 0
		end

		gcBusy = true
		local needle = string.lower(query)
		local isExecFn = Zenith.capFn("isexecutorclosure")
		local isLFn = Zenith.capFn("islclosure")
		local getKs = Zenith.capFn("getconstants") or (type(debug) == "table" and debug.getconstants)
		local getUps = Zenith.capFn("getupvalues") or (type(debug) == "table" and debug.getupvalues)
		local okG, gcList = pcall(getGC, true)
		if not okG or type(gcList) ~= "table" then
			gcBusy = false
			Zenith.log("Error", "getgc fallo: " .. tostring(gcList))
			return 0
		end

		local function pred(fn, obj)
			local ok, r = pcall(fn, obj)
			if ok then
				return r
			end
			return nil
		end

		local function safeStr(v)
			local t = type(v)
			if t == "string" then
				return quoteShort(string.sub(v, 1, 60))
			end
			if t == "number" or t == "boolean" or t == "nil" then
				return tostring(v)
			end
			return "<" .. typeof(v) .. ">"
		end

		local ownTables = {
			[Zenith] = true, [Zenith.values] = true, [Zenith.defaults] = true, [Zenith.actions] = true,
			[Zenith.hooks] = true, [Zenith.setters] = true, [Zenith.outputs] = true, [Zenith.lists] = true,
			[Dec] = true, [Index] = true, [Index.entries] = true, [Index.filteredEntries] = true,
			[Index.shown] = true, [Index.byLabel] = true, [Index.labelOf] = true, [xrefCache] = true,
			[gcList] = true,
		}

		local out = {
			"-- Zenith Live GC Search (Closures + Tables)",
			"-- Query: " .. query .. " | GC objects: " .. tostring(#gcList),
			"",
		}
		local matchedFn, matchedTbl, scannedFn, scannedTbl = 0, 0, 0, 0
		local okScan, errScan = pcall(function()
			for i, obj in ipairs(gcList) do
				local tObj = type(obj)
				if tObj == "function" then
					local skip = false
					if isExecFn and pred(isExecFn, obj) == true then
						skip = true
					elseif isLFn and pred(isLFn, obj) == false then
						skip = true
					end
					if not skip then
						scannedFn += 1
						local src, line, fname = "?", "?", ""
						if type(debug) == "table" and type(debug.info) == "function" then
							local okI, s, l, n = pcall(debug.info, obj, "sln")
							if okI then
								src = tostring(s or "?")
								line = tostring(l or "?")
								fname = tostring(n or "")
							end
						end
						if src ~= "[C]" then
							local hits = {}
							if fname ~= "" and string.find(string.lower(fname), needle, 1, true) then
								hits[#hits + 1] = "name=" .. fname
							end
							if string.find(string.lower(src), needle, 1, true) then
								hits[#hits + 1] = "src=" .. src
							end
							if getKs then
								local okK, ks = pcall(getKs, obj)
								if okK and type(ks) == "table" then
									for ki, kv in pairs(ks) do
										if type(kv) == "string" and string.find(string.lower(kv), needle, 1, true) then
											hits[#hits + 1] = "K[" .. tostring(ki) .. "]=" .. quoteShort(string.sub(kv, 1, 80))
										end
									end
								end
							end
							if getUps then
								local okU, ups = pcall(getUps, obj)
								if okU and type(ups) == "table" then
									for ui, uv in pairs(ups) do
										if type(uv) == "string" and string.find(string.lower(uv), needle, 1, true) then
											hits[#hits + 1] = "U[" .. tostring(ui) .. "]=" .. quoteShort(string.sub(uv, 1, 80))
										end
									end
								end
							end
							if #hits > 0 then
								matchedFn += 1
								if #out < 450 then
									out[#out + 1] = string.format("[GC FN #%d] %s:%s fn=%s", matchedFn, src, line, fname ~= "" and fname or "anon")
									for k = 1, math.min(#hits, 8) do
										out[#out + 1] = "  " .. hits[k]
									end
									out[#out + 1] = ""
								end
							end
						end
					end
				elseif tObj == "table" and not ownTables[obj] and getmetatable(obj) == nil then
					scannedTbl += 1
					local hits = {}
					local seenKeys = 0
					for tk, tv in pairs(obj) do
						seenKeys += 1
						if seenKeys > 64 then
							break
						end
						if type(tk) == "string" and string.find(string.lower(tk), needle, 1, true) then
							hits[#hits + 1] = string.format("[%s] = %s", quoteShort(string.sub(tk, 1, 40)), safeStr(tv))
						elseif type(tv) == "string" and string.find(string.lower(tv), needle, 1, true) then
							hits[#hits + 1] = string.format("[%s] = %s", safeStr(tk), quoteShort(string.sub(tv, 1, 60)))
						end
						if #hits >= 6 then
							break
						end
					end
					if #hits > 0 then
						matchedTbl += 1
						if #out < 450 then
							out[#out + 1] = string.format("[GC TBL #%d]", matchedTbl)
							for _, h in ipairs(hits) do
								out[#out + 1] = "  " .. h
							end
							out[#out + 1] = ""
						end
					end
				end
				if i % 400 == 0 then
					task.wait()
				end
			end
		end)
		gcBusy = false
		if not okScan then
			Zenith.log("Error", "GC Search fallo: " .. tostring(errScan))
			return 0
		end

		local totalMatched = matchedFn + matchedTbl
		out[3] = string.format("-- Escaneados: %d closures, %d tablas | Coincidencias: %d closures, %d tablas", scannedFn, scannedTbl, matchedFn, matchedTbl)
		if totalMatched == 0 then
			out[#out + 1] = "-- Sin coincidencias en memoria para: " .. query
		elseif #out >= 450 then
			out[#out + 1] = "-- (salida truncada: usa un termino mas especifico)"
		end
		Dec.current = { label = "GCSearch_" .. sanitize(query), inst = nil, bc = nil, size = 0, class = "XRef", static = out, staticExt = "txt" }
		publish(out, "txt")
		Zenith.log("Info", string.format("GC Search '%s': %d closures, %d tablas", query, matchedFn, matchedTbl))
		return totalMatched
	end

	local skipGlobalSearchHook = false
	function Zenith.findScriptXRefs(query)
		local q = tostring(query or "")
		if q == "" then
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Find Script XRefs: nombre de remote vacio")
			return 0
		end
		if Zenith.switchTab then
			pcall(Zenith.switchTab, "Decompiler")
		end
		skipGlobalSearchHook = true
		if Zenith.setters and Zenith.setters[N.globalSearch] then
			pcall(Zenith.setters[N.globalSearch], q)
		end
		skipGlobalSearchHook = false
		Zenith.values[N.globalSearch] = q
		return Dec.globalSearch(q)
	end

	local saved = {}
	local function hook(name, fn)
		local prev = Zenith.hooks[name]
		saved[name] = { prev = prev }
		Zenith.hooks[name] = function(...)
			if prev then
				pcall(prev, ...)
			end
			fn(...)
		end
	end

	local function selectedLabel(arg)
		if type(arg) == "string" and Index.byLabel[arg] then
			return arg
		end
		local v = Zenith.values[N.list]
		return type(v) == "string" and v or nil
	end

	local searchToken = 0

	Zenith.register("Decompiler", {
		requires = { "getscriptbytecode", "buffer" },
		optional = { "getscripts", "getloadedmodules", "getnilinstances", "writefile", "makefolder", "isfolder", "setclipboard", "decompile", "getgc", "getconstants", "getupvalues" },
		init = function()
			Zenith.actions[N.scan] = function()
				Index.scan()
			end
			Zenith.actions[N.run] = function()
				Dec.load(selectedLabel())
			end
			Zenith.actions[N.runPath] = function()
				Dec.loadPath(Zenith.values[N.path])
			end
			Zenith.actions[N.copyPath] = function()
				local lbl = selectedLabel() or (Dec.current and Dec.current.label)
				if not lbl or lbl == "" then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "Decompiler: selecciona un script primero")
				end
				local clean = string.gsub(lbl, "%s+#%d+$", "")
				local ok = Zenith.copy(clean)
				Zenith.log(ok and "Info" or "Warn", ok and ("Ruta copiada: " .. clean) or "Clipboard no soportado")
			end
			hook(N.globalSearch, function(q)
				if not skipGlobalSearchHook and string.match(tostring(q or ""), "%S") then
					Dec.globalSearch(q)
				end
			end)
			Zenith.actions[N.copyDec] = copyFull
			Zenith.actions[N.save] = saveFull
			Zenith.actions[N.dumpAll] = dumpAllScripts
			Zenith.actions[N.exportRbxmx] = exportScriptsRbxmx
			Zenith.actions[N.exportPlace] = exportPlaceRbxmx
			Zenith.actions[N.globalScan] = function()
				Dec.globalSearch(Zenith.values[N.globalSearch])
			end
			Zenith.actions[N.gcSearch] = function()
				Dec.gcSearch(Zenith.values[N.globalSearch])
			end

			hook(N.list, function(arg)
				task.defer(Dec.load, selectedLabel(arg))
			end)
			hook(N.class, function()
				task.defer(Index.apply)
			end)
			hook(N.search, function()
				searchToken += 1
				local mine = searchToken
				task.delay(0.2, function()
					if mine == searchToken then
						Index.apply()
					end
				end)
			end)
			hook(N.outputSearch, function()
				task.defer(function()
					if Dec.lines then
						publish(Dec.lines, Dec.ext)
					end
				end)
			end)
			hook(N.nilScripts, function()
				task.defer(Index.scan)
			end)
			hook(N.coreScripts, function()
				task.defer(Index.apply)
			end)
			for _, fName in ipairs({ N.ignoreInternals, N.ignorePlayerMod, N.ignoreServices, N.ignoreScripts }) do
				hook(fName, function()
					task.defer(Index.apply)
				end)
			end
			for _, name in ipairs({ N.mode, N.target, N.lineNumbers, N.includeConstants, N.includeUpvalues, N.strip, N.maxLines }) do
				hook(name, function()
					task.defer(Dec.render)
				end)
			end
			Zenith.log("Info", "Decompiler listo: pulsa Scan Scripts")
		end,
		stop = function()
			for name, s in pairs(saved) do
				Zenith.hooks[name] = s.prev
			end
			table.clear(saved)
			Zenith.actions[N.scan], Zenith.actions[N.run], Zenith.actions[N.runPath], Zenith.actions[N.save] = nil, nil, nil, nil
			Zenith.actions[N.copyDec], Zenith.actions[N.dumpAll], Zenith.actions[N.exportRbxmx], Zenith.actions[N.exportPlace], Zenith.actions[N.globalScan], Zenith.actions[N.gcSearch], Zenith.actions[N.copyPath] = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
			Dec.current, Dec.lines = nil, nil
			table.clear(cache)
			table.clear(cacheOrder)
			table.clear(scriptBytecodeCache)
			table.clear(xrefCache)
			xrefCount = 0
		end,
	})
end

-- Zenith RSpy (Motor vivo de hooks __namecall / hookfunction / OnClientEvent).
do
	local N = {
		enabled = "Remote Spy",
		calls = "Calls Logged",
		filter = "Spy Filter",
		search = "Spy Search",
		ignoreList = "Spy Ignore List",
		list = "Remote List",
		clear = "Clear Spy Logs",
		log = "Spy Log",
		copyCode = "Copy Call Code",
		copyPath = "Copy Remote Path",
		decompileCaller = "Decompile Caller",
		copyFnInfo = "Copy Function Info",
		findXRefs = "Find Script XRefs",
		exportSession = "Export RSpy Session",
		repeatCall = "Repeat Call",
		customArgs = "Custom Args",
		replayCustom = "Replay Custom Args",
		blockRemote = "Block Remote",
		ignoreRemote = "Ignore Remote",
		c2s = "Log Client To Server",
		s2c = "Log Server To Client",
		logReturn = "Log Return Values",
		showStack = "Show Call Stack",
		groupByRemote = "Group By Remote",
		autoScroll = "Spy Auto Scroll",
		maxLogs = "Spy Max Logs",
		ignoreInternals = "Ignore Roblox Internals",
		ignoreBindables = "Ignore Bindables",
		ignorePlayerMod = "Ignore PlayerModule & Chat",
		ignoreServices = "Ignore Services",
		ignoreRbxScripts = "Ignore Roblox Scripts",
		ignoreRbxRemotes = "Ignore Roblox Remotes",
		ignoreExecutor = "Ignore Executor Calls",
		logActors = "Log Actors",
		autoIgnoreSpam = "Auto Ignore Spammy",
		fullSerialize = "Full Serialization",
		fileLogging = "Spy File Logging",
		preferOth = "Prefer Stealth Hook (oth)",
		logInteractions = "Log Interactions",
		logAttributes = "Log Attributes",
	}

	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
	local stopped = false

	local OUTBOUND_CLASSES = {
		FireServer = { RemoteEvent = true, UnreliableRemoteEvent = true },
		InvokeServer = { RemoteFunction = true },
		Fire = { BindableEvent = true },
		Invoke = { BindableFunction = true },
	}

	local FILTER_MATCH = {
		RemoteEvent = { RemoteEvent = true, UnreliableRemoteEvent = true },
		RemoteFunction = { RemoteFunction = true },
		BindableEvent = { BindableEvent = true },
		BindableFunction = { BindableFunction = true },
		Interactions = { ProximityPrompt = true, ClickDetector = true },
	}

	local S2C_SOURCES = {
		RemoteEvent = "OnClientEvent",
		UnreliableRemoteEvent = "OnClientEvent",
		BindableEvent = "Event",
	}

	-- Roblox method names on instances are case-insensitive (legacy camelCase still works)
	local METHOD_LOWER = {}
	for m in pairs(OUTBOUND_CLASSES) do
		METHOD_LOWER[string.lower(m)] = m
	end
	local canonCache = {}
	local function canonMethod(method)
		if type(method) ~= "string" then
			return nil
		end
		local c = canonCache[method]
		if c == nil then
			c = METHOD_LOWER[string.lower(method)] or false
			canonCache[method] = c
		end
		return c or nil
	end

	local function flag(name, default)
		local v = Zenith.values[name]
		if v == nil then
			return default
		end
		return v == true
	end

	local function num(name, default)
		local v = tonumber(Zenith.values[name])
		return v or default
	end

	local function setVal(name, val)
		Zenith.values[name] = val
		local s = Zenith.setters and Zenith.setters[name]
		if s then
			pcall(s, val)
		end
	end

	local savedHooks = {}
	local function hookUI(name, fn)
		local prev = Zenith.hooks[name]
		if savedHooks[name] == nil then
			savedHooks[name] = { prev = prev }
		end
		Zenith.hooks[name] = function(...)
			if prev then
				pcall(prev, ...)
			end
			if stopped then
				return
			end
			local ok, err = pcall(fn, ...)
			if not ok then
				Zenith.log("Error", "RSpy hook '" .. tostring(name) .. "': " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
	end

	local function rawGet(obj, key)
		return obj[key]
	end
	local function safeRead(obj, prop)
		local ok, val = pcall(rawGet, obj, prop)
		if ok then
			return val
		end
		return nil
	end

	local ESC = { ["\n"] = "\\n", ["\r"] = "\\r", ["\t"] = "\\t", ['"'] = '\\"', ["\\"] = "\\\\" }
	local function quoteStr(s)
		return '"' .. (string.gsub(s, '[%c"\\]', function(c)
			return ESC[c] or string.format("\\%03d", string.byte(c))
		end)) .. '"'
	end

	local KEYWORDS = {}
	for w in string.gmatch("and break continue do else elseif end false for function if in local nil not or repeat return then true until while", "%a+") do
		KEYWORDS[w] = true
	end
	local function isIdent(s)
		return type(s) == "string" and not KEYWORDS[s] and string.match(s, "^[%a_][%w_]*$") ~= nil
	end

	local function instancePath(inst)
		if typeof(inst) ~= "Instance" then
			return "nil"
		end
		if inst == game then
			return "game"
		end
		if localPlayer and inst == localPlayer then
			return 'game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer'
		end
		local parts, cur, guard = {}, inst, 0
		while cur and cur ~= game and guard < 120 do
			local parent = safeRead(cur, "Parent")
			local name = safeRead(cur, "Name") or "?"
			if parent == game then
				local cls = safeRead(cur, "ClassName")
				if cls == "Workspace" then
					table.insert(parts, 1, "workspace")
				elseif cls and cls ~= "" and pcall(game.GetService, game, cls) then
					table.insert(parts, 1, string.format("game:GetService(%s)", quoteStr(cls)))
				else
					table.insert(parts, 1, string.format("game:FindFirstChild(%s)", quoteStr(name)))
				end
				cur = game
				break
			elseif localPlayer and cur == localPlayer then
				table.insert(parts, 1, 'game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer')
				cur = game
				break
			else
				if isIdent(name) then
					table.insert(parts, 1, "." .. name)
				else
					table.insert(parts, 1, string.format(":FindFirstChild(%s)", quoteStr(name)))
				end
			end
			cur = parent
			guard += 1
		end
		if cur ~= game then
			return string.format("nil --[[ detached: %s ]]", tostring(safeRead(inst, "Name") or "?"))
		end
		local joined = table.concat(parts, "")
		if string.sub(joined, 1, 1) == "." then
			joined = "game" .. joined
		end
		return joined
	end

	local function fmtNum(v)
		if v ~= v then
			return "0/0"
		elseif v == math.huge then
			return "math.huge"
		elseif v == -math.huge then
			return "-math.huge"
		end
		local s = string.format("%.14g", v)
		if tonumber(s) ~= v then
			s = string.format("%.17g", v)
		end
		return s
	end

	local serializeValue
	local function serializeRaw(v, depth, seen)
		depth = depth or 0
		seen = seen or {}
		local full = flag(N.fullSerialize, false)
		local strLimit = full and 8192 or 400
		local keyLimit = full and 500 or 40
		local depthLimit = full and 12 or 4
		local t = typeof(v)
		if t == "nil" then
			return "nil"
		elseif t == "boolean" then
			return tostring(v)
		elseif t == "number" then
			return fmtNum(v)
		elseif t == "string" then
			if #v > strLimit then
				return quoteStr(string.sub(v, 1, strLimit)) .. " --[[ +" .. (#v - strLimit) .. " bytes ]]"
			end
			return quoteStr(v)
		elseif t == "Instance" then
			return instancePath(v)
		elseif t == "Vector3" then
			return string.format("Vector3.new(%s, %s, %s)", fmtNum(v.X), fmtNum(v.Y), fmtNum(v.Z))
		elseif t == "Vector2" then
			return string.format("Vector2.new(%s, %s)", fmtNum(v.X), fmtNum(v.Y))
		elseif t == "Vector3int16" then
