			return string.format("Vector3int16.new(%d, %d, %d)", v.X, v.Y, v.Z)
		elseif t == "CFrame" then
			local comps = { v:GetComponents() }
			for i, c in ipairs(comps) do
				comps[i] = fmtNum(c)
			end
			return "CFrame.new(" .. table.concat(comps, ", ") .. ")"
		elseif t == "Color3" then
			return string.format("Color3.fromRGB(%d, %d, %d)", math.floor(v.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(v.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(v.B * 255 + 0.5))
		elseif t == "UDim2" then
			return string.format("UDim2.new(%s, %s, %s, %s)", fmtNum(v.X.Scale), fmtNum(v.X.Offset), fmtNum(v.Y.Scale), fmtNum(v.Y.Offset))
		elseif t == "UDim" then
			return string.format("UDim.new(%s, %s)", fmtNum(v.Scale), fmtNum(v.Offset))
		elseif t == "Rect" then
			return string.format("Rect.new(%s, %s, %s, %s)", fmtNum(v.Min.X), fmtNum(v.Min.Y), fmtNum(v.Max.X), fmtNum(v.Max.Y))
		elseif t == "BrickColor" then
			return string.format("BrickColor.new(%s)", quoteStr(tostring(v.Name)))
		elseif t == "EnumItem" or t == "Enum" then
			return tostring(v)
		elseif t == "NumberRange" then
			return string.format("NumberRange.new(%s, %s)", fmtNum(v.Min), fmtNum(v.Max))
		elseif t == "TweenInfo" then
			return string.format("TweenInfo.new(%s, %s, %s, %d, %s, %s)", fmtNum(v.Time), tostring(v.EasingStyle), tostring(v.EasingDirection), v.RepeatCount or 0, tostring(v.Reverses == true), fmtNum(v.DelayTime or 0))
		elseif t == "Ray" then
			return string.format("Ray.new(%s, %s)", serializeValue(v.Origin, depth + 1, seen), serializeValue(v.Direction, depth + 1, seen))
		elseif t == "Region3" then
			local half = v.Size * 0.5
			return string.format("Region3.new(%s, %s)", serializeValue(v.CFrame.Position - half, depth + 1, seen), serializeValue(v.CFrame.Position + half, depth + 1, seen))
		elseif t == "NumberSequence" then
			local kps = {}
			for _, kp in ipairs(v.Keypoints or {}) do
				kps[#kps + 1] = string.format("NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(%s, %s, %s)", fmtNum(kp.Time), fmtNum(kp.Value), fmtNum(kp.Envelope or 0))
			end
			return "NumberSequence.new({" .. table.concat(kps, ", ") .. "})"
		elseif t == "ColorSequence" then
			local kps = {}
			for _, kp in ipairs(v.Keypoints or {}) do
				kps[#kps + 1] = string.format("ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(%s, %s)", fmtNum(kp.Time), serializeValue(kp.Value, depth + 1, seen))
			end
			return "ColorSequence.new({" .. table.concat(kps, ", ") .. "})"
		elseif t == "DateTime" then
			return string.format("DateTime.fromUnixTimestampMillis(%d)", v.UnixTimestampMillis or 0)
		elseif t == "buffer" then
			return string.format("buffer.create(%d)", buffer.len(v))
		elseif t == "function" then
			return "function() end"
		elseif t == "table" then
			if seen[v] then
				return "{ --[[ cyclic ]] }"
			end
			if depth >= depthLimit then
				return "{ --[[ max depth ]] }"
			end
			seen[v] = true
			local pad = string.rep("\t", depth + 1)
			local closePad = string.rep("\t", depth)
			local parts, count = {}, 0
			for k, val in pairs(v) do
				count += 1
				if count > keyLimit then
					table.insert(parts, pad .. "--[[ ... more keys ]]")
					break
				end
				local keyStr
				if type(k) == "number" then
					keyStr = "[" .. fmtNum(k) .. "]"
				elseif isIdent(k) then
					keyStr = k
				else
					keyStr = "[" .. serializeValue(k, depth + 1, seen) .. "]"
				end
				table.insert(parts, string.format("%s%s = %s,", pad, keyStr, serializeValue(val, depth + 1, seen)))
			end
			seen[v] = nil
			if #parts == 0 then
				return "{}"
			end
			return "{\n" .. table.concat(parts, "\n") .. "\n" .. closePad .. "}"
		end
		local desc = string.gsub(tostring(v), "%]%]", "] ]")
		return string.format("nil --[[ %s: %s ]]", t, desc)
	end

	function serializeValue(v, depth, seen)
		local ok, res = pcall(serializeRaw, v, depth, seen)
		if ok then
			return res
		end
		return "nil --[[ serialize error ]]"
	end

	local state = {
		installed = false,
		seq = 0,
		entries = {}, -- oldest -> newest
		byLabel = {},
		groupByLabel = {},
		groupCount = {},
		groupKeyOf = {},
		shownLabels = {},
		selectedLabel = nil,
		selectedGroupKey = nil,
		blocked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
		ignoredNames = {},
		ignoredServices = {},
		ignoredRbxScripts = {},
		ignoredRbxRemotes = {},
		internal = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }), -- [thread] = true (our own calls)
		depth = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),    -- [thread] = re-entrancy depth
		queue = {},
		flushing = false,
		s2cConns = {},
		s2cHooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
		spyConnFns = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
		signalMapping = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "kv" }),
		signalHook = nil,
		wrappedCallbacks = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
		descendantConn = nil,
		actorChannel = nil,
		actorConns = {},
		actorHooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
		interactionConns = {},
		interactionHooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
		attrConns = {},
		attrHooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
		spamTrack = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),
		logFilePath = nil,
		oldNamecall = nil,
		namecallRec = nil,
		namecallVia = nil,
		oldNewindex = nil,
		newindexRec = nil,
		directHooks = {},
		ignoreGen = 0,
	}

	local function refreshIgnoreSet()
		state.ignoredNames = Zenith.parseCsvSet(Zenith.values[N.ignoreList])
		local defs = Zenith.robloxDefaults or {}
		state.ignoredServices = Zenith.parseCsvSet(Zenith.values[N.ignoreServices] or defs.services)
		state.ignoredRbxScripts = Zenith.parseCsvSet(Zenith.values[N.ignoreRbxScripts] or defs.scripts)
		state.ignoredRbxRemotes = Zenith.parseCsvSet(Zenith.values[N.ignoreRbxRemotes] or defs.remotes)
		state.ignoreGen = (state.ignoreGen or 0) + 1
	end

	local function isIgnoredName(name)
		return state.ignoredNames[string.lower(tostring(name or ""))] == true
	end

	local function isBindableClass(cls)
		return cls == "BindableEvent" or cls == "BindableFunction"
	end

	local function hasIgnoredAncestor(inst, setA, setB, checkPlayerMod)
		local cur, guard = inst, 0
		while cur and cur ~= game and guard < 24 do
			local parent = safeRead(cur, "Parent")
			local nm = string.lower(tostring(safeRead(cur, "Name") or ""))
			if setA and setA[nm] then
				return true
			end
			if setB and setB[nm] then
				return true
			end
			if parent == game then
				local svcCls = string.lower(tostring(safeRead(cur, "ClassName") or ""))
				if state.ignoredServices[svcCls] or state.ignoredServices[nm] then
					return true
				end
				break
			end
			if checkPlayerMod and (nm == "playermodule" or nm == "chatscript" or nm == "bubblechat" or nm == "rbxcharactersounds" or nm == "defaultchatsystemchatevents" or nm == "experiencechat") then
				return true
			end
			cur = parent
			guard += 1
		end
		return false
	end

	local function remoteIgnored(inst, rName, cls)
		if isIgnoredName(rName) then
			return true
		end
		if cls and isBindableClass(cls) and flag(N.ignoreBindables, true) then
			return true
		end
		if not flag(N.ignoreInternals, true) then
			return false
		end
		local lowerName = string.lower(tostring(rName or ""))
		if lowerName ~= "" and state.ignoredRbxRemotes[lowerName] then
			return true
		end
		if typeof(inst) == "Instance" and hasIgnoredAncestor(inst, state.ignoredRbxRemotes, nil, flag(N.ignorePlayerMod, true)) then
			return true
		end
		return false
	end

	local function callerIgnored(callerInst, isExecutor)
		if isExecutor and flag(N.ignoreExecutor, true) then
			return true
		end
		if not flag(N.ignoreInternals, true) then
			return false
		end
		if typeof(callerInst) == "Instance" and hasIgnoredAncestor(callerInst, state.ignoredRbxScripts, nil, flag(N.ignorePlayerMod, true)) then
			return true
		end
		return false
	end

	local function isRobloxIgnored(inst, rName, callerInst, cls, isExecutor)
		return remoteIgnored(inst, rName, cls) or callerIgnored(callerInst, isExecutor)
	end

	local function formatCallCode(entry)
		local lines = {}
		local dirTag = entry.dir == "S2C" and "S->C" or (entry.dir == "INT" and "INT" or (entry.dir == "ATTR" and "ATTR" or "C->S"))
		local blkTag = entry.blocked and " [BLOCKED]" or ""
		local execTag = entry.isExecutor and " [EXECUTOR]" or ""
		local actorTag = entry.isActor and " [ACTOR]" or ""
		table.insert(lines, string.format("-- [%s] %s:%s | %s%s%s%s", dirTag, entry.className, entry.method, entry.timeStr or "?", blkTag, execTag, actorTag))
		if entry.caller and entry.caller ~= "" then
			table.insert(lines, "-- Caller: " .. entry.caller)
		end
		if entry.sourceLine and entry.sourceLine ~= "" then
			table.insert(lines, string.format("-- Origin: %s (%s)", tostring(entry.funcName or "anon"), entry.sourceLine))
		elseif entry.funcName and entry.funcName ~= "" then
			table.insert(lines, "-- Function: " .. entry.funcName)
		end
		if entry.funcHash and entry.funcHash ~= "" then
			table.insert(lines, "-- Hash: " .. entry.funcHash)
		end
		if flag(N.showStack, false) and entry.stack and entry.stack ~= "" then
			table.insert(lines, "-- Stack:")
			for ln in string.gmatch(entry.stack, "[^\n]+") do
				table.insert(lines, "--   " .. ln)
			end
		end

		if entry.dir == "INT" then
			local fnName = entry.className == "ProximityPrompt" and "fireproximityprompt" or "fireclickdetector"
			table.insert(lines, string.format("%s(%s)", fnName, entry.path))
			return table.concat(lines, "\n")
		end

		if entry.dir == "ATTR" then
			local attrKey = serializeValue(entry.args[1], 1, {})
			local attrVal = serializeValue(entry.args[2], 1, {})
			table.insert(lines, string.format("%s:SetAttribute(%s, %s)", entry.path, attrKey, attrVal))
			return table.concat(lines, "\n")
		end

		local argCount = entry.args.n or #entry.args
		if argCount == 0 then
			if entry.dir == "S2C" then
				table.insert(lines, string.format("-- Received by %s.%s()", entry.path, entry.method))
			else
				table.insert(lines, string.format("%s:%s()", entry.path, entry.method))
			end
		else
			table.insert(lines, "local args = {")
			for i = 1, argCount do
				table.insert(lines, string.format("\t[%d] = %s,", i, serializeValue(entry.args[i], 1, {})))
			end
			table.insert(lines, "}")
			if entry.dir == "S2C" then
				table.insert(lines, string.format("-- Received by %s.%s(table.unpack(args, 1, %d))", entry.path, entry.method, argCount))
			else
				table.insert(lines, string.format("%s:%s(table.unpack(args, 1, %d))", entry.path, entry.method, argCount))
			end
		end

		if entry.returns then
			local rCount = entry.returns.n or #entry.returns
			table.insert(lines, "")
			if rCount == 0 then
				table.insert(lines, "-- Return: (void)")
			else
				table.insert(lines, "-- Return Values:")
				for i = 1, rCount do
					table.insert(lines, string.format("--   [%d] = %s", i, serializeValue(entry.returns[i], 1, {})))
				end
			end
		end

		return table.concat(lines, "\n")
	end

	local function safeFormat(entry)
		local ok, res = pcall(formatCallCode, entry)
		if ok then
			return res
		end
		return "-- error formatting call: " .. tostring(res)
	end

	local function getSelectedEntry()
		local label = state.selectedLabel or Zenith.values[N.list]
		return label and (state.byLabel[label] or state.groupByLabel[label]) or nil, label
	end

	local function renderSelected()
		local entry, label = getSelectedEntry()
		local out = Zenith.outputs and Zenith.outputs[N.log]
		if not out then
			return
		end
		if not entry then
			out.setText("-- Selecciona una llamada capturada en Remote List.")
			return
		end
		local txt = safeFormat(entry)
		local gCount = label and state.groupCount[label]
		if gCount and gCount > 1 then
			txt = string.format("-- Group: %d llamadas capturadas (mostrando #%03d)\n", gCount, entry.id) .. txt
		end
		out.setText(txt)
	end

	local function selectLabel(label)
		state.selectedLabel = label
		state.selectedGroupKey = label and state.groupKeyOf[label] or nil
	end

	-- selectNewest: jump selection to the newest visible entry (auto scroll)
	local function applyListFilter(selectNewest)
		local prevLabel, prevKey = state.selectedLabel, state.selectedGroupKey
		local filter = tostring(Zenith.values[N.filter] or "All")
		local needle = string.lower(tostring(Zenith.values[N.search] or ""))
		local grouped = flag(N.groupByRemote, false)
		table.clear(state.shownLabels)
		table.clear(state.groupByLabel)
		table.clear(state.groupCount)
		table.clear(state.groupKeyOf)
		local shownSet = {}

		local function passesFilter(e)
			if e.ignGen ~= state.ignoreGen then
				e.ign = isRobloxIgnored(e.remote, e.name, e.callerInst, e.className, e.isExecutor)
				e.ignGen = state.ignoreGen
			end
			if e.ign then
				return false
			end
			local classOk
			if filter == "All" then
				classOk = true
			elseif filter == "Interactions" then
				classOk = (e.dir == "INT")
			elseif filter == "Attributes" then
				classOk = (e.dir == "ATTR")
			else
				classOk = (FILTER_MATCH[filter] ~= nil and FILTER_MATCH[filter][e.className] == true)
			end
			if not classOk then
				return false
			end
			return needle == ""
				or string.find(string.lower(e.label), needle, 1, true) ~= nil
				or string.find(string.lower(e.path), needle, 1, true) ~= nil
		end

		local function tagOf(e)
			return e.dir == "S2C" and "S->C" or (e.dir == "INT" and "INT" or (e.dir == "ATTR" and "ATTR" or "C->S"))
		end

		if grouped then
			local order, groups = {}, {}
			for i = #state.entries, 1, -1 do
				local e = state.entries[i]
				if passesFilter(e) then
					local gKey = e.path .. "|" .. e.dir .. "|" .. e.method
					local g = groups[gKey]
					if not g then
						g = { latest = e, count = 1, key = gKey }
						groups[gKey] = g
						order[#order + 1] = g
					else
						g.count += 1
					end
				end
			end
			for _, g in ipairs(order) do
				local e = g.latest
				local dirTag = tagOf(e)
				local blkTag = e.blocked and "[BLK] " or ""
				local actTag = e.isActor and "[ACT] " or ""
				local gLabel = string.format("[%s] (x%d) %s%s%s | %s", dirTag, g.count, blkTag, actTag, e.method, e.name)
				if state.groupByLabel[gLabel] then
					gLabel = gLabel .. " #" .. e.id
				end
				state.groupByLabel[gLabel] = e
				state.groupCount[gLabel] = g.count
				state.groupKeyOf[gLabel] = g.key
				table.insert(state.shownLabels, gLabel)
				shownSet[gLabel] = true
			end
		else
			for i = #state.entries, 1, -1 do
				local e = state.entries[i]
				if passesFilter(e) then
					table.insert(state.shownLabels, e.label)
					shownSet[e.label] = true
				end
			end
		end

		Zenith.setItems(N.list, table.clone(state.shownLabels))

		local keep
		if selectNewest ~= true then
			if grouped and prevKey then
				for _, lbl in ipairs(state.shownLabels) do
					if state.groupKeyOf[lbl] == prevKey then
						keep = lbl
						break
					end
				end
			elseif prevLabel and shownSet[prevLabel] then
				keep = prevLabel
			end
		end
		local newest = state.shownLabels[1]
		selectLabel((selectNewest == true and newest) or keep or newest)
		Zenith.values[N.list] = state.selectedLabel
		renderSelected()
	end

	local function appendSpyFileLog(entry)
		if not flag(N.fileLogging, false) then
			return
		end
		local app = Zenith.capFn("appendfile")
		local wf = Zenith.capFn("writefile")
		if not app and not wf then
			return
		end
		local mk, isf = Zenith.capFn("makefolder"), Zenith.capFn("isfolder")
		for _, dir in ipairs({ "Zenith", "Zenith/Logs" }) do
			if mk and not (isf and isf(dir)) then
				pcall(mk, dir)
			end
		end
		if not state.logFilePath then
			local stamp = tostring(os.date("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S"))
			state.logFilePath = "Zenith/Logs/RSpy_" .. stamp .. ".log"
			if wf then
				pcall(wf, state.logFilePath, "-- Zenith RSpy Session Log\n")
			end
		end
		local block = safeFormat(entry) .. "\n\n"
		if app then
			pcall(app, state.logFilePath, block)
		elseif wf then
			local rf = Zenith.capFn("readfile")
			local prev = ""
			if rf then
				local okR, data = pcall(rf, state.logFilePath)
				if okR and type(data) == "string" then
					prev = data
				end
			end
			pcall(wf, state.logFilePath, prev .. block)
		end
	end

	local function addEntry(item)
		local rName = tostring(safeRead(item.remote, "Name") or "?")
		if isRobloxIgnored(item.remote, rName, item.callerInst, item.className, item.isExecutor) then
			return
		end

		if flag(N.autoIgnoreSpam, false) and (item.dir == "C2S" or item.dir == "S2C") and typeof(item.remote) == "Instance" and not item.blocked then
			local now = os.clock()
			local rec = state.spamTrack[item.remote]
			if not rec or (now - rec.t0) > 1 then
				rec = { t0 = now, count = 1 }
				state.spamTrack[item.remote] = rec
			else
				rec.count += 1
				if rec.count >= 30 and not isIgnoredName(rName) then
					local cur = tostring(Zenith.values[N.ignoreList] or "")
					local nextStr = (cur ~= "" and (cur .. ", " .. rName)) or rName
					setVal(N.ignoreList, nextStr)
					refreshIgnoreSet()
					Zenith.log("Info", "RSpy: auto-ignorado por spam -> " .. rName)
					return
				end
			end
		end

		state.seq += 1
		local path = instancePath(item.remote)
		local dirTag = item.dir == "S2C" and "S->C" or (item.dir == "INT" and "INT" or (item.dir == "ATTR" and "ATTR" or "C->S"))
		local blkTag = item.blocked and "[BLK] " or ""
		local actTag = item.isActor and "[ACT] " or ""
		local label = string.format("#%03d [%s] %s%s%s | %s", state.seq, dirTag, blkTag, actTag, item.method, rName)
		local callerStr = ""
		if item.callerInst then
			callerStr = instancePath(item.callerInst)
		end
		local entry = {
			id = state.seq,
			label = label,
			remote = item.remote,
			name = rName,
			className = item.className,
			method = item.method,
			dir = item.dir,
			path = path,
			args = item.args or { n = 0 },
			returns = item.returns,
			blocked = item.blocked,
			caller = callerStr,
			callerInst = item.callerInst,
			func = item.func,
			funcName = item.funcName,
			sourceLine = item.sourceLine,
			funcHash = item.funcHash,
			isExecutor = item.isExecutor == true,
			isActor = item.isActor == true,
			stack = item.stack,
			timeStr = item.timeStr,
		}
		table.insert(state.entries, entry)
		state.byLabel[label] = entry
		pcall(appendSpyFileLog, entry)
	end

	local function flushQueue()
		state.flushing = false
		if stopped or #state.queue == 0 then
			return
		end
		local batch = state.queue
		state.queue = {}

		for _, item in ipairs(batch) do
			local ok, err = pcall(addEntry, item)
			if not ok then
				Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: error registrando llamada: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end

		local maxCap = math.max(10, math.floor(num(N.maxLogs, 300)))
		local nEntries = #state.entries
		local excess = nEntries - maxCap
		if excess > 0 then
			for i = 1, excess do
				local oldest = state.entries[i]
				if oldest then
					state.byLabel[oldest.label] = nil
					if state.selectedLabel == oldest.label then
						state.selectedLabel = nil
					end
				end
			end
			table.move(state.entries, excess + 1, nEntries, 1)
			for i = nEntries - excess + 1, nEntries do
				state.entries[i] = nil
			end
		end

		setVal(N.calls, state.seq)
		applyListFilter(flag(N.autoScroll, true))
	end

	local function enqueue(item)
		if #state.queue >= 2000 then
			return -- flood protection
		end
		table.insert(state.queue, item)
		if not state.flushing then
			state.flushing = true
			task.defer(flushQueue)
		end
	end

	local metaCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local function extractFuncMeta(fn)
		if type(fn) ~= "function" then
			return nil, nil, nil
		end
		local hit = metaCache[fn]
		if hit then
			return hit[1], hit[2], hit[3]
		end
		local fnName, sourceLine, fnHash = nil, nil, nil
		if type(debug) == "table" and type(debug.info) == "function" then
			local okN, nm, src, ln = pcall(debug.info, fn, "nsl")
			if okN then
				if type(nm) == "string" and nm ~= "" then
					fnName = nm
				end
				if type(src) == "string" and src ~= "" then
					sourceLine = string.format("%s:%s", src, tostring(ln or "?"))
				end
			end
		end
		local isl = Zenith.capFn("islclosure")
		local gfh = Zenith.capFn("getfunctionhash")
		if gfh then
			local isLua = true
			if isl then
				local okL, r = pcall(isl, fn)
				isLua = okL and r == true
			end
			if isLua then
				local okH, h = pcall(gfh, fn)
				if okH and h ~= nil then
					fnHash = tostring(h)
				end
			end
		end
		metaCache[fn] = { fnName, sourceLine, fnHash }
		return fnName, sourceLine, fnHash
	end

	local OWN_SOURCE
	if type(debug) == "table" and type(debug.info) == "function" then
		local okOwn, srcOwn = pcall(debug.info, function() end, "s")
		OWN_SOURCE = okOwn and srcOwn or nil
	end

	local function captureCallerInfo()
		local getCallerFn = Zenith.capFn("getcallingscript")
		local callerInst = nil
		if getCallerFn then
			local ok, s = pcall(getCallerFn)
			if ok and typeof(s) == "Instance" then
				callerInst = s
			end
		end

		local ccFn = Zenith.capFn("checkcaller")
		local isExec = false
		if ccFn then
			local okC, resC = pcall(ccFn)
			if okC and resC == true then
				isExec = true
			end
		end

		local callerFn, fnName, sourceLine, fnHash = nil, nil, nil, nil
		if type(debug) == "table" and type(debug.info) == "function" then
			for i = 2, 14 do
				local okI, f, src, ln = pcall(debug.info, i, "fsl")
				if not okI or not f or not src then
					break
				end
				if src ~= "[C]" and (not OWN_SOURCE or src ~= OWN_SOURCE) then
					callerFn = f
					fnName, sourceLine, fnHash = extractFuncMeta(f)
					if not sourceLine then
						sourceLine = string.format("%s:%s", tostring(src), tostring(ln or "?"))
					end
					if not isExec then
						local isExFn = Zenith.capFn("isexecutorclosure")
						if isExFn then
							local okE, rE = pcall(isExFn, f)
							if okE and rE == true then
								isExec = true
							end
						end
					end
					break
				end
			end
		end

		local stack = nil
		if flag(N.showStack, false) and type(debug) == "table" and type(debug.traceback) == "function" then
			local ok, tb = pcall(debug.traceback, "", 3)
			if ok and type(tb) == "string" then
				stack = tb
			end
		end
		return callerInst, stack, isExec, callerFn, fnName, sourceLine, fnHash
	end

	local function timeStamp()
		local ok, s = pcall(os.date, "%H:%M:%S")
		return ok and s or "00:00:00"
	end

	local function callOrig(f, ...)
		return table.pack(f(...))
	end

	local MAIN_THREAD = {}

	-- rec = { active = bool, old = original function }
	local function handleOutbound(rec, self, method, ...)
		local origFn = rec.old
		if not rec.active or typeof(self) ~= "Instance" then
			return origFn(self, ...)
		end
		local canon = canonMethod(method)
		if not canon then
			return origFn(self, ...)
		end
		local thread = coroutine.running() or MAIN_THREAD
		if state.internal[thread] then
			return origFn(self, ...)
		end
		local cls = safeRead(self, "ClassName")
		if not cls or not OUTBOUND_CLASSES[canon][cls] then
			return origFn(self, ...)
		end

		local isBlocked = state.blocked[self] == true

		-- Re-entrancy on the SAME thread (namecall internally calling the hooked function)
		if state.depth[thread] then
			if isBlocked then
				return nil
			end
			return origFn(self, ...)
		end

		local shouldLog = flag(N.enabled, false) and flag(N.c2s, true)
		if shouldLog and isBindableClass(cls) and flag(N.ignoreBindables, true) then
			shouldLog = false
		end
		if not shouldLog and not isBlocked then
			return origFn(self, ...)
		end

		local item
		if shouldLog and not isBlocked and remoteIgnored(self, safeRead(self, "Name"), cls) then
			shouldLog = false
		end
		if shouldLog then
			local callerInst, stack, isExec, callerFn, fnName, sourceLine, fnHash = captureCallerInfo()
			if not isBlocked and callerIgnored(callerInst, isExec) then
				shouldLog = false
			else
				item = {
					remote = self,
					className = cls,
					method = canon,
					dir = "C2S",
					args = table.pack(...),
					blocked = isBlocked,
					callerInst = callerInst,
					func = callerFn,
					funcName = fnName,
					sourceLine = sourceLine,
					funcHash = fnHash,
					isExecutor = isExec,
					stack = stack,
					timeStr = timeStamp(),
				}
			end
		end
		if not shouldLog and not isBlocked then
			return origFn(self, ...)
		end

		if isBlocked then
			if item then
				enqueue(item)
			end
			return nil
		end

		local wantRet = shouldLog and flag(N.logReturn, false) and (canon == "InvokeServer" or canon == "Invoke")
		if shouldLog and not wantRet then
			enqueue(item) -- log before the call so hanging invokes are still visible
		end

		state.depth[thread] = true
		local ok, res = pcall(callOrig, origFn, self, ...)
		state.depth[thread] = nil

		if wantRet then
			if ok then
				item.returns = res
			end
			enqueue(item)
		end
		if not ok then
			error(res, 0)
		end
		return table.unpack(res, 1, res.n)
	end

	----------------------------------------------------------------
	-- OnClientInvoke / OnInvoke (callback members)
	----------------------------------------------------------------
	local CALLBACK_SOURCES = {
		RemoteFunction = "OnClientInvoke",
		BindableFunction = "OnInvoke",
	}
	local function getCallbackReader()
		return Zenith.capFn("getcallbackvalue") or Zenith.capFn("getcallbackmember")
	end

	local function readCallback(inst, prop)
		local fnReader = getCallbackReader()
		if not fnReader then
			return nil
		end
		local ok, fn = pcall(fnReader, inst, prop)
		if ok and type(fn) == "function" then
			return fn
		end
		return nil
	end

	-- Assign a callback without our own __newindex hook intercepting it
	local function setCallbackRaw(inst, prop, fn)
		local thread = coroutine.running() or MAIN_THREAD
		local prevFlag = state.internal[thread]
		state.internal[thread] = true
		local ok = pcall(function()
			inst[prop] = fn
		end)
		state.internal[thread] = prevFlag
		return ok
	end

	local function resolveScriptOrigin(thread, fn)
		local gsft = Zenith.capFn("getscriptfromthread")
		if gsft and thread then
			local ok, s = pcall(gsft, thread)
			if ok and typeof(s) == "Instance" then
				return s
			end
		end
		if type(fn) == "function" and type(getfenv) == "function" then
			local okE, env = pcall(getfenv, fn)
			if okE and type(env) == "table" then
				local s = rawget(env, "script")
				if typeof(s) == "Instance" then
					return s
				end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function checkFnIsExecutor(fn)
		local isEx = Zenith.capFn("isexecutorclosure")
		if isEx and type(fn) == "function" then
			local ok, res = pcall(isEx, fn)
			if ok and res == true then
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function makeCallbackWrapper(inst, cls, prop, origFn)
		local prev = state.wrappedCallbacks[inst]
		if prev and prev.rec.active then
			if origFn == prev.wrapper or origFn == prev.orig then
				return prev.wrapper
			end
			prev.rec.active = false -- replaced by a new callback; old wrapper becomes pass-through
		end

		local rec = { active = true, orig = origFn }
		local function wrapper(...)
			if not rec.active or stopped or not (flag(N.enabled, false) and flag(N.s2c, true)) then
				return rec.orig(...)
			end
			local origin = resolveScriptOrigin(coroutine.running(), rec.orig)
			local isExec = checkFnIsExecutor(rec.orig)
			if isRobloxIgnored(inst, safeRead(inst, "Name"), origin, cls, isExec) then
				return rec.orig(...)
			end

			local fnName, sourceLine, fnHash = extractFuncMeta(rec.orig)
			local item = {
				remote = inst,
				className = cls,
				method = prop,
				dir = "S2C",
				args = table.pack(...),
				blocked = false,
				callerInst = origin,
				func = rec.orig,
				funcName = fnName,
				sourceLine = sourceLine,
				funcHash = fnHash,
				isExecutor = isExec,
				timeStr = timeStamp(),
			}

			local wantRet = flag(N.logReturn, false)
			if not wantRet then
				enqueue(item)
			end

			local ok, res = pcall(callOrig, rec.orig, ...)
			if wantRet then
				if ok then
					item.returns = res
				end
				enqueue(item)
			end
			if not ok then
				error(res, 0)
			end
			return table.unpack(res, 1, res.n)
		end

		local setHidden = Zenith.capFn("setstackhidden")
		if setHidden then
			pcall(setHidden, wrapper, true)
		end
		local wrapC = Zenith.capFn("newcclosure")
		local finalWrapper = wrapC and wrapC(wrapper) or wrapper
		state.wrappedCallbacks[inst] = { prop = prop, orig = origFn, wrapper = finalWrapper, rec = rec }
		return finalWrapper
	end

	local function attachCallbackInstance(inst, cls)
		local cbProp = CALLBACK_SOURCES[cls]
		if not cbProp or state.wrappedCallbacks[inst] then
			return
		end
		if isRobloxIgnored(inst, safeRead(inst, "Name"), nil, cls) then
			return
		end
		local origFn = readCallback(inst, cbProp)
		if not origFn then
			return
		end
		local wrapped = makeCallbackWrapper(inst, cls, cbProp, origFn)
		if not setCallbackRaw(inst, cbProp, wrapped) then
			local e = state.wrappedCallbacks[inst]
			if e then
				e.rec.active = false
			end
			state.wrappedCallbacks[inst] = nil
		end
	end

	local function restoreWrappedCallbacks()
		local list = {}
		for inst, info in pairs(state.wrappedCallbacks) do
			list[#list + 1] = { inst, info }
		end
		state.wrappedCallbacks = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
		local hasReader = getCallbackReader() ~= nil
		for _, p in ipairs(list) do
			local inst, info = p[1], p[2]
			info.rec.active = false
			local shouldRestore = true
			if hasReader then
				-- only restore if the game hasn't replaced/cleared it since
				shouldRestore = readCallback(inst, info.prop) == info.wrapper
			end
			if shouldRestore then
				setCallbackRaw(inst, info.prop, info.orig)
			end
		end
	end

	local function handleNewindex(rec, self, key, value)
		local oldFn = rec.old
		-- cheapest checks first: this runs on EVERY property write in the game
		if (key ~= "OnClientInvoke" and key ~= "OnInvoke") or not rec.active or stopped then
			return oldFn(self, key, value)
		end
		if typeof(self) ~= "Instance" or state.internal[coroutine.running() or MAIN_THREAD] then
			return oldFn(self, key, value)
		end
		local cls = safeRead(self, "ClassName")
		if not cls or CALLBACK_SOURCES[cls] ~= key then
			return oldFn(self, key, value)
		end

		if type(value) == "function" and flag(N.enabled, false) and flag(N.s2c, true)
			and not isRobloxIgnored(self, safeRead(self, "Name"), nil, cls) then
			return oldFn(self, key, (makeCallbackWrapper(self, cls, key, value)))
		end

		local existing = state.wrappedCallbacks[self]
		if existing then
			existing.rec.active = false
			state.wrappedCallbacks[self] = nil
		end
		return oldFn(self, key, value)
	end

	local function installNewindexHook(wrapC)
		if state.newindexRec then
			return true
		end
		local hookMM = Zenith.capFn("hookmetamethod")

		if hookMM then
			local rec = { active = true, old = nil }
			local ok, old = pcall(hookMM, game, "__newindex", wrapC(function(self, key, value)
				return handleNewindex(rec, self, key, value)
			end))
			if ok and type(old) == "function" then
				rec.old = old
				state.oldNewindex = old
				state.newindexRec = rec
				return true
			end
			rec.active = false
		end

		if Zenith.cap("getrawmetatable") then
			local getRMT = Zenith.capFn("getrawmetatable")
			local setRO = typeof(setreadonly) == "function" and setreadonly or nil
			local ok, mt = pcall(getRMT, game)
			if ok and type(mt) == "table" and type(mt.__newindex) == "function" then
				local rec = { active = true, old = mt.__newindex }
				if setRO then pcall(setRO, mt, false) end
				local okSet = pcall(function()
					mt.__newindex = wrapC(function(self, key, value)
						return handleNewindex(rec, self, key, value)
					end)
				end)
				if setRO then pcall(setRO, mt, true) end
				if okSet then
					state.oldNewindex = rec.old
					state.newindexRec = rec
					return true
				end
				rec.active = false
			end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function uninstallNewindexHook()
		if state.newindexRec then
			state.newindexRec.active = false -- wrapper becomes pass-through immediately
		end
		restoreWrappedCallbacks()
		if state.oldNewindex then
			local hookMM = Zenith.capFn("hookmetamethod")
			if hookMM then
				pcall(hookMM, game, "__newindex", state.oldNewindex)
			elseif Zenith.cap("getrawmetatable") then
				local getRMT = Zenith.capFn("getrawmetatable")
				local setRO = typeof(setreadonly) == "function" and setreadonly or nil
				local ok, mt = pcall(getRMT, game)
				if ok and type(mt) == "table" then
					if setRO then pcall(setRO, mt, false) end
					pcall(function()
						mt.__newindex = state.oldNewindex
					end)
					if setRO then pcall(setRO, mt, true) end
				end
			end
			state.oldNewindex = nil
		end
		state.newindexRec = nil
	end

	local function attachS2CInstance(inst)
		if typeof(inst) ~= "Instance" then
			return
		end
		if inst == state.actorChannel then
			return
		end
		local cls = safeRead(inst, "ClassName")
		if cls and CALLBACK_SOURCES[cls] then
			return attachCallbackInstance(inst, cls)
		end
		if state.s2cHooked[inst] then
			return
		end
		local sigName = cls and S2C_SOURCES[cls]
		if not sigName then
			return
		end
		if isRobloxIgnored(inst, safeRead(inst, "Name"), nil, cls) then
			return
		end
		local sig = safeRead(inst, sigName)
		if not sig then
			return
		end
		state.signalMapping[sig] = inst
		local spyFn = function(...)
			if not (flag(N.enabled, false) and flag(N.s2c, true)) then
				return
			end
			local rName = safeRead(inst, "Name")
			local getConns = Zenith.capFn("getconnections")
			local loggedFromConn = false
			if getConns then
				local okC, conns = pcall(getConns, sig)
				if okC and type(conns) == "table" then
					for _, conn in ipairs(conns) do
						local fn = safeRead(conn, "Function")
						if not safeRead(conn, "ForeignState") and not (fn and state.spyConnFns[fn]) then
							local origin = resolveScriptOrigin(safeRead(conn, "Thread"), fn)
							local isExec = checkFnIsExecutor(fn)
							local fnName, sourceLine, fnHash = extractFuncMeta(fn)
							loggedFromConn = true
							if not isRobloxIgnored(inst, rName, origin, cls, isExec) then
								enqueue({
									remote = inst,
									className = cls,
									method = sigName,
									dir = "S2C",
									args = table.pack(...),
									blocked = false,
									callerInst = origin,
									func = fn,
									funcName = fnName,
									sourceLine = sourceLine,
									funcHash = fnHash,
									isExecutor = isExec,
									timeStr = timeStamp(),
								})
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
			if not loggedFromConn and not isRobloxIgnored(inst, rName, nil, cls) then
				enqueue({
					remote = inst,
					className = cls,
					method = sigName,
					dir = "S2C",
					args = table.pack(...),
					blocked = false,
					timeStr = timeStamp(),
				})
			end
		end
		state.spyConnFns[spyFn] = true
		local ok, conn = pcall(function()
			return sig:Connect(spyFn)
		end)
		if ok and conn then
			state.s2cHooked[inst] = true
			table.insert(state.s2cConns, conn)
		else
			state.spyConnFns[spyFn] = nil
		end
	end

	local function disconnectS2C()
		for i = #state.s2cConns, 1, -1 do
			local c = state.s2cConns[i]
			pcall(function()
				c:Disconnect()
			end)
			state.s2cConns[i] = nil
		end
		if state.descendantConn then
			pcall(function()
				state.descendantConn:Disconnect()
			end)
			state.descendantConn = nil
		end
		state.s2cHooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	end

	-- enabledOv / s2cOv: values coming straight from the UI callback (avoid stale reads)
	local function syncS2C(enabledOv, s2cOv)
		disconnectS2C()
		local en, s2c = enabledOv, s2cOv
		if en == nil then
			en = flag(N.enabled, false)
		end
		if s2c == nil then
			s2c = flag(N.s2c, true)
		end
		if not (en and s2c) then
			restoreWrappedCallbacks()
			return
		end
		local okD, desc = pcall(game.GetDescendants, game)
		if okD and type(desc) == "table" then
			for _, inst in ipairs(desc) do
				attachS2CInstance(inst)
			end
		end
		local getNil = Zenith.cap("getnilinstances") and Zenith.capFn("getnilinstances")
		if getNil then
			local okn, nils = pcall(getNil)
			if okn and type(nils) == "table" then
				for _, inst in ipairs(nils) do
					attachS2CInstance(inst)
				end
			end
		end
		local okC, conn = pcall(function()
			return game.DescendantAdded:Connect(attachS2CInstance)
		end)
		if okC and conn then
			state.descendantConn = conn
		end
	end

	local SIGNAL_CONN_KEYS = {
		Connect = true,
		ConnectParallel = true,
		Once = true,
		connect = true,
		connectParallel = true,
	}

	local function installSignalHook(wrapC)
		if state.signalHook then
			return true
		end
		local hookFn = Zenith.capFn("hookfunction")
		local getRMT = Zenith.capFn("getrawmetatable")
		local getConns = Zenith.capFn("getconnections")
		if not hookFn or not getRMT or not getConns then
			return false
		end
		local okP, part = pcall(Instance.new, "Part")
		if not okP or not part then
			return false
		end
		local sig = safeRead(part, "Touched")
		local okM, sigMt = pcall(getRMT, sig)
		pcall(function()
			part:Destroy()
		end)
		if not okM or type(sigMt) ~= "table" or type(sigMt.__index) ~= "function" then
			return false
		end
		local targetIdx = sigMt.__index
		local rec = { active = true, target = targetIdx, old = nil }
		local okH, old = pcall(hookFn, targetIdx, wrapC(function(self, key)
			local res = rec.old(self, key)
			if not rec.active or stopped or not SIGNAL_CONN_KEYS[key] or type(res) ~= "function" then
				return res
			end
			local inst = state.signalMapping[self]
			if not inst or not state.blocked[inst] then
				return res
			end
			return wrapC(function(s, callback)
				local ret = table.pack(res(s, callback))
				if state.blocked[inst] then
					local cls = safeRead(inst, "ClassName")
					local sigName = cls and S2C_SOURCES[cls]
					if sigName then
						local okC, conns = pcall(getConns, safeRead(inst, sigName))
						if okC and type(conns) == "table" then
							for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
								if not safeRead(c, "ForeignState") and safeRead(c, "Function") == callback and type(c.Disable) == "function" then
									pcall(c.Disable, c)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
				return table.unpack(ret, 1, ret.n)
			end)
		end))
		if okH and type(old) == "function" then
			rec.old = old
			state.signalHook = rec
			return true
		end
		rec.active = false
		return false
	end

	local ACTOR_HOOK_SRC = [[
		local chId, actorInst = ...
		local ch = get_comm_channel(chId)
		if not ch or _G.__ZENITH_ACTOR_ACTIVE then return end
		_G.__ZENITH_ACTOR_ACTIVE = true
		local active = true
		ch.Event:Connect(function(cmd)
			if cmd == "Unload" then active = false _G.__ZENITH_ACTOR_ACTIVE = nil end
		end)
		if typeof(hookmetamethod) == "function" and typeof(getnamecallmethod) == "function" then
			local allowed = { RemoteEvent = true, UnreliableRemoteEvent = true, RemoteFunction = true, BindableEvent = true, BindableFunction = true }
			local methods = { fireserver = "FireServer", invokeserver = "InvokeServer", fire = "Fire", invoke = "Invoke" }
			local wrap = typeof(newcclosure) == "function" and newcclosure or function(f) return f end
			local oldNC
			oldNC = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", wrap(function(self, ...)
				if active and typeof(self) == "Instance" and self ~= ch then
					local m = methods[string.lower(tostring(getnamecallmethod() or ""))]
					local cls = m and self.ClassName
					if m and allowed[cls] then
						local caller = typeof(getcallingscript) == "function" and getcallingscript() or nil
						local isEx = typeof(checkcaller) == "function" and checkcaller() or false
						local fnName, srcLine = nil, nil
						if type(debug) == "table" and type(debug.info) == "function" then
							local f, s, l = debug.info(3, "fsl")
							if f and s ~= "[C]" then
								fnName = debug.info(f, "n")
								srcLine = tostring(s) .. ":" .. tostring(l or "?")
							end
						end
						pcall(ch.Fire, ch, "ActorCall", self, "C2S", cls, m, table.pack(...), caller, fnName, srcLine, isEx)
					end
				end
				return oldNC(self, ...)
			end))
		end
	]]

	local function uninstallActorHooks()
		local ch = state.actorChannel
		if ch then
			pcall(ch.Fire, ch, "Unload")
			task.delay(1, function()
				pcall(ch.Destroy, ch)
			end)
			state.actorChannel = nil
		end
		for i = #state.actorConns, 1, -1 do
			local c = state.actorConns[i]
			pcall(function()
				c:Disconnect()
			end)
			state.actorConns[i] = nil
		end
		state.actorHooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	end

	local function installActorHooks()
		if state.actorChannel or not flag(N.logActors, true) then
			return false
		end
		local runActor = Zenith.capFn("run_on_actor")
		local getActors = Zenith.capFn("getactors")
		local mkChan = Zenith.capFn("create_comm_channel")
		if not runActor or not getActors or not mkChan then
			return false
		end
		local okC, chId, ch = pcall(mkChan)
		if not okC or not chId or not ch then
			return false
		end
		state.actorChannel = ch
		local evConn = ch.Event:Connect(function(kind, inst, dir, cls, method, argsPack, callerInst, fnName, srcLine, isExec)
			if kind ~= "ActorCall" or stopped or not (flag(N.enabled, false) and flag(N.logActors, true)) then
				return
			end
			if typeof(inst) ~= "Instance" then
				return
			end
			if isRobloxIgnored(inst, safeRead(inst, "Name"), callerInst, cls, isExec == true) then
				return
			end
			enqueue({
				remote = inst,
				className = cls or safeRead(inst, "ClassName") or "RemoteEvent",
				method = method or "FireServer",
				dir = dir or "C2S",
				args = type(argsPack) == "table" and argsPack or { n = 0 },
				blocked = state.blocked[inst] == true,
				callerInst = callerInst,
				funcName = fnName,
				sourceLine = srcLine,
				isExecutor = isExec == true,
				isActor = true,
				timeStr = timeStamp(),
			})
		end)
		table.insert(state.actorConns, evConn)

		local function hookOneActor(act)
			if typeof(act) ~= "Instance" or state.actorHooked[act] then
				return
			end
			state.actorHooked[act] = true
			task.spawn(function()
				pcall(runActor, act, ACTOR_HOOK_SRC, chId, act)
			end)
		end

		local okA, actors = pcall(getActors)
		if okA and type(actors) == "table" then
			for _, a in ipairs(actors) do
				hookOneActor(a)
			end
		end
		local okD, dConn = pcall(function()
			return game.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(inst)
				if typeof(inst) == "Instance" then
					local okI, isA = pcall(inst.IsA, inst, "Actor")
					if okI and isA then
						hookOneActor(inst)
					end
				end
			end)
		end)
		if okD and dConn then
			table.insert(state.actorConns, dConn)
		end
		return true
	end

	local function installHooks()
		if state.installed then
			return true
		end
		local wrapC = Zenith.capFn("newcclosure") or function(f) return f end
		local getNCM = Zenith.capFn("getnamecallmethod")
		local hookMM = Zenith.capFn("hookmetamethod")
		local hookFn = Zenith.capFn("hookfunction")
		local othHook = flag(N.preferOth, true) and Zenith.capFn("oth_hook") or nil

		if hookMM and getNCM then
			local rec = { active = true, old = nil }
			local ok, old = pcall(hookMM, game, "__namecall", wrapC(function(self, ...)
				local method = getNCM()
				return handleOutbound(rec, self, method, ...)
			end))
			if ok and type(old) == "function" then
				rec.old = old
				state.oldNamecall = old
				state.namecallRec = rec
				state.namecallVia = "hookmm"
				state.installed = true
			else
				rec.active = false
				Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: hookmetamethod fallo (" .. tostring(old) .. ")")
			end
		end
		if not state.namecallRec and Zenith.cap("getrawmetatable") and getNCM then
			local getRMT = Zenith.capFn("getrawmetatable")
			local setRO = typeof(setreadonly) == "function" and setreadonly or nil
			local ok, mt = pcall(getRMT, game)
			if ok and type(mt) == "table" and type(mt.__namecall) == "function" then
				local rec = { active = true, old = mt.__namecall }
				if setRO then pcall(setRO, mt, false) end
				local okSet = pcall(function()
					mt.__namecall = wrapC(function(self, ...)
						local method = getNCM()
						return handleOutbound(rec, self, method, ...)
					end)
				end)
				if setRO then pcall(setRO, mt, true) end
				if okSet then
					state.oldNamecall = rec.old
					state.namecallRec = rec
					state.namecallVia = "rawmt"
					state.installed = true
				else
					rec.active = false
					Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: no se pudo escribir __namecall")
				end
			end
		end

		if hookFn or othHook then
			local specs = {
				{ "RemoteEvent", "FireServer" },
				{ "UnreliableRemoteEvent", "FireServer" },
				{ "RemoteFunction", "InvokeServer" },
				{ "BindableEvent", "Fire" },
				{ "BindableFunction", "Invoke" },
			}
			local hookedTargets = {}
			for _, sp in ipairs(specs) do
				local cls, method = sp[1], sp[2]
				local okI, dummy = pcall(Instance.new, cls)
				if okI and dummy then
					local fn = safeRead(dummy, method)
					if type(fn) == "function" and not hookedTargets[fn] then
						hookedTargets[fn] = true
						local record = { active = true, target = fn, old = nil, usedOth = false }
						local hooked = false
						if othHook then
							local okO, oldO = pcall(othHook, fn, wrapC(function(self, ...)
								return handleOutbound(record, self, method, ...)
							end))
							if okO and type(oldO) == "function" then
								record.old = oldO
								record.usedOth = true
								table.insert(state.directHooks, record)
								state.installed = true
								hooked = true
							end
						end
						if not hooked and hookFn then
							local okH, old = pcall(hookFn, fn, wrapC(function(self, ...)
								return handleOutbound(record, self, method, ...)
							end))
							if okH and type(old) == "function" then
								record.old = old
								table.insert(state.directHooks, record)
								state.installed = true
							else
								record.active = false
							end
						elseif not hooked then
							record.active = false
						end
					end
					pcall(function()
						dummy:Destroy()
					end)
				end
			end
		end

		if installNewindexHook(wrapC) then
			state.installed = true
		end
		installSignalHook(wrapC)
		installActorHooks()

		return state.installed
	end

	local function uninstallInteractionHooks()
		for i = #state.interactionConns, 1, -1 do
			local c = state.interactionConns[i]
			pcall(function()
				c:Disconnect()
			end)
			state.interactionConns[i] = nil
		end
		state.interactionHooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	end

	local function installInteractionHooks()
		uninstallInteractionHooks()
		if stopped or not (flag(N.enabled, false) and flag(N.logInteractions, false)) then
			return false
		end

		local okP, pps = pcall(game.GetService, game, "ProximityPromptService")
		if okP and pps and pps.PromptTriggered then
			local okC, conn = pcall(function()
				return pps.PromptTriggered:Connect(function(prompt, plr)
					if stopped or not (flag(N.enabled, false) and flag(N.logInteractions, false)) then
						return
					end
					if plr and localPlayer and plr ~= localPlayer then
						return
					end
					if typeof(prompt) ~= "Instance" then
						return
					end
					enqueue({
						remote = prompt,
						className = "ProximityPrompt",
						method = "FireProximityPrompt",
						dir = "INT",
						args = { n = 0 },
						timeStr = timeStamp(),
					})
				end)
			end)
			if okC and conn then
				table.insert(state.interactionConns, conn)
			end
		end

		local function watchClickDetector(cd)
			if typeof(cd) ~= "Instance" or state.interactionHooked[cd] then
				return
			end
			local okIs, isCD = pcall(cd.IsA, cd, "ClickDetector")
			if not okIs or not isCD then
				return
			end
			state.interactionHooked[cd] = true
			local okC, conn = pcall(function()
				return cd.MouseClick:Connect(function(plr)
					if stopped or not (flag(N.enabled, false) and flag(N.logInteractions, false)) then
						return
					end
					if plr and localPlayer and plr ~= localPlayer then
						return
					end
					enqueue({
						remote = cd,
						className = "ClickDetector",
						method = "FireClickDetector",
						dir = "INT",
						args = { n = 0 },
						timeStr = timeStamp(),
					})
				end)
			end)
			if okC and conn then
				table.insert(state.interactionConns, conn)
			end
		end

		local okW, ws = pcall(game.GetService, game, "Workspace")
		if okW and ws then
			local okD, desc = pcall(ws.GetDescendants, ws)
			if okD and type(desc) == "table" then
				for _, d in ipairs(desc) do
					watchClickDetector(d)
				end
			end
			local okA, addConn = pcall(function()
				return ws.DescendantAdded:Connect(watchClickDetector)
			end)
			if okA and addConn then
				table.insert(state.interactionConns, addConn)
			end
		end
		return #state.interactionConns > 0
	end

	local function uninstallAttributeHooks()
		for i = #state.attrConns, 1, -1 do
			local c = state.attrConns[i]
			pcall(function()
				c:Disconnect()
			end)
			state.attrConns[i] = nil
		end
		state.attrHooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	end

	local function installAttributeHooks()
		uninstallAttributeHooks()
		if stopped or not (flag(N.enabled, false) and flag(N.logAttributes, false)) then
			return false
		end

		local watchedCount = 0
		local function watchAttr(inst)
			if typeof(inst) ~= "Instance" or state.attrHooked[inst] or watchedCount >= 4000 then
				return
			end
			if not inst.AttributeChanged then
				return
			end
			state.attrHooked[inst] = true
			watchedCount += 1
			local okC, conn = pcall(function()
				return inst.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(attrName)
					if stopped or not (flag(N.enabled, false) and flag(N.logAttributes, false)) then
						return
					end
					local okV, val = pcall(inst.GetAttribute, inst, attrName)
					enqueue({
						remote = inst,
						className = safeRead(inst, "ClassName") or "Instance",
						method = "SetAttribute",
						dir = "ATTR",
						args = { n = 2, attrName, okV and val or nil },
						timeStr = timeStamp(),
					})
				end)
			end)
			if okC and conn then
				table.insert(state.attrConns, conn)
			end
		end

		if localPlayer then
			watchAttr(localPlayer)
			if localPlayer.Character then
				watchAttr(localPlayer.Character)
			end
		end
		for _, svcName in ipairs({ "Workspace", "ReplicatedStorage" }) do
			local okS, svc = pcall(game.GetService, game, svcName)
			if okS and svc then
				watchAttr(svc)
				local okD, desc = pcall(svc.GetDescendants, svc)
				if okD and type(desc) == "table" then
					for _, d in ipairs(desc) do
						watchAttr(d)
					end
				end
				local okA, addConn = pcall(function()
					return svc.DescendantAdded:Connect(watchAttr)
				end)
				if okA and addConn then
					table.insert(state.attrConns, addConn)
				end
			end
		end
		return #state.attrConns > 0
	end

	local function uninstallHooks()
		-- 1) Deactivate first: every live wrapper becomes a pure pass-through immediately
		if state.namecallRec then
			state.namecallRec.active = false
		end
		if state.signalHook then
			state.signalHook.active = false
		end
		for _, rec in ipairs(state.directHooks) do
			rec.active = false
		end

		-- 2) Disconnect S2C / callbacks / newindex / actors / interactions / attributes
		disconnectS2C()
		restoreWrappedCallbacks()
		uninstallNewindexHook()
		uninstallActorHooks()
		uninstallInteractionHooks()
		uninstallAttributeHooks()

		local hookMM = Zenith.capFn("hookmetamethod")
		if state.oldNamecall then
			if state.namecallVia == "hookmm" and hookMM then
				pcall(hookMM, game, "__namecall", state.oldNamecall)
			elseif Zenith.cap("getrawmetatable") then
				local getRMT = Zenith.capFn("getrawmetatable")
				local setRO = typeof(setreadonly) == "function" and setreadonly or nil
				local ok, mt = pcall(getRMT, game)
				if ok and type(mt) == "table" then
					if setRO then pcall(setRO, mt, false) end
					pcall(function()
						mt.__namecall = state.oldNamecall
					end)
					if setRO then pcall(setRO, mt, true) end
				end
			end
			state.oldNamecall = nil
			state.namecallVia = nil
		end
		state.namecallRec = nil

		local hookFn = Zenith.capFn("hookfunction")
		if state.signalHook and state.signalHook.target and state.signalHook.old then
			local restoredSig = false
			if typeof(restorefunction) == "function" then
				restoredSig = pcall(restorefunction, state.signalHook.target)
			end
			if not restoredSig and hookFn then
				pcall(hookFn, state.signalHook.target, state.signalHook.old)
			end
		end
		state.signalHook = nil

		local othUnhook = Zenith.capFn("oth_unhook")
		for _, rec in ipairs(state.directHooks) do
			if rec.target and rec.old then
				local restored = false
				if rec.usedOth and othUnhook then
					restored = pcall(othUnhook, rec.target)
				end
				if not restored and typeof(restorefunction) == "function" then
					restored = pcall(restorefunction, rec.target)
				end
				if not restored and hookFn then
					pcall(hookFn, rec.target, rec.old)
				end
			end
		end
		table.clear(state.directHooks)
		state.installed = false
	end

	Zenith.RSpy = state

	Zenith.register("RSpy", {
		requires = {},
		optional = {
			"hookmetamethod", "hookfunction", "getrawmetatable", "getnamecallmethod", "newcclosure",
			"getcallingscript", "getnilinstances", "getcallbackvalue", "getcallbackmember", "setclipboard",
			"getconnections", "getscriptfromthread", "getfunctionhash", "checkcaller", "isexecutorclosure",
			"islclosure", "setstackhidden", "run_on_actor", "getactors", "create_comm_channel",
			"oth_hook", "oth_unhook", "fireproximityprompt", "fireclickdetector",
			"writefile", "appendfile", "makefolder", "isfolder",
		},
		init = function()
			stopped = false
			Zenith.RSpy = state
			refreshIgnoreSet()

			local function onEnabled(on)
				if on then
					local ok = installHooks()
					syncS2C(true, nil)
					installInteractionHooks()
					installAttributeHooks()
					Zenith.log(ok and "Info" or "Warn", ok and "RSpy: captura en vivo activada" or "RSpy: sin soporte de hooks (__namecall/hookfunction), solo OnClientEvent")
				else
					disconnectS2C()
					uninstallInteractionHooks()
					uninstallAttributeHooks()
					restoreWrappedCallbacks()
					Zenith.log("Info", "RSpy: captura pausada")
				end
			end

			hookUI(N.enabled, function(on)
				onEnabled(on == true)
			end)

			hookUI(N.s2c, function(v)
				syncS2C(nil, v == true)
			end)

			hookUI(N.list, function(label)
				selectLabel(label)
				renderSelected()
			end)

			hookUI(N.filter, function() applyListFilter() end)
			hookUI(N.search, function() applyListFilter() end)
			hookUI(N.ignoreList, function()
				refreshIgnoreSet()
				applyListFilter()
			end)
			for _, fName in ipairs({ N.ignoreInternals, N.ignoreBindables, N.ignorePlayerMod, N.ignoreServices, N.ignoreRbxScripts, N.ignoreRbxRemotes, N.ignoreExecutor }) do
				hookUI(fName, function()
					refreshIgnoreSet()
					applyListFilter()
					if flag(N.enabled, false) and flag(N.s2c, true) then
						syncS2C(true, true)
					end
				end)
			end
			hookUI(N.logActors, function(on)
				if on and flag(N.enabled, false) then
					installActorHooks()
				elseif not on then
					uninstallActorHooks()
				end
			end)
			hookUI(N.logInteractions, function(on)
				if on and flag(N.enabled, false) then
					installInteractionHooks()
				elseif not on then
					uninstallInteractionHooks()
				end
			end)
			hookUI(N.logAttributes, function(on)
				if on and flag(N.enabled, false) then
					installAttributeHooks()
				elseif not on then
					uninstallAttributeHooks()
				end
			end)
			hookUI(N.fullSerialize, function() renderSelected() end)
			hookUI(N.showStack, function() renderSelected() end)
			hookUI(N.groupByRemote, function() applyListFilter(true) end)

			Zenith.actions[N.clear] = function()
				state.seq = 0
				table.clear(state.entries)
				table.clear(state.byLabel)
				table.clear(state.groupByLabel)
				table.clear(state.groupCount)
				table.clear(state.groupKeyOf)
				table.clear(state.shownLabels)
				table.clear(state.queue)
				selectLabel(nil)
				Zenith.values[N.list] = nil
				setVal(N.calls, 0)
				Zenith.setItems(N.list, {})
				renderSelected()
				Zenith.log("Info", "RSpy: logs limpiados")
			end

			Zenith.actions[N.copyCode] = function()
				local entry = getSelectedEntry()
				if not entry then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: selecciona una llamada primero")
				end
				local ok = Zenith.copy(safeFormat(entry))
				Zenith.log(ok and "Info" or "Warn", ok and "RSpy: codigo copiado" or "Clipboard no soportado")
			end

			Zenith.actions[N.copyPath] = function()
				local entry = getSelectedEntry()
				if not entry then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: selecciona una llamada primero")
				end
				local ok = Zenith.copy(entry.path)
				Zenith.log(ok and "Info" or "Warn", ok and "RSpy: ruta copiada" or "Clipboard no soportado")
			end

			Zenith.actions[N.decompileCaller] = function()
				local entry = getSelectedEntry()
				if not entry then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: selecciona una llamada primero")
				end
				local callerInst = entry.callerInst
				if not callerInst and type(entry.func) == "function" then
					callerInst = resolveScriptOrigin(nil, entry.func)
				end
				if not callerInst then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: esta llamada no tiene Script caller identificable")
				end
				if Zenith.decompileInstance then
					Zenith.decompileInstance(callerInst)
				else
					Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: modulo Decompiler no cargado")
				end
			end

			Zenith.actions[N.copyFnInfo] = function()
				local entry = getSelectedEntry()
				if not entry then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: selecciona una llamada primero")
				end
				local lines = {
					"-- Zenith Function Info",
					"-- Remote     : " .. tostring(entry.path),
					"-- Caller     : " .. tostring(entry.caller ~= "" and entry.caller or "Unknown"),
					"-- Function   : " .. tostring(entry.funcName or "Anonymous"),
					"-- Source     : " .. tostring(entry.sourceLine or "Unknown"),
					"-- Hash       : " .. tostring(entry.funcHash or "N/A"),
					"-- Executor   : " .. tostring(entry.isExecutor == true),
				}
				local fn = entry.func
				if type(fn) == "function" then
					local isl = Zenith.capFn("islclosure")
					local isL = not isl or (pcall(isl, fn) and isl(fn) == true)
					if isL then
						local gUps = Zenith.capFn("getupvalues")
						local gKs = Zenith.capFn("getconstants")
						local gPr = Zenith.capFn("getprotos")
						if gUps then
							local okU, ups = pcall(gUps, fn)
							lines[#lines + 1] = "local upvalues = " .. (okU and serializeValue(ups, 0, {}) or "{}")
						end
						if gKs then
							local okK, ks = pcall(gKs, fn)
							lines[#lines + 1] = "local constants = " .. (okK and serializeValue(ks, 0, {}) or "{}")
						end
						if gPr then
							local okP, prs = pcall(gPr, fn)
							lines[#lines + 1] = "-- Protos count: " .. tostring(okP and type(prs) == "table" and #prs or 0)
						end
					else
						lines[#lines + 1] = "-- (C Closure)"
					end
				end
				local ok = Zenith.copy(table.concat(lines, "\n"))
				Zenith.log(ok and "Info" or "Warn", ok and "RSpy: Function Info copiado" or "Clipboard no soportado")
			end

			Zenith.actions[N.exportSession] = function()
				if #state.entries == 0 then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: no hay llamadas capturadas para exportar")
				end
				local parts = {
					"-- Zenith RSpy Session Export",
					"-- PlaceId: " .. tostring(game.PlaceId or 0) .. " | Calls: " .. tostring(#state.entries),
					"",
				}
				for _, e in ipairs(state.entries) do
					parts[#parts + 1] = safeFormat(e)
					parts[#parts + 1] = ""
				end
				local payload = table.concat(parts, "\n")
				if Zenith.cap("writefile") then
					local mk, isf = Zenith.capFn("makefolder"), Zenith.capFn("isfolder")
					for _, dir in ipairs({ "Zenith", "Zenith/Logs" }) do
						if mk and not (isf and isf(dir)) then
							pcall(mk, dir)
						end
					end
					local fname = "Zenith/Logs/RSpy_Export_" .. tostring(os.date("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")) .. ".lua"
					local okW, errW = pcall(Zenith.capFn("writefile"), fname, payload)
					if okW then
						Zenith.log("Info", string.format("RSpy: exportadas %d llamadas -> %s", #state.entries, fname))
						return
					end
					Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy export writefile fallo: " .. tostring(errW))
				end
				local okC = Zenith.copy(payload)
				Zenith.log(okC and "Info" or "Warn", okC and "RSpy: sesion copiada al portapapeles" or "Clipboard no soportado")
			end

			Zenith.actions[N.findXRefs] = function()
				local entry = getSelectedEntry()
				if not entry then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: selecciona una llamada primero")
				end
				if Zenith.findScriptXRefs then
					Zenith.findScriptXRefs(entry.name)
				else
					Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: modulo Decompiler no cargado")
				end
			end

			local function runAsInternal(fn, ...)
				local thread = coroutine.running() or MAIN_THREAD
				local prev = state.internal[thread]
				state.internal[thread] = true
				local ok, err = pcall(fn, ...)
				state.internal[thread] = prev
				return ok, err
			end

			Zenith.actions[N.repeatCall] = function()
				local entry = getSelectedEntry()
				if not entry or not entry.remote then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: selecciona una llamada valida")
				end
				if entry.dir == "S2C" then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: no se puede reenviar una llamada Server->Client")
				end
				if entry.dir == "INT" then
					local fnName = entry.className == "ProximityPrompt" and "fireproximityprompt" or "fireclickdetector"
					local fn = Zenith.capFn(fnName)
					if not fn then
						return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: " .. fnName .. " no soportado en este executor")
					end
					task.spawn(function()
						local ok, err = runAsInternal(fn, entry.remote)
						Zenith.log(ok and "Info" or "Error", ok and ("RSpy: repetida " .. entry.name) or ("RSpy Repeat error: " .. tostring(err)))
					end)
					return
				end
				if entry.dir == "ATTR" then
					task.spawn(function()
						local ok, err = runAsInternal(function()
							entry.remote:SetAttribute(entry.args[1], entry.args[2])
						end)
						Zenith.log(ok and "Info" or "Error", ok and ("RSpy: atributo restaurado " .. tostring(entry.args[1])) or ("RSpy Attribute error: " .. tostring(err)))
					end)
					return
				end
				task.spawn(function()
					local ok, err = runAsInternal(function()
						local fn = safeRead(entry.remote, entry.method)
						if type(fn) ~= "function" then
							error("metodo no encontrado: " .. tostring(entry.method))
						end
						local n = entry.args.n or #entry.args
						fn(entry.remote, table.unpack(entry.args, 1, n))
					end)
					Zenith.log(ok and "Info" or "Error", ok and ("RSpy: repetida " .. entry.name) or ("RSpy Repeat error: " .. tostring(err)))
				end)
			end

			Zenith.actions[N.replayCustom] = function()
				local entry = getSelectedEntry()
				if not entry or not entry.remote then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: selecciona una llamada valida")
				end
				if entry.dir == "S2C" then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: no se puede reenviar una llamada Server->Client")
				end
				if entry.dir == "INT" or entry.dir == "ATTR" then
					return Zenith.actions[N.repeatCall]()
				end
				local raw = string.match(tostring(Zenith.values[N.customArgs] or ""), "^%s*(.-)%s*$")
				if raw == "" then
					return Zenith.actions[N.repeatCall]()
				end
				local loader = (typeof(loadstring) == "function" and loadstring) or (typeof(load) == "function" and load)
				if not loader then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: este entorno no soporta loadstring/load para Custom Args")
				end
				local chunk, compileErr = loader("return " .. raw)
				if not chunk then
					return Zenith.log("Error", "RSpy Custom Args sintaxis: " .. tostring(compileErr))
				end
				local evalPack = table.pack(pcall(chunk))
				if not evalPack[1] then
					return Zenith.log("Error", "RSpy Custom Args eval: " .. tostring(evalPack[2]))
				end
				local argCount = math.max(0, (evalPack.n or #evalPack) - 1)
				task.spawn(function()
					local ok, err = runAsInternal(function()
						local fn = safeRead(entry.remote, entry.method)
						if type(fn) ~= "function" then
							error("metodo no encontrado: " .. tostring(entry.method))
						end
						fn(entry.remote, table.unpack(evalPack, 2, 1 + argCount))
					end)
					Zenith.log(ok and "Info" or "Error", ok and string.format("RSpy: replay custom (%d args) -> %s", argCount, entry.name) or ("RSpy Replay Custom error: " .. tostring(err)))
				end)
			end

			Zenith.actions[N.blockRemote] = function()
				local entry = getSelectedEntry()
				if not entry or not entry.remote then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: selecciona un remote primero")
				end
				local nowBlocked = not (state.blocked[entry.remote] == true)
				state.blocked[entry.remote] = nowBlocked or nil
				Zenith.log("Info", string.format("RSpy: %s %s", entry.name, nowBlocked and "BLOQUEADO" or "desbloqueado"))
			end

			Zenith.actions[N.ignoreRemote] = function()
				local entry = getSelectedEntry()
				if not entry then
					return Zenith.log("Warn", "RSpy: selecciona un remote primero")
				end
				local cur = tostring(Zenith.values[N.ignoreList] or "")
				if not isIgnoredName(entry.name) then
					local nextStr = (cur ~= "" and (cur .. ", " .. entry.name)) or entry.name
					setVal(N.ignoreList, nextStr)
					refreshIgnoreSet()
					applyListFilter()
					Zenith.log("Info", "RSpy: ignorado " .. entry.name)
				end
			end

			-- Config may already have the spy enabled at startup
			if flag(N.enabled, false) then
				onEnabled(true)
			end

			Zenith.log("Info", "RSpy listo: activa 'Remote Spy' para capturar")
		end,
		stop = function()
			stopped = true
			uninstallHooks()
			for name, s in pairs(savedHooks) do
				Zenith.hooks[name] = s.prev
			end
			table.clear(savedHooks)
			for _, k in ipairs({ N.clear, N.copyCode, N.copyPath, N.decompileCaller, N.copyFnInfo, N.findXRefs, N.exportSession, N.repeatCall, N.replayCustom, N.blockRemote, N.ignoreRemote }) do
				Zenith.actions[k] = nil
			end
			table.clear(state.queue)
			if Zenith.RSpy == state then
				Zenith.RSpy = nil
			end
		end,
	})
end

-- Zenith | Spy Overlay (Cobalt-style floating inspector integrated with Zenith.RSpy).
do
	local TITLE, PER_PAGE, MAX_GROUPS, MIN_W, MIN_H = "Zenith | Spy", 8, 400, 520, 320
	local Players, UIS = game:GetService("Players"), game:GetService("UserInputService")
	local okCG, CoreGui = pcall(game.GetService, game, "CoreGui")
	if not okCG then
		CoreGui = nil
	end
	local env = (typeof(getgenv) == "function" and pcall(getgenv) and getgenv()) or {}
	local FONT, BOLD, MONO = Enum.Font.GothamMedium, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Enum.Font.RobotoMono

	local T = {
		bg = Color3.fromRGB(17, 17, 19), panel = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 29), panel2 = Color3.fromRGB(33, 33, 37),
		row = Color3.fromRGB(21, 21, 24), hover = Color3.fromRGB(46, 46, 52), sel = Color3.fromRGB(54, 54, 60),
		stroke = Color3.fromRGB(48, 48, 54), text = Color3.fromRGB(236, 236, 240), sub = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158),
		mute = Color3.fromRGB(95, 95, 102), accent = Color3.fromRGB(205, 205, 212), good = Color3.fromRGB(105, 200, 125),
		warn = Color3.fromRGB(232, 182, 80), bad = Color3.fromRGB(222, 92, 92), num = Color3.fromRGB(236, 200, 88),
		str = Color3.fromRGB(140, 210, 130), bool = Color3.fromRGB(240, 150, 90), inst = Color3.fromRGB(110, 170, 240),
		purple = Color3.fromRGB(186, 150, 240), orange = Color3.fromRGB(240, 140, 80),
	}
	local RN = {
		overlay = "Spy Overlay", enabled = "Remote Spy", list = "Remote List", clear = "Clear Spy Logs", copyCode = "Copy Call Code",
		copyPath = "Copy Remote Path", decompileCaller = "Decompile Caller", findXRefs = "Find Script XRefs",
		repeatCall = "Repeat Call", blockRemote = "Block Remote", ignoreRemote = "Ignore Remote",
		ignoreList = "Spy Ignore List", c2s = "Log Client To Server", s2c = "Log Server To Client",
		logReturn = "Log Return Values", showStack = "Show Call Stack", maxLogs = "Spy Max Logs",
		ignoreInternals = "Ignore Roblox Internals", ignoreBindables = "Ignore Bindables",
		ignoreExecutor = "Ignore Executor Calls", autoIgnoreSpam = "Auto Ignore Spammy", logActors = "Log Actors",
		logInteractions = "Log Interactions", logAttributes = "Log Attributes", fullSerialize = "Full Serialization",
	}
	local DEFAULTS = {
		[RN.enabled] = false, [RN.c2s] = true, [RN.s2c] = true, [RN.logReturn] = false, [RN.showStack] = false,
		[RN.fullSerialize] = false, [RN.logActors] = true, [RN.logInteractions] = false, [RN.logAttributes] = false,
		[RN.ignoreInternals] = true, [RN.ignoreBindables] = true, [RN.ignoreExecutor] = true, [RN.autoIgnoreSpam] = false,
	}
	local TABS = {
		{ text = "Outgoing", page = "spy", dir = "C2S" },
		{ text = "Incoming", page = "spy", dir = "S2C" },
		{ text = "Events", page = "spy", dir = "EV" },
		{ text = "Hooks", page = "hooks" },
		{ text = "Settings", page = "settings" },
	}
	local PARENT_MODES = { "auto", "zenith", "gethui", "coregui", "playergui" }
	local TOGGLES = {
		{ "CAPTURA", { { RN.enabled, "Remote Spy" }, { RN.c2s, "Log Client -> Server" }, { RN.s2c, "Log Server -> Client" }, { RN.logReturn, "Log Return Values" }, { RN.showStack, "Show Call Stack" }, { RN.fullSerialize, "Full Serialization" } } },
		{ "FUENTES", { { RN.logActors, "Log Actors" }, { RN.logInteractions, "Log Interactions" }, { RN.logAttributes, "Log Attributes" } } },
		{ "FILTROS", { { RN.ignoreInternals, "Ignore Roblox Internals" }, { RN.ignoreBindables, "Ignore Bindables" }, { RN.ignoreExecutor, "Ignore Executor Calls" }, { RN.autoIgnoreSpam, "Auto Ignore Spammy" } } },
	}

	local st = {
		alive = false, gen = 0, tab = 1, selKey = nil, page = 1, search = "", paused = false, dirty = true,
		conns = {}, mode = "auto", parentLabel = "?", msg = "", msgUntil = 0, minimized = false, nGroups = 0,
	}
	local U = {}
	local lowerCache = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local savedOverlayHook = nil
	local refresh

	local function new(class, props, parent)
		local i = Instance.new(class)
		for k, v in pairs(props) do
			i[k] = v
		end
		if parent then
			i.Parent = parent
		end
		return i
	end
	local function corner(p, r) return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r) }, p) end
	local function stroke(p, c) return new("UIStroke", { Color = c or T.stroke, Thickness = 1, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, p) end
	local function pad(p, l, t, r, b)
		return new("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, l), PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, t or l), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, r or l), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, b or t or l) }, p)
	end
	local function vlist(p, gap, dir)
		return new("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, gap or 4), FillDirection = dir or Enum.FillDirection.Vertical }, p)
	end
	local function label(p, text, props)
		local l = new("TextLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = text, TextColor3 = T.text, Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BorderSizePixel = 0 })
		for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do
			l[k] = v
		end
		l.Parent = p
		return l
	end
	local function button(p, text, props, cb)
		local b = new("TextButton", { Text = text, Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.text, BackgroundColor3 = T.panel2, AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0 })
		for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do
			b[k] = v
		end
		corner(b, 5); stroke(b)
		b.Parent = p
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() b.BackgroundColor3 = T.hover end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() b.BackgroundColor3 = T.panel2 end)
		if cb then
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local ok, err = pcall(cb)
				if not ok then
					Zenith.log("Error", "Overlay: " .. tostring(err))
				end
			end)
		end
		return b
	end
	local function isPressOv(i) return i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch end
	local function isMoveOv(i) return i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch end
	local function sectionHeader(parent, text, order)
		local f = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 24), LayoutOrder = order }, parent)
		new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 12), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 6) }, f)
		local t = label(f, text, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, 0), Font = BOLD, TextColor3 = T.sub, TextSize = 11 })
		return f, t
	end
	local function makePool(parent, buildFn)
		local pool = { rows = {} }
		function pool.begin() pool.seen = {} end
		function pool.get(id, order)
			local r = pool.rows[id]
			if not r then
				r = buildFn(id)
				r.root.Parent = parent
				pool.rows[id] = r
			end
			r.root.LayoutOrder = order
			pool.seen[id] = true
			return r
		end
		function pool.finish()
			for id, r in pairs(pool.rows) do
				if not pool.seen[id] then
					r.root:Destroy()
					pool.rows[id] = nil
				end
			end
		end
		return pool
	end

	local function getFlag(name)
		local v = Zenith.values[name]
		if v == nil then v = DEFAULTS[name] end
		return v == true
	end
	local function setFlag(name, v)
		Zenith.values[name] = v
		local s = Zenith.setters and Zenith.setters[name]
		if s then
			pcall(s, v)
		elseif Zenith.hooks and Zenith.hooks[name] then
			pcall(Zenith.hooks[name], v)
		end
	end
	local function toast(msg)
		st.msg, st.msgUntil = tostring(msg), os.clock() + 2.5
		if U.statusLbl then U.statusLbl.Text = st.msg end
	end
	local function runAction(name, entry)
		local act = Zenith.actions and Zenith.actions[name]
		if not act then return toast("Accion no disponible: " .. name) end
		local S = Zenith.RSpy
		if entry and S then
			S.selectedLabel, S.selectedGroupKey = entry.label, nil
			Zenith.values[RN.list] = entry.label
		end
		local ok, err = pcall(act)
		if not ok then toast("Error: " .. tostring(err)) end
		st.dirty = true
	end
	local function pathOfInst(inst)
		local ok, s = pcall(inst.GetFullName, inst)
		return ok and s or "?"
	end

	local ESC_OV = { ["\n"] = "\\n", ["\r"] = "\\r", ["\t"] = "\\t" }
	local function clip(s, n) return #s > n and (string.sub(s, 1, n) .. "...") or s end
	local function fmtNumber(v)
		if v ~= v or v == math.huge or v == -math.huge then return tostring(v) end
		if v % 1 == 0 and math.abs(v) < 2 ^ 53 then return string.format("%d", v) end
		return string.format("%.3f", v)
	end
	local function short(v)
		local t = typeof(v)
		if t == "string" then return '"' .. clip((string.gsub(v, "[\n\r\t]", ESC_OV)), 24) .. '"' end
		if t == "Instance" then
			local ok, n = pcall(function() return v.Name end)
			return ok and tostring(n) or "Instance"
		end
		if t == "table" then return "{...}" end
		if t == "buffer" then return string.format("buffer(%d)", buffer.len(v)) end
		local ok, s = pcall(tostring, v)
		return ok and s or t
	end
	local function fmtVal(v, full)
		local lim, t = full and 4000 or 120, typeof(v)
		if t == "string" then return '"' .. clip((string.gsub(v, "[\n\r\t]", ESC_OV)), lim) .. '"', "string", T.str
		elseif t == "number" then return fmtNumber(v), "number", T.num
		elseif t == "boolean" then return tostring(v), "boolean", T.bool
		elseif t == "nil" then return "nil", "nil", T.mute
		elseif t == "Vector3" then return string.format("%.3f, %.3f, %.3f", v.X, v.Y, v.Z), "Vector3", T.num
		elseif t == "Vector2" then return string.format("%.3f, %.3f", v.X, v.Y), "Vector2", T.num
		elseif t == "CFrame" then local p = v.Position; return string.format("%.3f, %.3f, %.3f", p.X, p.Y, p.Z), "CFrame", T.num
		elseif t == "Color3" then return string.format("%d, %d, %d", v.R * 255 + 0.5, v.G * 255 + 0.5, v.B * 255 + 0.5), "Color3", T.num
		elseif t == "buffer" then
			local len = buffer.len(v)
			if full then
				local raw = buffer.readstring(v, 0, math.min(len, 512))
				return string.format("buffer.fromstring(%q)", raw), "buffer", T.purple
			end
			return string.format("buffer (%d bytes)", len), "buffer", T.purple
		elseif t == "Instance" then
			local okC, cls = pcall(function() return v.ClassName end)
			return pathOfInst(v), okC and tostring(cls) or "Instance", T.inst
		elseif t == "table" then
			local parts, n, maxN = {}, 0, full and 40 or 4
			for k, val in pairs(v) do
				n += 1
				if n > maxN then break end
				parts[#parts + 1] = (type(k) == "number" and "" or (tostring(k) .. "=")) .. short(val)
			end
			return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ", ") .. (n > maxN and ", ..." or "") .. "}", "table", T.purple
		end
		local ok, s = pcall(tostring, v)
		return ok and s or t, t, T.sub
	end
	local function safeFmt(v, full)
		local ok, a, b, c = pcall(fmtVal, v, full)
		if ok then return a, b, c end
		return "<error>", typeof(v), T.mute
	end
	local ICON = {
		RemoteEvent = { "E", T.orange }, UnreliableRemoteEvent = { "U", T.orange }, RemoteFunction = { "F", T.inst },
		BindableEvent = { "B", T.purple }, BindableFunction = { "B", T.purple }, ProximityPrompt = { "P", T.num }, ClickDetector = { "C", T.num },
	}

	local function findZenithGui()
		if typeof(shared) == "table" and typeof(shared.ZenithGui) == "Instance" and shared.ZenithGui ~= U.gui then
			return shared.ZenithGui
		end
		local roots = {}
		local gh = env.gethui
		if type(gh) == "function" then
			local ok, h = pcall(gh)
			if ok and typeof(h) == "Instance" then roots[#roots + 1] = h end
		end
		if CoreGui then roots[#roots + 1] = CoreGui end
		local pg = localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
		if pg then roots[#roots + 1] = pg end
		for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
			local okC, kids = pcall(root.GetChildren, root)
			if okC and type(kids) == "table" then
				for _, g in ipairs(kids) do
					if g ~= U.gui and g:IsA("ScreenGui") then
						local okD, desc = pcall(g.GetDescendants, g)
						if okD and type(desc) == "table" then
							for i = 1, math.min(#desc, 300) do
								local d = desc[i]
								if (d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton")) and string.sub(d.Text, 1, 6) == "Zenith" then
									return g
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function candidates(mode)
		local c = {}
		local function add(inst, lbl)
			if typeof(inst) == "Instance" then c[#c + 1] = { inst, lbl } end
		end
		if (mode == "auto" or mode == "zenith") and U.zGui then add(U.zGui.Parent, "Zenith parent") end
		if mode == "auto" or mode == "gethui" then
			local gh = env.gethui
			if type(gh) == "function" then
				local ok, h = pcall(gh)
				if ok then add(h, "gethui") end
			end
		end
		if mode == "auto" or mode == "coregui" then add(CoreGui, "CoreGui") end
		if mode == "auto" or mode == "playergui" then add(localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui"), "PlayerGui") end
		return c
	end
	local function attach(gui, mode)
		local c = candidates(mode)
		if #c == 0 then c = candidates("auto") end
		local prot = env.protect_gui or (type(env.syn) == "table" and env.syn.protect_gui) or env.protectgui
		if type(prot) == "function" then pcall(prot, gui) end
		gui.DisplayOrder = ((U.zGui and U.zGui.DisplayOrder) or 0) + 1
		for _, cand in ipairs(c) do
			local ok = pcall(function() gui.Parent = cand[1] end)
			if ok and gui.Parent == cand[1] then
				st.parentLabel = cand[2]
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end
	local function cycleParent()
		local idx = 1
		for i, m in ipairs(PARENT_MODES) do
			if m == st.mode then idx = i end
		end
		st.mode = PARENT_MODES[(idx % #PARENT_MODES) + 1]
		U.zGui = findZenithGui()
		if not attach(U.gui, st.mode) then
			toast("No se pudo re-parentar")
		else
			toast("Parent: " .. st.mode .. " -> " .. st.parentLabel)
		end
	end

	local function curGroup() return st.byKey and st.byKey[st.selKey] or nil end
	local function callerName(e)
		if typeof(e.callerInst) == "Instance" then
			local ok, n = pcall(function() return e.callerInst.Name end)
			if ok then return tostring(n) end
		end
		if e.funcName and e.funcName ~= "" then return tostring(e.funcName) end
		return e.isExecutor and "Executor" or "desconocido"
	end
	local function addValueRow(card, order, idx, v)
		local text, typ, col = safeFmt(v)
		local row = new("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = T.row, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), LayoutOrder = order }, card)
		corner(row, 5)
		label(row, tostring(idx), { Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 1, 0), TextColor3 = T.sub })
