		label(row, text, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -122, 1, 0), TextColor3 = col, Font = MONO, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
		label(row, typ, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 76, 1, 0), TextColor3 = T.sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
		row.MouseEnter:Connect(function() row.BackgroundColor3 = T.hover end)
		row.MouseLeave:Connect(function() row.BackgroundColor3 = T.row end)
		row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			local full = safeFmt(v, true)
			toast(Zenith.copy(full) and "Valor copiado" or "Clipboard no soportado")
		end)
	end
	local function buildCard(e, order)
		local card = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.panel2, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = order }, U.cardScroll)
		corner(card, 7); stroke(card); pad(card, 6, 5); vlist(card, 3)
		local o = 0
		local function nx() o += 1; return o end
		local args = e.args or { n = 0 }
		local n = args.n or #args
		if n == 0 then
			label(card, "(sin argumentos)", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), TextColor3 = T.mute, LayoutOrder = nx() })
		end
		for i = 1, math.min(n, 12) do addValueRow(card, nx(), i, args[i]) end
		if n > 12 then label(card, string.format("... +%d argumentos mas", n - 12), { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), TextColor3 = T.sub, LayoutOrder = nx() }) end
		if e.returns then
			label(card, "RETURNS", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16), TextColor3 = T.sub, Font = BOLD, TextSize = 10, LayoutOrder = nx() })
			local rn = e.returns.n or #e.returns
			if rn == 0 then label(card, "(void)", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18), TextColor3 = T.mute, LayoutOrder = nx() }) end
			for i = 1, math.min(rn, 6) do addValueRow(card, nx(), "r" .. i, e.returns[i]) end
		end
		local foot = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), LayoutOrder = nx() }, card)
		local tags = (e.blocked and "  [BLOCKED]" or "") .. (e.isExecutor and "  [EXECUTOR]" or "") .. (e.isActor and "  [ACTOR]" or "")
		label(foot, "Script: " .. callerName(e) .. tags, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 1, 0), TextColor3 = e.blocked and T.bad or T.sub, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
		label(foot, "Time: " .. tostring(e.timeStr or "?"), { Position = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 1, 0), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextColor3 = T.sub })
		local btnOrder, stackOrder = nx(), nx()
		local stackLbl
		if e.stack and e.stack ~= "" then
			stackLbl = label(card, e.stack, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, TextWrapped = true, Font = MONO, TextSize = 10, TextColor3 = T.sub, Visible = false, LayoutOrder = stackOrder, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top })
		end
		local bar = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20), LayoutOrder = btnOrder }, card)
		vlist(bar, 4, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal)
		local function mini(text, w, cb) return button(bar, text, { Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 1, 0), TextSize = 11, LayoutOrder = #bar:GetChildren() }, cb) end
		mini("Copy", 48, function() runAction(RN.copyCode, e) end)
		if e.dir ~= "S2C" then mini("Repeat", 54, function() runAction(RN.repeatCall, e) end) end
		mini("Caller", 52, function() runAction(RN.decompileCaller, e) end)
		if stackLbl then mini("Stack", 48, function() stackLbl.Visible = not stackLbl.Visible end) end
		U.cards[#U.cards + 1] = card
	end
	local function renderCards(g)
		for _, c in ipairs(U.cards) do c:Destroy() end
		U.cards = {}
		if not g then
			U.pageChip.Text = "- / -"
			return
		end
		local total = #g.calls
		local pages = math.max(1, math.ceil(total / PER_PAGE))
		if st.page > pages then st.page = pages end
		if st.page < 1 then st.page = 1 end
		U.pageChip.Text = string.format("%d / %d", st.page, pages)
		local first = total - (st.page - 1) * PER_PAGE
		local last = math.max(1, first - PER_PAGE + 1)
		local order = 0
		for i = first, last, -1 do
			order += 1
			buildCard(g.calls[i], order)
		end
	end

	local function selectGroup(key)
		st.selKey, st.page, st.dirty = key, 1, true
		refresh(true)
	end
	local function buildGroupRow(id)
		local btn = new("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = T.panel, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 30) })
		corner(btn, 6)
		local icon = new("TextLabel", { BackgroundColor3 = T.row, Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -9), Font = BOLD, TextSize = 11, Text = "?", TextColor3 = T.orange, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, btn)
		corner(icon, 4)
		local nm = label(btn, "", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -86, 1, 0), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
		local cnt = label(btn, "", { Position = UDim2.new(1, -52, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 1, 0), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextColor3 = T.sub })
		btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() btn.BackgroundColor3 = T.hover end)
		btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() btn.BackgroundColor3 = (st.selKey == id) and T.sel or T.panel end)
		btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectGroup(id) end)
		return { root = btn, icon = icon, name = nm, count = cnt }
	end
	local function computeGroups(S)
		local mode = TABS[st.tab].dir
		local needle = string.lower(st.search)
		local byKey, order, counts = {}, {}, { C2S = 0, S2C = 0, EV = 0 }
		if not S then return order, byKey, counts end
		local ign = S.ignoredNames or {}
		for _, e in ipairs(S.entries) do
			local d = e.dir
			local cat = (d == "INT" or d == "ATTR") and "EV" or d
			if counts[cat] ~= nil then
				local ln = lowerCache[e]
				if not ln then
					ln = string.lower(tostring(e.name))
					lowerCache[e] = ln
				end
				if not ign[ln] then
					counts[cat] += 1
					if cat == mode and (needle == "" or string.find(ln, needle, 1, true)) then
						local key = tostring(e.className) .. "|" .. tostring(e.path) .. "|" .. tostring(e.method)
						local g = byKey[key]
						if not g then
							g = { key = key, name = e.name, className = e.className, method = e.method, path = e.path, calls = {}, count = 0 }
							byKey[key] = g
							order[#order + 1] = g
						end
						g.count += 1
						g.calls[g.count] = e
						g.last = e
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return order, byKey, counts
	end
	local function updateStatus(S)
		if os.clock() < st.msgUntil then
			U.statusLbl.Text = st.msg
			return
		end
		local on = getFlag(RN.enabled)
		U.statusDot.BackgroundColor3 = on and T.good or T.mute
		U.statusLbl.Text = string.format("%s  |  %d calls  |  %d remotes  |  cola %d", on and "Spy ON" or "Spy OFF", S and S.seq or 0, st.nGroups, (S and S.queue and #S.queue) or 0)
	end

	local function collectHookRows(S)
		local rows = {}
		local function H(id, text) rows[#rows + 1] = { id = "h:" .. id, text = text } end
		local function R(id, text, state, detail, btnText, onClick)
			rows[#rows + 1] = { id = id, text = text, state = state, detail = detail, btnText = btnText, onClick = onClick }
		end
		local function recState(r) if not r then return "off" end; return r.active and "on" or "warn" end
		if not S then
			H("none", "RSPY")
			R("nospy", "Zenith.RSpy", "bad", "modulo no cargado")
		else
			H("hooks", "HOOKS")
			local nc = S.namecallRec
			R("nc", "__namecall", recState(nc), nc and (nc.active and ("activo (" .. tostring(S.namecallVia or "hook") .. ")") or "pass-through") or "no instalado")
			local dh = S.directHooks or {}
			local act = 0
			for _, r in ipairs(dh) do if r.active then act += 1 end end
			R("dh", "hookfunction / oth", #dh == 0 and "off" or (act > 0 and "on" or "warn"), string.format("%d/%d activos", act, #dh))
			for i, r in ipairs(dh) do
				local okN, nm = pcall(debug.info, r.target, "n")
				R("dh" .. i, "   " .. ((okN and type(nm) == "string" and nm ~= "") and nm or ("fn#" .. i)), recState(r), r.usedOth and "oth" or "hookfunction")
			end
			R("ni", "__newindex (callbacks)", recState(S.newindexRec), S.newindexRec and (S.newindexRec.active and "activo" or "pass-through") or "no instalado")
			R("sg", "Signal hook (__index)", recState(S.signalHook), S.signalHook and (S.signalHook.active and "activo" or "pass-through") or "no instalado")
			R("ac", "Actors", S.actorChannel and "on" or "off", string.format("%d conexiones", #(S.actorConns or {})))
			local s2c = #(S.s2cConns or {})
			R("s2c", "S2C connections", s2c > 0 and "on" or "off", tostring(s2c))
			local wc = 0
			for _ in pairs(S.wrappedCallbacks or {}) do wc += 1 end
			R("wc", "Callbacks wrapped", wc > 0 and "on" or "off", tostring(wc))
			R("int", "Interactions", #(S.interactionConns or {}) > 0 and "on" or "off", tostring(#(S.interactionConns or {})))
			R("att", "Attributes", #(S.attrConns or {}) > 0 and "on" or "off", tostring(#(S.attrConns or {})))
			H("pipe", "PIPELINE")
			local on = getFlag(RN.enabled)
			R("spy", "Remote Spy", on and "on" or "off", on and "capturando" or "pausado", on and "Pausar" or "Activar", function() setFlag(RN.enabled, not getFlag(RN.enabled)) end)
			local q = #(S.queue or {})
			R("q", "Cola", q > 1500 and "warn" or "on", string.format("%d en cola", q))
			R("log", "Entradas", "on", string.format("%d / %d  (seq %d)", #S.entries, tonumber(Zenith.values[RN.maxLogs]) or 300, S.seq or 0))
		end
		H("par", "OVERLAY PARENT")
		R("par", "Overlay", U.gui and U.gui.Parent and "on" or "bad", (U.gui and U.gui.Parent and pathOfInst(U.gui.Parent) or "?") .. " [" .. st.parentLabel .. "]", "Cambiar", cycleParent)
		R("zui", "Zenith GUI", U.zGui and "on" or "off", U.zGui and pathOfInst(U.zGui) or "no detectado", "Detectar", function() U.zGui = findZenithGui(); toast(U.zGui and "Zenith GUI detectado" or "No detectado") end)
		R("ord", "DisplayOrder", "on", tostring(U.gui and U.gui.DisplayOrder or 0))
		if S then
			local bl, n = {}, 0
			for inst, v in pairs(S.blocked or {}) do
				if v == true and typeof(inst) == "Instance" then
					n += 1
					if n <= 30 then bl[#bl + 1] = inst end
				end
			end
			H("blk", "BLOCKED REMOTES (" .. n .. ")")
			for i, inst in ipairs(bl) do
				R("b" .. i, clip(pathOfInst(inst), 40), "bad", inst.ClassName, "Unblock", function() S.blocked[inst] = nil; st.dirty = true end)
			end
		end
		H("cap", "CAPABILITIES")
		local have, total, missing = 0, 0, {}
		for name, statusVal in pairs(Zenith.capabilities and Zenith.capabilities.status or {}) do
			total += 1
			if statusVal ~= nil and statusVal ~= "missing" then
				have += 1
			else
				missing[#missing + 1] = name
			end
		end
		table.sort(missing)
		R("capn", "Disponibles", have == total and "on" or "warn", string.format("%d / %d", have, total))
		if #missing > 0 then R("capm", "Faltan", "warn", table.concat(missing, ", ")) end
		return rows
	end
	local function updateHooks()
		local rows = collectHookRows(Zenith.RSpy)
		U.hookPool.begin()
		for i, d in ipairs(rows) do
			local r = U.hookPool.get(d.id, i)
			r.name.Text = d.text
			if r.dot then
				r.dot.BackgroundColor3 = d.state == "on" and T.good or d.state == "warn" and T.warn or d.state == "bad" and T.bad or T.mute
				r.detail.Text = d.detail or ""
				if d.btnText then
					r.btn.Visible, r.btn.Text, r.onClick = true, d.btnText, d.onClick
					r.detail.Size = UDim2.new(0.58, -78, 1, 0)
				else
					r.btn.Visible, r.onClick = false, nil
					r.detail.Size = UDim2.new(0.58, -10, 1, 0)
				end
			end
		end
		U.hookPool.finish()
	end

	function refresh(force)
		if not st.alive or not U.gui then return end
		local S = Zenith.RSpy
		updateStatus(S)
		local tab = TABS[st.tab]
		if tab.page == "hooks" then return updateHooks() end
		if tab.page == "settings" then
			for _, fn in ipairs(U.refreshers) do fn() end
			return
		end
		local g0 = curGroup()
		if g0 and g0.last then
			U.blockBtn.Text = (S and S.blocked[g0.last.remote] == true) and "Unblock" or "Block"
		end
		if st.paused and not force then return end
		local sig = table.concat({ S and S.seq or -1, S and #S.entries or -1, (S and S.entries[1] and S.entries[1].id) or 0, S and S.ignoreGen or 0, getFlag(RN.enabled) and 1 or 0, st.tab, st.search }, "|")
		if sig == st.sig and not st.dirty and not force then return end
		st.sig, st.dirty = sig, false
		local order, byKey, counts = computeGroups(S)
		st.byKey, st.nGroups = byKey, #order
		U.tabBtns[1].Text = "Outgoing  " .. counts.C2S
		U.tabBtns[2].Text = "Incoming  " .. counts.S2C
		U.tabBtns[3].Text = "Events  " .. counts.EV
		if not byKey[st.selKey] then
			st.selKey, st.page = order[1] and order[1].key or nil, 1
		end
		U.listPool.begin()
		for i = 1, math.min(#order, MAX_GROUPS) do
			local g = order[i]
			local r = U.listPool.get(g.key, i)
			local blocked = S and g.last and S.blocked[g.last.remote] == true
			local ic = ICON[g.className] or { "A", T.good }
			r.icon.Text, r.icon.TextColor3 = ic[1], ic[2]
			r.name.Text = g.name
			r.name.TextColor3 = blocked and T.bad or T.text
			r.count.Text = "x" .. g.count
			r.root.BackgroundColor3 = (st.selKey == g.key) and T.sel or T.panel
		end
		U.listPool.finish()
		local g = byKey[st.selKey]
		if g then
			U.info.Text = g.path .. "  :" .. tostring(g.method)
			U.empty.Visible = false
			local cs = table.concat({ g.key, g.count, st.page, g.last.id }, "|")
			if cs ~= st.cardSig or force then
				st.cardSig = cs
				renderCards(g)
			end
		else
			U.info.Text = ""
			st.cardSig = ""
			renderCards(nil)
			U.empty.Text = getFlag(RN.enabled) and "Sin llamadas todavia..." or "Remote Spy esta apagado. Activalo en Settings o en la pestana Hooks."
			U.empty.Visible = true
		end
	end

	local function buildSettings(parent)
		local o = 0
		local function nx() o += 1; return o end
		for _, sec in ipairs(TOGGLES) do
			sectionHeader(parent, sec[1], nx())
			for _, t in ipairs(sec[2]) do
				local key = t[1]
				local row = new("TextButton", { Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BackgroundColor3 = T.panel, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 28), LayoutOrder = nx() }, parent)
				corner(row, 6); stroke(row)
				local box = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.row, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -8) }, row)
				corner(box, 4); stroke(box, T.mute)
				local fill = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 1, -6), Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 3) }, box)
				corner(fill, 2)
				label(row, t[2], { Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0) })
				row.MouseEnter:Connect(function() row.BackgroundColor3 = T.hover end)
				row.MouseLeave:Connect(function() row.BackgroundColor3 = T.panel end)
				row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setFlag(key, not getFlag(key)); fill.Visible = getFlag(key) end)
				U.refreshers[#U.refreshers + 1] = function() fill.Visible = getFlag(key) end
				fill.Visible = getFlag(key)
			end
		end
		local function addSlider(order, text, minV, maxV, step, getFn, setFn, fmtFn)
			local f = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.panel, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 44), LayoutOrder = order }, parent)
			corner(f, 6); stroke(f)
			label(f, text, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 16) })
			local val = label(f, "", { Position = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.new(0.4, -10, 0, 16), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, Font = BOLD })
			local track = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.row, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 30), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 4) }, f)
			corner(track, 2)
			local fill = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.accent, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0) }, track)
			corner(fill, 2)
			local knob = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.text, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0) }, track)
			corner(knob, 6)
			local hit = new("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 22), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20) }, f)
			local function show()
				local v = tonumber(getFn()) or minV
				local rel = math.clamp((v - minV) / (maxV - minV), 0, 1)
				fill.Size = UDim2.new(rel, 0, 1, 0)
				knob.Position = UDim2.new(rel, 0, 0.5, 0)
				val.Text = fmtFn and fmtFn(v) or tostring(v)
			end
			local dragging = false
			local function apply(x)
				local rel = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, track.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
				local v = math.clamp(math.floor((minV + rel * (maxV - minV)) / step + 0.5) * step, minV, maxV)
				setFn(v)
				show()
			end
			hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if isPressOv(i) then dragging = true; apply(i.Position.X) end end)
			st.conns[#st.conns + 1] = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i) if dragging and isMoveOv(i) then apply(i.Position.X) end end)
			st.conns[#st.conns + 1] = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i) if isPressOv(i) then dragging = false end end)
			U.refreshers[#U.refreshers + 1] = show
			show()
		end
		sectionHeader(parent, "LIMITES", nx())
		addSlider(nx(), "Spy Max Logs", 50, 1000, 50, function() return Zenith.values[RN.maxLogs] or 300 end, function(v) setFlag(RN.maxLogs, v) end)
		local box = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.panel, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 30), LayoutOrder = nx() }, parent)
		corner(box, 6); stroke(box)
		label(box, "Ignore List", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 1, 0) })
		local tb = new("TextBox", { BackgroundColor3 = T.row, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 94, 0.5, -11), Size = UDim2.new(1, -104, 0, 22), Font = MONO, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.text, PlaceholderText = "remote1, remote2", PlaceholderColor3 = T.mute, Text = "", ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left }, box)
		corner(tb, 5); pad(tb, 6, 0)
		tb.FocusLost:Connect(function() setFlag(RN.ignoreList, tb.Text); st.dirty = true end)
		U.refreshers[#U.refreshers + 1] = function() if not tb:IsFocused() then tb.Text = tostring(Zenith.values[RN.ignoreList] or "") end end
		sectionHeader(parent, "OVERLAY", nx())
		addSlider(nx(), "Escala de ventana", 0.7, 1.4, 0.05, function() return U.scale and U.scale.Scale or 1 end, function(v) if U.scale then U.scale.Scale = v end end, function(v) return string.format("%d%%", v * 100 + 0.5) end)
		label(parent, "F4 / Toggle 'Spy Overlay': mostrar / ocultar", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 20), TextColor3 = T.mute, LayoutOrder = nx() })
	end

	local Overlay = {}
	function Overlay.destroy()
		st.alive = false
		st.gen += 1
		for _, c in ipairs(st.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
		st.conns = {}
		if U.gui then pcall(function() U.gui:Destroy() end) end
		U = {}
	end

	local function buildOverlay(startVisible)
		if U.gui then
			if U.win then U.win.Visible = startVisible == true end
			return
		end
		U = { cards = {}, refreshers = {}, tabBtns = {} }
		st.sig, st.cardSig, st.dirty, st.selKey, st.page, st.minimized = "", "", true, nil, 1, false
		U.zGui = findZenithGui()
		local gui = new("ScreenGui", { Name = "ZenithSpyOverlay", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling })
		U.gui = gui
		if not attach(gui, st.mode) then
			gui:Destroy()
			U = {}
			return Zenith.log("Error", "Overlay: no se pudo parentear el GUI")
		end
		U.scale = new("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, gui)
		local win = new("Frame", { Name = "Window", BackgroundColor3 = T.bg, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 600, 0, 430), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -300, 0.5, -215), ClipsDescendants = true, Active = true, Visible = startVisible == true }, gui)
		U.win = win
		corner(win, 9); stroke(win)

		local hdr = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(32, 32, 36), BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36) }, win)
		new("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 43), Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 25)), Rotation = 90 }, hdr)
		new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.stroke, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1) }, hdr)
		label(hdr, TITLE, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -240, 1, 0), Font = BOLD, TextSize = 13 })
		local search = new("TextBox", { BackgroundColor3 = T.row, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(1, -216, 0, 7), Size = UDim2.new(0, 142, 0, 22), Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = T.text, PlaceholderText = "Buscar", PlaceholderColor3 = T.mute, Text = "", ClearTextOnFocus = false }, hdr)
		corner(search, 6); stroke(search)
		search:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() st.search, st.page, st.dirty = search.Text, 1, true end)
		button(hdr, "-", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0, 7), TextSize = 14 }, function()
			st.minimized = not st.minimized
			U.content.Visible, U.tabBar.Visible, U.statusBar.Visible = not st.minimized, not st.minimized, not st.minimized
			if st.minimized then
				U.savedH = win.Size.Y.Offset
				win.Size = UDim2.new(win.Size.X.Scale, win.Size.X.Offset, 0, 36)
			else
				win.Size = UDim2.new(win.Size.X.Scale, win.Size.X.Offset, 0, U.savedH or 430)
			end
		end)
		button(hdr, "X", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 22), Position = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 7) }, function()
			setFlag(RN.overlay, false)
		end)

		local tabBar = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.panel, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 42), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 28) }, win)
		U.tabBar = tabBar
		corner(tabBar, 8); stroke(tabBar); pad(tabBar, 3); vlist(tabBar, 4, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal)
		local content = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 76), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -106) }, win)
		U.content = content
		local function scroller(visible)
			local s = new("ScrollingFrame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.mute, Visible = visible }, content)
			pad(s, 2); vlist(s, 4)
			return s
		end

		local spy = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) }, content)
		local left = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.panel, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 190, 1, 0) }, spy)
		corner(left, 8); stroke(left)
		local lscroll = new("ScrollingFrame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.mute }, left)
		pad(lscroll, 4); vlist(lscroll, 3)
		U.listPool = makePool(lscroll, buildGroupRow)
		local right = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 196, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -196, 1, 0) }, spy)
		U.info = label(right, "", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 18), Font = MONO, TextSize = 11, TextColor3 = T.sub, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
		local toolbar = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 20), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24) }, right)
		vlist(toolbar, 4, Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal)
		local function tb(text, w, order, cb) return button(toolbar, text, { Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 1, 0), LayoutOrder = order, TextSize = 11 }, cb) end
		local function groupAct(name)
			local g = curGroup()
			if not g then return toast("Selecciona un remote") end
			runAction(name, g.last)
		end
		tb("Copy Path", 74, 1, function() groupAct(RN.copyPath) end)
		U.blockBtn = tb("Block", 58, 2, function() groupAct(RN.blockRemote) end)
		tb("Ignore", 54, 3, function() groupAct(RN.ignoreRemote) end)
		tb("XRefs", 50, 4, function() groupAct(RN.findXRefs) end)
		tb("Clear", 50, 5, function() runAction(RN.clear); st.selKey = nil end)
		U.cardScroll = new("ScrollingFrame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 48), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -78), CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollBarThickness = 3, ScrollBarImageColor3 = T.mute }, right)
		vlist(U.cardScroll, 6)
		U.empty = label(right, "", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 60), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 60), TextColor3 = T.mute, TextWrapped = true, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
		local pager = new("Frame", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -26), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24) }, right)
		new("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, pager)
		button(pager, "<", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 22), LayoutOrder = 1 }, function() if st.page > 1 then st.page -= 1; refresh(true) end end)
		U.pageChip = label(pager, "1 / 1", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 22), LayoutOrder = 2, BackgroundTransparency = 0, BackgroundColor3 = T.sel, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
		corner(U.pageChip, 5)
		button(pager, ">", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 22), LayoutOrder = 3 }, function() st.page += 1; refresh(true) end)

		local hooksPage, setPage = scroller(false), scroller(false)
		U.hookPool = makePool(hooksPage, function(id)
			if string.sub(id, 1, 2) == "h:" then
				local f, t = sectionHeader(nil, "", 0)
				return { root = f, name = t }
			end
			local f = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.panel, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 26) })
			corner(f, 6); stroke(f)
			local dot = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.mute, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8), Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -4) }, f)
			corner(dot, 4)
			local name = label(f, "", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.42, -22, 1, 0), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
			local detail = label(f, "", { Position = UDim2.new(0.42, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0.58, -10, 1, 0), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextColor3 = T.sub, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Font = MONO, TextSize = 11 })
			local r = { root = f, dot = dot, name = name, detail = detail }
			r.btn = button(f, "", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 20), Position = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0.5, -10), Visible = false, TextSize = 11 }, function() if r.onClick then r.onClick() end end)
			return r
		end)
		buildSettings(setPage)
		local pages = { spy = spy, hooks = hooksPage, settings = setPage }

		local function setTab(i)
			st.tab, st.selKey, st.page, st.sig, st.cardSig, st.dirty = i, nil, 1, "", "", true
			for k, b in ipairs(U.tabBtns) do
				b.BackgroundTransparency = (k == i) and 0 or 1
				b.TextColor3 = (k == i) and T.text or T.sub
			end
			for name, p in pairs(pages) do p.Visible = (name == TABS[i].page) end
			refresh(true)
		end
		for i, t in ipairs(TABS) do
			local b = button(tabBar, t.text, { Size = UDim2.new(1 / #TABS, -4, 1, 0), LayoutOrder = i, BackgroundColor3 = T.sel }, function() setTab(i) end)
			b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() b.BackgroundColor3 = T.sel end)
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() b.BackgroundColor3 = T.sel end)
			U.tabBtns[i] = b
		end

		local sb = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.panel, BorderSizePixel = 0, Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -24), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24) }, win)
		U.statusBar = sb
		new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.stroke, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1) }, sb)
		U.statusDot = new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.mute, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8), Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -4) }, sb)
		corner(U.statusDot, 4)
		U.statusLbl = label(sb, "", { Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -130, 1, 0), TextColor3 = T.sub, TextSize = 11, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
		local pauseBtn
		pauseBtn = button(sb, "Live", { Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(1, -92, 0.5, -9), TextSize = 11 }, function()
			st.paused = not st.paused
			pauseBtn.Text = st.paused and "Paused" or "Live"
			pauseBtn.TextColor3 = st.paused and T.warn or T.text
			if not st.paused then refresh(true) end
		end)
		local grip = new("TextButton", { Text = "", BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18), Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, -9) }, sb)
		for _, p in ipairs({ { 12, 4 }, { 8, 8 }, { 12, 8 }, { 4, 12 }, { 8, 12 }, { 12, 12 } }) do
			new("Frame", { BackgroundColor3 = T.mute, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2), Position = UDim2.new(0, p[1], 0, p[2]) }, grip)
		end

		local drag, resize
		hdr.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if isPressOv(i) then drag = { start = i.Position, pos = win.Position } end end)
		grip.InputBegan:Connect(function(i) if isPressOv(i) and not st.minimized then resize = { start = i.Position, w = win.Size.X.Offset, h = win.Size.Y.Offset } end end)
		st.conns[#st.conns + 1] = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
			if not isMoveOv(i) then return end
			local sc = U.scale and U.scale.Scale or 1
			if drag then
				local d = (i.Position - drag.start) / sc
				win.Position = UDim2.new(drag.pos.X.Scale, drag.pos.X.Offset + d.X, drag.pos.Y.Scale, drag.pos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
			elseif resize then
				local d = (i.Position - resize.start) / sc
				win.Size = UDim2.new(0, math.max(MIN_W, resize.w + d.X), 0, math.max(MIN_H, resize.h + d.Y))
			end
		end)
		st.conns[#st.conns + 1] = UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i) if isPressOv(i) then drag, resize = nil, nil end end)

		st.alive = true
		st.gen += 1
		local myGen = st.gen
		setTab(1)
		task.spawn(function()
			while st.alive and st.gen == myGen and U.gui do
				if U.win and U.win.Visible then
					local ok, err = pcall(refresh)
					if not ok and err ~= st.lastErr then
						st.lastErr = err
						Zenith.log("Warn", "Overlay: " .. tostring(err))
					end
				end
				task.wait(0.25)
			end
		end)
		Zenith.log("Info", "Spy Overlay listo (F4 o toggle 'Spy Overlay')")
	end

	function Overlay.toggle(show)
		local target = show
		if target == nil then
			target = not (U.win and U.win.Visible)
		end
		if target then
			if not U.gui then
				buildOverlay(true)
			elseif U.win then
				U.win.Visible = true
				refresh(true)
			end
		elseif U.win then
			U.win.Visible = false
		end
	end

	Zenith.Overlay = Overlay

	Zenith.register("SpyOverlay", {
		requires = {},
		optional = { "gethui" },
		init = function()
			Zenith.Overlay = Overlay
			savedOverlayHook = Zenith.hooks[RN.overlay]
			Zenith.hooks[RN.overlay] = function(on)
				if savedOverlayHook then pcall(savedOverlayHook, on) end
				Overlay.toggle(on == true)
			end
			if Zenith.values[RN.overlay] == true then
				Overlay.toggle(true)
			end
		end,
		stop = function()
			Zenith.hooks[RN.overlay] = savedOverlayHook
			savedOverlayHook = nil
			Overlay.destroy()
			if Zenith.Overlay == Overlay then
				Zenith.Overlay = nil
			end
		end,
	})
end

local function initializeScript()
	if typeof(shared) == "table" and shared.ZenithGui then
		pcall(function()
			shared.ZenithGui:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	local guiParent = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

	if typeof(gethui) == "function" then
		local success, hiddenParent = pcall(gethui)
		if success and hiddenParent then
			guiParent = hiddenParent
		end
	end

	local screenGui = create("ScreenGui", {
		Name = "ZenithGui",
		ResetOnSpawn = false,
		IgnoreGuiInset = true,
		ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
	}, guiParent)

	if typeof(shared) == "table" then
		shared.ZenithGui = screenGui
		shared.Zenith = Zenith
	end

	if typeof(getgenv) == "function" then
		getgenv().Zenith = Zenith
	end

	screenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()
		emit("Zenith Unload")
		Zenith.cleanup()
		Zenith.disconnectUI()
	end)

	local toastHolder = create("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 1, -18),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 400),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		ZIndex = 20,
	}, screenGui)

	create("UIListLayout", {
		Padding = UDim.new(0, 8),
		SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
		HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,
		VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom,
	}, toastHolder)

	Zenith.notify = function(name, enabled)
		if not toastHolder.Parent then
			return
		end

		local toast = create("CanvasGroup", {
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(290, 48),
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 11),
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
			GroupTransparency = 1,
		}, toastHolder)

		create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, toast)
		addStroke(toast, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), enabled and 0.35 or 0.7, 1.5)

		local bar = create("Frame", {
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -14),
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 7),
			BackgroundColor3 = enabled and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
		}, toast)

		create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, bar)

		create("TextLabel", {
			Size = UDim2.new(1, -96, 1, 0),
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Text = name,
			TextColor3 = enabled and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or OFF_TEXT_COLOR,
			TextSize = 17,
			Font = BOLD_FONT,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
		}, toast)

		create("TextLabel", {
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 48),
			Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Text = enabled and "ON" or "OFF",
			TextColor3 = enabled and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
			TextSize = 15,
			Font = BOLD_FONT,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
		}, toast)

		tween(toast, FAST_TWEEN, { GroupTransparency = 0 })

		task.delay(2, function()
			tween(toast, MEDIUM_TWEEN, { GroupTransparency = 1 }).Completed:Wait()
			toast:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	local window = create("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(WINDOW_WIDTH, WINDOW_HEIGHT),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -WINDOW_WIDTH / 2, 0.5, -WINDOW_HEIGHT / 2),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
	}, screenGui)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, window)
	create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(BACKGROUND_TOP, BACKGROUND_BOTTOM), Rotation = 90 }, window)

	local windowStroke = addStroke(window, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.2, 1.5)
	addGradient(windowStroke, 45)

	local windowScale = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.85 }, window)
	tween(windowScale, POP_TWEEN, { Scale = 1 })

	local sheen = create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 140), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, window)
	create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.965, 1), Rotation = 90 }, sheen)

	local titleBar = create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, TITLE_HEIGHT), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BorderSizePixel = 0 }, window)
	local titleGradient = addGradient(titleBar, 0)
	titleGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.8, 0.92)

	local titleLine = create("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, titleBar)

	addGradient(titleLine, 0)

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -260, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		RichText = true,
		Text = "<b>" .. TITLE_TEXT .. "</b>",
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 14,
		Font = BOLD_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
	}, titleBar)

	local searchBox = create("TextBox", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(130, 22),
		Position = UDim2.new(1, -200, 0.5, -11),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.2,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		PlaceholderText = "Search",
		PlaceholderColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
		Text = "",
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		ClearTextOnFocus = false,
	}, titleBar)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5) }, searchBox)
	local searchStroke = addStroke(searchBox, ACCENT_A, 1)

	searchBox.Focused:Connect(function()
		tween(searchStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Transparency = 0 })
	end)

	searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		tween(searchStroke, FAST_TWEEN, { Transparency = 1 })
	end)

	local minimizeButton = createTitleButton(titleBar, "-", -60, ACCENT_B)
	local closeButton = createTitleButton(titleBar, "X", -32, ACCENT_B)

	makeDraggable(titleBar, window)

	Zenith.unload = function()
		tween(windowScale, FAST_TWEEN, { Scale = 0 }).Completed:Connect(function()
			screenGui:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	local minimized = false
	local windowWidth = WINDOW_WIDTH
	local windowHeight = WINDOW_HEIGHT

	minimizeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		minimized = not minimized
		Zenith.resizeHandle.Visible = not minimized
		tween(window, MEDIUM_TWEEN, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(windowWidth, minimized and TITLE_HEIGHT or windowHeight) })
	end)

	closeButton.Activated:Connect(Zenith.unload)

	local tabBar = create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, TAB_HEIGHT), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, TITLE_HEIGHT), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, window)

	local indicator = create("Frame", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1 / #TAB_NAMES, -12, 1, -6),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 3),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.9,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, tabBar)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6) }, indicator)
	addStroke(indicator, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.8)

	local underline = create("Frame", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1),
		Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 2),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 1),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
	}, indicator)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, underline)
	addGradient(underline, 0)
	Zenith.indicator = indicator

	local contentTop = TITLE_HEIGHT + TAB_HEIGHT + 8
	local content = create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -(contentTop + 10)), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, contentTop), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, window)

	local resizeHandle = create("TextButton", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18),
		Position = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = "",
		ZIndex = 10,
	}, window)

	Zenith.resizeHandle = resizeHandle

	for gripIndex = 1, 3 do
		create("Frame", {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(gripIndex * 4, 1),
			Position = UDim2.new(1, -3, 1, -3 - (3 - gripIndex) * 4 - 2),
			BackgroundColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
			BorderSizePixel = 0,
		}, resizeHandle)
	end

	local resizing = false
	local resizeStart, startWidth, startHeight

	resizeHandle.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		for _, grip in resizeHandle:GetChildren() do
			tween(grip, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT_A })
		end
	end)

	resizeHandle.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if resizing then
			return
		end

		for _, grip in resizeHandle:GetChildren() do
			tween(grip, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR })
		end
	end)

	resizeHandle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not isPress(input) then
			return
		end

		resizing = true
		resizeStart = input.Position
		startWidth, startHeight = windowWidth, windowHeight
	end)

	connectInput(userInputService.InputChanged, function(input)
		if not resizing or not isMove(input) then
			return
		end

		local delta = input.Position - resizeStart
		windowWidth = math.clamp(startWidth + delta.X, MIN_WIDTH, MAX_WIDTH)
		windowHeight = math.clamp(startHeight + delta.Y, MIN_HEIGHT, MAX_HEIGHT)
		window.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(windowWidth, windowHeight)
	end)

	connectInput(userInputService.InputEnded, function(input)
		if not resizing or not isPress(input) then
			return
		end

		resizing = false
		for _, grip in resizeHandle:GetChildren() do
			tween(grip, FAST_TWEEN, { BackgroundColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR })
		end
	end)

	for index, name in TAB_NAMES do
		local button = create("TextButton", {
			Size = UDim2.fromScale(1 / #TAB_NAMES, 1),
			Position = UDim2.fromScale((index - 1) / #TAB_NAMES, 0),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Text = name,
			TextColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
			TextSize = 13,
			Font = BOLD_FONT,
			AutoButtonColor = false,
		}, tabBar)

		local page = create("CanvasGroup", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false }, content)

		button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			if Zenith.selected ~= name then
				tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = OFF_TEXT_COLOR })
			end
		end)

		button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			if Zenith.selected ~= name then
				tween(button, FAST_TWEEN, { TextColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR })
			end
		end)

		button.Activated:Connect(function()
			switchTab(name)
		end)

		Zenith.pages[name] = page
		Zenith.tabButtons[name] = button
		Zenith.tabIndex[name] = index
	end

	-- Zenith Console (floating terminal).
	local LEVELS = { Debug = 0, Info = 1, Warn = 2, Error = 3 }
	local LEVEL_COLORS = {
		Debug = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
		Info = OFF_TEXT_COLOR,
		Warn = TEXT_COLOR,
		Error = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
	}

	local consoleFrame = create("Frame", {
		Name = "Zenith Console",
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(380, 240),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, -256),
		BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 13),
		BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ClipsDescendants = true,
		Visible = false,
	}, screenGui)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8) }, consoleFrame)
	addStroke(consoleFrame, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.7, 1.5)

	local consoleHeader = create("Frame", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, consoleFrame)

	create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, 0),
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = "Zenith Console",
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 13,
		Font = BOLD_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
	}, consoleHeader)

	local consoleClose = createTitleButton(consoleHeader, "X", -28, ACCENT_B)
	local consoleClear = createTitleButton(consoleHeader, "C", -56, ACCENT_B)
	makeDraggable(consoleHeader, consoleFrame)

	local consoleBody = create("ScrollingFrame", {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 30),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -62),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 3,
		CanvasSize = UDim2.new(),
		AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
	}, consoleFrame)

	create("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, consoleBody)

	local consoleInput = create("TextBox", {
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 1, -28),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 22),
		BackgroundColor3 = ELEMENT_COLOR,
		BorderSizePixel = 0,
		PlaceholderText = "Type a command (help)",
		PlaceholderColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
		Text = "",
		TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = CODE_FONT,
		ClearTextOnFocus = false,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
	}, consoleFrame)

	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4) }, consoleInput)
	create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6) }, consoleInput)
	addStroke(consoleInput, DIM_TEXT_COLOR, 0.6)

	local consoleLines = {}
	local consoleOrder = 0

	local function clearConsole()
		for _, label in consoleLines do
			label:Destroy()
		end
		table.clear(consoleLines)
	end

	Zenith.log = function(level, message)
		level = LEVELS[level] and level or "Info"
		if LEVELS[level] < (LEVELS[Zenith.values["Console Log Level"]] or 0) then
			return
		end

		consoleOrder += 1
		local stamp = Zenith.values["Console Timestamps"] ~= false and os.date("%H:%M:%S ") or ""

		local label = create("TextLabel", {
			Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
			AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			Text = string.format("%s[%s] %s", stamp, string.upper(level), tostring(message)),
			TextColor3 = LEVEL_COLORS[level],
			TextSize = 11,
			Font = CODE_FONT,
			TextWrapped = true,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
			LayoutOrder = consoleOrder,
		}, consoleBody)

		table.insert(consoleLines, label)
		while #consoleLines > (Zenith.values["Console Max Lines"] or 200) do
			table.remove(consoleLines, 1):Destroy()
		end

		task.delay(0.03, function()
			if consoleBody.Parent then
				consoleBody.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, consoleBody.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y)
			end
		end)
	end

	Zenith.actions["Clear Console"] = clearConsole
	consoleClear.Activated:Connect(clearConsole)

	consoleClose.Activated:Connect(function()
		Zenith.setters["Console Visible"](false)
	end)

	consoleInput.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
		local text = consoleInput.Text
		consoleInput.Text = ""

		if not enterPressed or text == "" then
			return
		end

		Zenith.log("Info", "> " .. text)
		local command, rest = string.match(text, "^(%S+)%s*(.-)$")
		command = string.lower(command)

		if command == "help" then
			Zenith.log("Info", "help, clear, echo <text>, uptime, version, unload")
		elseif command == "clear" then
			clearConsole()
		elseif command == "echo" then
			Zenith.log("Info", rest)
		elseif command == "uptime" then
			Zenith.log("Info", string.format("%.0f seconds", os.clock() - Zenith.startTime))
		elseif command == "version" then
			Zenith.log("Info", "Zenith " .. Zenith.version)
		elseif command == "unload" then
			Zenith.unload()
		elseif Zenith.hooks["Console Command"] then
			emit("Console Command", text)
		else
			Zenith.log("Warn", "Unknown command: " .. command)
		end
	end)

	-- Internal hooks that make part of the menu live.
	local function refreshSpeed()
		local enabled = Zenith.values["Animations"] ~= false
		animationSpeed = enabled and math.max(Zenith.values["Tween Speed"] or 1, 0.1) or 1000
	end

	Zenith.internal["Animations"] = refreshSpeed
	Zenith.internal["Tween Speed"] = refreshSpeed

	Zenith.internal["Menu Opacity"] = function(value)
		window.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - value
	end

	Zenith.internal["Menu Scale"] = function(value)
		windowScale.Scale = value
	end

	Zenith.internal["Console Visible"] = function(value)
		consoleFrame.Visible = value
	end

	-- Decompiler tab (tab principal).
	local decLeft = createColumn(Zenith.pages.Decompiler, UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
	local decRight = createColumn(Zenith.pages.Decompiler, UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 0))

	addSection(decLeft, "Script Scanner")
	addCounter(decLeft, "Scripts Found", 0)
	addCounter(decLeft, "Decompiled", 0)
	addButton(decLeft, "Scan Scripts")
	addCycle(decLeft, "Script Class Filter", { "All", "LocalScript", "ModuleScript", "Script" })
	addText(decLeft, "Script Search", "Filter scripts")
	addText(decLeft, "Global Search", "Search bytecode strings/imports")
	addButton(decLeft, "Global Scan Scripts")
	addButton(decLeft, "GC Closure Search")
	addList(decLeft, "Script List", {}, 155)
	addButton(decLeft, "Decompile Selected")
	addButton(decLeft, "Copy Script Path")
	addText(decLeft, "Script Path", "Script path")
	addButton(decLeft, "Decompile Path")
	addButton(decLeft, "Dump All Scripts")
	addButton(decLeft, "Export Scripts .rbxmx")
	addButton(decLeft, "Export Place .rbxmx")

	addSection(decRight, "Decompiler Output")
	addCycle(decRight, "Decompile Mode", { "Source", "Function", "Bytecode", "Constants", "Upvalues", "Protos", "Strings & Refs", "Script Info" })
	addText(decRight, "Function Target", "Fn index or name (empty = main)")
	addText(decRight, "Output Search", "Search in output")
	addOutput(decRight, "Decompiler Output", 230)
	addButton(decRight, "Copy Decompiled")
	addButton(decRight, "Save Decompiled")

	-- RSpy tab (Cobalt-style clean 2-panel layout: capture list left, code & actions right).
	local rspyLeft = createColumn(Zenith.pages.RSpy, UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
	local rspyRight = createColumn(Zenith.pages.RSpy, UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 0))

	addSection(rspyLeft, "Captured Remotes")
	addToggle(rspyLeft, "Remote Spy", nil, false)
	addToggle(rspyLeft, "Spy Overlay", "F4", false)
	addCounter(rspyLeft, "Calls Logged", 0)
	addCycle(rspyLeft, "Spy Filter", { "All", "RemoteEvent", "RemoteFunction", "BindableEvent", "BindableFunction", "Interactions", "Attributes" })
	addText(rspyLeft, "Spy Search", "Search remotes")
	addText(rspyLeft, "Spy Ignore List", "Names, comma separated")
	addToggle(rspyLeft, "Group By Remote", nil, false)
	addList(rspyLeft, "Remote List", {}, 220)
	addButton(rspyLeft, "Clear Spy Logs")
	addButton(rspyLeft, "Export RSpy Session")

	addSection(rspyRight, "Call Inspector")
	addOutput(rspyRight, "Spy Log", 205)
	addButton(rspyRight, "Copy Call Code")
	addButton(rspyRight, "Copy Remote Path")
	addButton(rspyRight, "Decompile Caller")
	addButton(rspyRight, "Copy Function Info")
	addButton(rspyRight, "Find Script XRefs")
	addButton(rspyRight, "Repeat Call")
	addText(rspyRight, "Custom Args", 'e.g. "Sword", 999, true')
	addButton(rspyRight, "Replay Custom Args")
	addButton(rspyRight, "Block Remote")
	addButton(rspyRight, "Ignore Remote")

	addSection(rspyRight, "Spy Options")
	addToggle(rspyRight, "Log Client To Server", nil, true)
	addToggle(rspyRight, "Log Server To Client", nil, true)
	addToggle(rspyRight, "Log Return Values", nil, false)
	addToggle(rspyRight, "Show Call Stack", nil, false)
	addToggle(rspyRight, "Spy Auto Scroll", nil, true)
	addSlider(rspyRight, "Spy Max Logs", 50, 1000, 300, 0)

	-- Core tab (Kernel & Console).
	local coreLeft = createColumn(Zenith.pages.Core, UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
	local coreRight = createColumn(Zenith.pages.Core, UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 0))

	addSection(coreLeft, "Zenith Console")
	addToggle(coreLeft, "Console Visible", "Backquote", false)
	addToggle(coreLeft, "Console Timestamps", nil, true)
	addCycle(coreLeft, "Console Log Level", { "Debug", "Info", "Warn", "Error" })
	addSlider(coreLeft, "Console Max Lines", 50, 1000, 200, 0)
	addButton(coreLeft, "Clear Console")
	addButton(coreLeft, "Copy Console", function()
		local parts = {}
		for _, label in consoleLines do
			table.insert(parts, label.Text)
		end

		local copied = Zenith.copy(table.concat(parts, "\n"))
		Zenith.log(copied and "Info" or "Warn", copied and "Console copied." or "Clipboard is not supported here.")
	end)

	addSection(coreRight, "Zenith Core")
	addCounter(coreRight, "Modules Loaded", 0)
	addCounter(coreRight, "Active Connections", 0)
	addCounter(coreRight, "Uptime", "00:00:00")
	addToggle(coreRight, "Safe Boot", nil, true)
	addToggle(coreRight, "Track Connections", nil, true)
	addList(coreRight, "Core Services", { "Players", "Workspace", "ReplicatedStorage", "RunService", "UserInputService", "TweenService", "HttpService", "Lighting", "CoreGui", "TeleportService" }, 115)
	addButton(coreRight, "Refresh Services")
	addButton(coreRight, "Cleanup Connections")
	addButton(coreRight, "Reload Zenith")
	addOutput(coreRight, "Boot Log", 110)

	-- Config tab (Roblox Ignore/Filters, Decompiler Config & Menu/Configs).
	local cfgLeft = createColumn(Zenith.pages.Config, UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
	local cfgRight = createColumn(Zenith.pages.Config, UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 0))

	local statusLabel = create("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 18),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -20),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = "",
		TextColor3 = DIM_TEXT_COLOR,
		TextSize = 12,
		Font = TEXT_FONT,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		ZIndex = 5,
	}, Zenith.pages.Config)

	local function setStatus(message)
		statusLabel.Text = message
		statusLabel.TextTransparency = 0
		Zenith.log("Info", message)

		task.delay(3, function()
			if statusLabel.Text == message then
				tween(statusLabel, MEDIUM_TWEEN, { TextTransparency = 1 })
			end
		end)
	end

	addSection(cfgLeft, "Roblox Ignore & Filters")
	addToggle(cfgLeft, "Ignore Roblox Internals", nil, true)
	addToggle(cfgLeft, "Ignore Bindables", nil, true)
	addToggle(cfgLeft, "Ignore PlayerModule & Chat", nil, true)
	addText(cfgLeft, "Ignore Services", "Services, comma separated", Zenith.robloxDefaults.services)
	addText(cfgLeft, "Ignore Roblox Scripts", "Scripts, comma separated", Zenith.robloxDefaults.scripts)
	addText(cfgLeft, "Ignore Roblox Remotes", "Remotes, comma separated", Zenith.robloxDefaults.remotes)
	addButton(cfgLeft, "Reset Roblox Filters", function()
		Zenith.setters["Ignore Roblox Internals"](true)
		Zenith.setters["Ignore Bindables"](true)
		Zenith.setters["Ignore PlayerModule & Chat"](true)
		Zenith.setters["Ignore Services"](Zenith.robloxDefaults.services)
		Zenith.setters["Ignore Roblox Scripts"](Zenith.robloxDefaults.scripts)
		Zenith.setters["Ignore Roblox Remotes"](Zenith.robloxDefaults.remotes)
		setStatus("Roblox filters reset.")
	end)

	addSection(cfgLeft, "Decompiler Config")
	addToggle(cfgLeft, "Include Core Scripts", nil, false)
	addToggle(cfgLeft, "Include Nil Scripts", nil, false)
	addToggle(cfgLeft, "Show Line Numbers", nil, true)
	addToggle(cfgLeft, "Include Constants", nil, false)
	addToggle(cfgLeft, "Include Upvalues", nil, false)
	addToggle(cfgLeft, "Strip Comments", nil, false)
	addToggle(cfgLeft, "Save Bytecode", nil, false)
	addToggle(cfgLeft, "Anonymize Dump", nil, false)
	addSlider(cfgLeft, "Decompile Timeout", 1, 60, 10, 0)
	addSlider(cfgLeft, "Decompile Max Lines", 100, 2000, 600, 0)

	addSection(cfgRight, "RSpy Advanced")
	addToggle(cfgRight, "Ignore Executor Calls", nil, true)
	addToggle(cfgRight, "Log Actors", nil, true)
	addToggle(cfgRight, "Prefer Stealth Hook (oth)", nil, true)
	addToggle(cfgRight, "Log Interactions", nil, false)
	addToggle(cfgRight, "Log Attributes", nil, false)
	addToggle(cfgRight, "Auto Ignore Spammy", nil, false)
	addToggle(cfgRight, "Full Serialization", nil, false)
	addToggle(cfgRight, "Spy File Logging", nil, false)

	addSection(cfgRight, "Menu & Configs")
	addCycle(cfgRight, "Menu Keybind", { "RightShift", "Insert", "RightControl", "Delete", "End" })
	addText(cfgRight, "Config Name", "my config")
	Zenith.transient["Config Name"] = true
	addList(cfgRight, "Config Presets", {}, 80)
	addButton(cfgRight, "Save")
	addButton(cfgRight, "Load")
	addButton(cfgRight, "Delete Config")
	addToggle(cfgRight, "Autoload", nil, false)
	addButton(cfgRight, "Reset Settings")
	addButton(cfgRight, "Unload Zenith", function()
		Zenith.unload()
	end)

	-- Output limits driven by their sliders.
	local limitLinks = {
		["Decompile Max Lines"] = "Decompiler Output",
		["Spy Max Logs"] = "Spy Log",
	}

	for sliderName, outputName in limitLinks do
		local api = Zenith.outputs[outputName]
		if api then
			api.maxLines = Zenith.values[sliderName]
			Zenith.internal[sliderName] = function(value)
				api.maxLines = value
			end
		end
	end

	Zenith.internal["Spy Auto Scroll"] = function(value)
		Zenith.outputs["Spy Log"].autoScroll = value
	end

	-- Config.
	local function getConfigPath()
		local name = string.gsub(tostring(Zenith.values["Config Name"] or ""), "[^%w_%- ]", "")
		if name == "" then
			return CONFIG_FILE
		end

		return CONFIG_FOLDER .. "/" .. name .. ".json"
	end

	local function encodeValues()
		local data = {}

		for name, value in Zenith.values do
			if Zenith.transient[name] then
				continue
			end

			if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
				data[name] = {
					R = math.floor(value.R * 255 + 0.5),
					G = math.floor(value.G * 255 + 0.5),
					B = math.floor(value.B * 255 + 0.5),
					__color = true,
				}
			else
				data[name] = value
			end
		end

		return httpService:JSONEncode(data)
	end

	local function writeConfig(path)
		if not writefile then
			return false, "Saving is not supported here."
		end

		return pcall(function()
			if makefolder and isfolder and not isfolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) then
				makefolder(CONFIG_FOLDER)
			end

			writefile(path, encodeValues())
		end)
	end

	local function readConfig(path)
		if not (readfile and isfile and isfile(path)) then
			return nil, "No saved settings found."
		end

		local ok, data = pcall(function()
			return httpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path))
		end)

		if not ok or typeof(data) ~= "table" then
			return nil, "Settings file is corrupted."
		end

		return data
	end

	local function applyConfig(data)
		for name, value in data do
			local setter = Zenith.setters[name]
			if not setter or Zenith.transient[name] then
				continue
			end

			if typeof(value) == "table" and value.__color then
				value = Color3.fromRGB(value.R, value.G, value.B)
			end

			pcall(setter, value)
		end
	end

	local function refreshPresets()
		local names = {}

		if typeof(listfiles) == "function" and typeof(isfolder) == "function" and isfolder(CONFIG_FOLDER) then
			local ok, files = pcall(listfiles, CONFIG_FOLDER)
			if ok then
				for _, file in files do
					local name = string.match(file, "([^/\\]+)%.json$")
					if name then
						table.insert(names, name)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		table.sort(names)
		Zenith.setItems("Config Presets", names)
	end

	local function saveSettings()
		local ok, result = writeConfig(getConfigPath())
		setStatus(ok and "Settings saved." or ("Save failed: " .. tostring(result)))
		refreshPresets()
	end

	Zenith.internal["Config Presets"] = function(name)
		if name ~= "" then
			Zenith.setters["Config Name"](name)
		end
	end

	Zenith.actions["Save"] = saveSettings

	Zenith.actions["Load"] = function()
		local data, message = readConfig(getConfigPath())
		if not data then
			return setStatus(message)
		end

		applyConfig(data)
		setStatus("Settings loaded.")
	end

	Zenith.actions["Delete Config"] = function()
		local path = getConfigPath()
		if typeof(delfile) == "function" and isfile and isfile(path) then
			delfile(path)
			setStatus("Config deleted.")
			refreshPresets()
		else
			setStatus("Nothing to delete.")
		end
	end

	Zenith.actions["Reset Settings"] = function()
		for name, value in Zenith.defaults do
			if not Zenith.transient[name] and Zenith.setters[name] then
				Zenith.setters[name](value)
			end
		end

		setStatus("Settings reset.")
	end

	refreshPresets()

	local savedData = readConfig(CONFIG_FILE)
	if savedData and savedData.Autoload == true then
		applyConfig(savedData)
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while screenGui.Parent do
			if Zenith.values["Autoload"] then
				writeConfig(CONFIG_FILE)
			end

			task.wait(math.max(Zenith.values["Autosave Interval"] or 15, 5))
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while screenGui.Parent do
			local up = math.floor(os.clock() - Zenith.startTime)
			Zenith.setters["Uptime"](string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", up // 3600, up % 3600 // 60, up % 60))
			Zenith.refreshConnections()
			task.wait(1)
		end
	end)

	searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		local query = string.lower(searchBox.Text)

		for _, element in Zenith.searchables do
			element.Visible = (query == "" or string.find(string.lower(element.Name), query, 1, true) ~= nil) and not element:GetAttribute("GroupHidden")
		end

		for _, section in Zenith.sections do
			section.Visible = query == ""
		end

		for _, box in Zenith.boxes do
			local anyVisible = query == ""

			for _, child in box:GetChildren() do
				if child:IsA("GuiObject") and child.Visible and not table.find(Zenith.sections, child) then
					anyVisible = true
					break
				end
			end

			box.Visible = anyVisible
		end
	end)

	connectInput(userInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gameProcessed)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			return
		end

		if Zenith.listening then
			return Zenith.listening(input.KeyCode)
		end

		if gameProcessed then
			return
		end

		if input.KeyCode.Name == Zenith.values["Menu Keybind"] then
			window.Visible = not window.Visible
			return
		end

		for featureName, keyCode in Zenith.keybinds do
			if keyCode == input.KeyCode then
				Zenith.setters[featureName](not Zenith.values[featureName])
				Zenith.announce(featureName, Zenith.values[featureName])
			end
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while screenGui.Parent do
			local visible = Zenith.values["Keybind Hints"] ~= false

			for _, keybindButton in Zenith.keyLabels do
				keybindButton.Visible = visible
			end

			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)

	refreshSpeed()
	Zenith.outputs["Boot Log"].push("Zenith " .. Zenith.version .. " ready.")
	Zenith.log("Info", "Zenith " .. Zenith.version .. " loaded.")
	switchTab(TAB_NAMES[1])
	Zenith.start()
end

local function onInitializeError(error)
	warn("Failed to initialize.")
	warn(error)
	warn(debug.traceback())
end

xpcall(initializeScript, onInitializeError)
